We provide evidence that this arbitration can be read from activations in a structured margin readout. We introduce Constitutional Value Potentials (CVP). For each value we learn a scalar potential from the hidden state: an internal pressure to preserve that value, supervised not by the prompt but by an independent judge's verdict on which value the model's own response actually preserved. The signed difference of two potentials is a priority margin. A constitutional clause becomes the claim that a margin stays positive, and a single monitor score flags when it does not. The monitor predicts conflict violations with AUROC up to 0.95, beats a strong hidden-state probe, and generalizes to held-out synthetic conflicts across three Qwen2.5 scales. The signal appears as the answer begins, from the prompt tail and first response token. Read this early, the same signal reveals whether an adversarial priority hack has actually pushed the model toward a violation, rather than only whether the prompt looks adversarial. The same directions also support intervention tests: under selected steering settings, moving along a value direction shifts judged trade-offs in the intended direction. Together, these results suggest that some constitution-relevant priorities are accessible as activation-space margins, rather than only as output behavior.

</details>


### [69] [Post-Launch Capability Expansion of Vision-Language Models via Prompting for On-Orbit Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15427)
*Nicholas A. Welsh,Lennon J. Shikhman,Monty Nehru Attazs,Seemanthini K. Putane,Van Minh Nguyen,Ryan T. White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在航天器在轨检测场景下，使用冻结权重的提示驱动视觉-语言模型（SAM3）实现发射后语义扩展的可行性。在129张未见卫星图像上进行零样本实例分割，SAM3在固定全局阈值下达到0.385 mAP@0.5和0.267 mAP@0.5:0.95。性能具有强尺度依赖性：大型结构（航天器主体0.639 AP@0.5、太阳翼0.598 AP@0.5）定位可靠，而小型部件（天线0.221 AP@0.5、推进器0.081 AP@0.5）仍具挑战。结构化提示（含空间和几何描述）相比简短类别名提示可提升高达82%的性能。模型在嵌入式GPU内存和计算范围内运行，表明提示驱动的地面定位可为在轨语义扩展提供实用机制，但也凸显了零样本定位在轨道域偏移下对精细部件的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天星载检测系统通常发射前部署感知模型，发射后更新模型权重或扩展固定标签集在操作上不可行。虽然监督模型可以飞行前集成，但在轨增加新语义能力需要重新训练和重新上传参数。本文旨在探究提示驱动的视觉-语言模型能否实现发射后语义扩展，允许通过自然语言提示指定新航天器部件，而无需修改星上权重。

Method: 采用严格冻结、单次推理的零样本实例分割协议，使用SAM3模型在129张未见卫星测试集上进行评估。固定全局阈值，无后处理。比较不同提示形式的影响，包括简短类别名提示与包含空间和几何描述的结构化提示。

Result: SAM3达到0.385 mAP@0.5和0.267 mAP@0.5:0.95。大型结构表现良好：航天器主体0.639 AP@0.5，太阳翼0.598 AP@0.5；小型部件表现较差：天线0.221 AP@0.5，推进器0.081 AP@0.5。结构化提示相比简短提示提升高达82%。模型在嵌入式GPU内存和计算范围内运行。

Conclusion: 提示驱动的地面定位可为在轨语义扩展提供实用机制，尤其适用于主导航天器结构；但零样本定位在轨道域偏移下对精细部件仍存在局限性。提示公式化对性能有显著影响。

Abstract: Spaceborne inspection systems often deploy perception models prior to launch, after which updating model weights or expanding fixed label sets becomes operationally impractical. While supervised models can be integrated pre-flight, adding new semantic capabilities in orbit requires retraining and re-uploading parameters. We investigate whether prompt-driven vision--language models can enable post-launch semantic expansion, allowing new spacecraft components to be specified via natural-language prompts without modifying onboard weights. We evaluate zero-shot instance segmentation of spacecraft components under a strictly frozen, single-pass inference protocol on a test set of $129$ images of previously unseen satellites. Under fixed global thresholds and no post-processing, SAM3 achieves $0.385$ mAP@$0.5$ and $0.267$ mAP@$0.5{:}0.95$. Performance is strongly scale-dependent: large structural elements like spacecraft bodies ($0.639$ AP@$0.50$) and solar arrays ($0.598$ AP@$0.5$) localize reliably, while relatively small appendages like antennas ($0.221$ AP@$0.5$) and thrusters ($0.081$ AP@$0.5$) remain difficult. Prompt formulation influences performance, with structured prompts incorporating spatial and geometric descriptors yielding up to $82%$ improvement over short category-name prompts. The model operates within the memory and compute envelope of contemporary embedded GPUs, suggesting prompt-driven grounding can provide a practical mechanism for post-launch semantic extension of dominant spacecraft structures while highlighting limitations of zero-shot localization for fine-scale components under orbital domain shift.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Classification: A Cough Regression Benchmark for Respiratory Acoustic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15436)
*Mayur Sanap,Prasanna Desikan,Edgar Lobaton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估呼吸声学基础模型在咳嗽音频上连续健康指标（年龄、BMI、疾病概率）的回归预测能力，比较5个模型（OPERA-CT/CE/GT, HeAR, M2D+Resp）在3个数据集上的表现，发现HeAR在年龄回归上最优（MAE 9.12年），MLP-small头优于线性探测但全MLP在小数据上过拟合，存在数据集大小与头容量权衡，OPERA-GT生成式预训练优于OPERA-CT，跨数据集迁移呈现强不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 咳嗽分类任务已被充分研究，但连续健康指标（年龄、BMI、疾病概率）的被动估计在无法获取物理测量值的临床场景中具有重要价值，而呼吸声学基础模型在该方向的能力尚未被探索。

Method: 构建多模型、多目标咳嗽回归基准，评估5个基础模型（OPERA-CT/CE/GT, HeAR, M2D+Resp）在3个数据集上对6个目标的回归性能，采用受试者不重叠协议，比较线性头、MLP-small和全MLP三种回归头。

Result: MLP-small在全部任务上优于均值预测基线，在30个模型×任务组合中23个优于线性探测；全MLP在小临床数据上过拟合但在大数据上恢复，揭示了数据集大小与头容量的权衡。HeAR在Coswara数据集年龄回归最优（9.12年MAE），但CIDRZ结果因可能训练重叠被排除。OPERA-GT在三个数据集上年龄回归均优于OPERA-CT。HeAR和M2D+Resp在N=50时接近满性能，OPERA模型需N=400。跨数据集迁移强不对称：大多样数据泛化到小临床人群（CoughVID→CIDRZ: -0.17年），反之则差（CIDRZ→Coswara: +2.43年,+26.6%）。

Conclusion: 咳嗽音频可以用于连续健康指标的回归预测，但存在模型-数据-头容量的复杂权衡；生成式预训练在年龄回归上具有优势；少量样本即可达到良好性能；跨数据集迁移需谨慎，大规模多样化数据更适合作为源域。

Abstract: Respiratory acoustic foundation models (FMs) excel at cough classification, yet their ability to predict continuous health quantities from cough audio remains largely unexplored, despite the clinical value of passive age, BMI, and disease probability estimation in settings where physical measurements are unavailable. We introduce the multi-model, multi-target cough regression benchmark evaluating five FMs (OPERA-CT, OPERA-CE, OPERA-GT, HeAR, M2D+Resp) across six targets on three datasets under subject-disjoint protocols, comparing linear, MLP-small, and full MLP regression heads. MLP-small beats the mean-predictor baseline on all tasks and linear probing in 23 of 30 model x task cases, with full MLP overfitting on small clinical data but recovering on larger sets, revealing a dataset size x head-capacity trade-off. HeAR leads within-dataset age regression on Coswara (9.12 yr MAE); its CIDRZ result is excluded from headline claims owing to possible HeAR-CIDRZ pretraining overlap. OPERA-GT is favored over OPERA-CT on age in all three datasets, with the CIDRZ margin within seed variance, extending a generative-pretraining advantage from breath to cough. HeAR and M2D+Resp reach near-full performance at N = 50 samples while OPERA models require N = 400. Cross-dataset transfer is strongly asymmetric as large diverse data generalises to small clinical populations (CoughVID to CIDRZ: -0.17 yr) but not vice versa (CIDRZ to Coswara: +2.43 yr, +26.6%).

</details>


### [71] [Understanding Diversity Collapse in RLVR via the Lens of Overtraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15455)
*Suqin Yuan,Jinkun Chen,Jiyang Zheng,Muyang Li,Lei Feng,Dadong Wang,Tao Xiang,Tongliang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR 训练存在“多样性坍塌”问题（Pass@1 提升但高 k 的 Pass@k 下降），本文将其形式化为“过度训练”问题，并提出 Bayesian Boundary Gating (BBG) 方法，通过估计每个问题对推理边界的边际贡献来优化训练，在多个推理基准上提升了平均 Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 虽能提升大模型的推理能力，但存在多样性坍塌问题：Pass@1 提高而高 k 的 Pass@k 下降，这意味着模型的推理边界在缩小。需要理解这一现象的根本原因并设计相应干预方法。

Method: 1) 形式化多样性坍塌为“过度训练”：当某个问题对参考指标的贡献饱和后，继续更新不再扩展模型能解决的问题集，而是将概率质量集中在采样偏好的轨迹上；2) 提出 Bayesian Boundary Gating (BBG)：估计每个问题对推理边界的边际贡献，将优化从过度训练中转移出来。

Result: 1) 将更新限制在零成功观测的问题上，可在困难基准上将 Pass@256 提升至基础模型之上；2) 标准 RLVR 训练中，相当一部分初始不可解问题变得可解；3) BBG 在多个推理基准上提升了不同 k 值下的平均 Pass@k。

Conclusion: RLVR 的多样性坍塌源于过度训练，标准 RLVR 大多更新是边界角度的过度训练；RLVR 能否真正扩展推理能力不能仅看高 k Pass@k 的下降；BBG 通过估计边际贡献有效缓解了过度训练问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, RLVR often suffers from \emph{diversity collapse}: Pass@$1$ improves while high-$k$ Pass@$k$ degrades, which is viewed as a narrowing of the model's reasoning boundary. We formalize this diversity collapse through the lens of \emph{overtraining}: once a problem's contribution to the reference metric has effectively saturated, further updates no longer expand what the model can solve but still concentrate probability mass on the trajectories favored by on-policy sampling. Under a standard setup with few rollouts per problem, even a single observed success places a problem in a nearly saturated regime for high-$k$ Pass@$k$, so most updates in standard RLVR are overtraining from the boundary perspective. This perspective also suggests a reading of whether RLVR can expand the model's reasoning abilities beyond the base model: since RLVR is structurally biased against high-$k$ Pass@$k$, its aggregate decline does not by itself mean that no new reasoning gains occurred. Interventionally, restricting updates to problems with zero observed success lifts Pass@$256$ above the base model on difficult benchmarks; observationally, a non-trivial fraction of initially unsolvable problems become solvable during standard RLVR training. Building on these findings, we propose \emph{Bayesian Boundary Gating} (BBG), which redirects optimization away from overtraining by estimating each problem's marginal contribution to the reasoning boundary. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, BBG improves average Pass@$k$ across a wide range of $k$.

</details>


### [72] [Bayesian 3D Steerable CNNs: Enabling Equivariance and Uncertainty Quantification Simultaneously](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15479)
*Abhishek Keripale,Ponkrshnan Thiagarajan,Susanta Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯可转向CNN，在保持SE(3)-等变性的同时，通过对基系数进行贝叶斯推断实现不确定性量化，在分类精度和校准误差上表现优异，并能提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可转向CNN保证了SE(3)-等变性，但其确定性本质无法进行不确定性量化，限制了在需要置信度估计的场景中的应用。

Method: 将基系数上的后验分布引入可转向CNN，通过变分推断和Bayes-by-Backpropagation优化损失函数，实现随机核的同时保持精确等变性，并将预测不确定性分解为认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: 模型在分类精度上具有竞争力，期望校准误差为0.0263；在高斯噪声引起的分布偏移下，比确定性版本提升高达6.17%；利用不确定性估计在84%测试集上获得约4%的精度提升；认知不确定性与预测误差呈显著负相关。

Conclusion: 该框架将贝叶斯不确定性量化与等变CNN的归纳偏置统一起来，证明了学习到的后验方差具有语义意义。

Abstract: Steerable convolutional neural networks (Steerable-CNNs) guarantee SE(3)-equivariance by parameterizing kernels as linear combinations of steerable basis functions, but their deterministic nature precludes uncertainty quantification - limiting their use in settings where confidence estimates are essential. We propose a Bayesian Steerable-CNN that places posterior distributions over the basis coefficients, yielding stochastic kernels while preserving equivariance exactly. The loss function of the model is obtained via variational inference and minimized by Bayes-by-Backpropagation. The framework admits a decomposition of predictive uncertainty into epistemic and aleatoric components. Empirically, the model attains competitive classification accuracy alongside an expected calibration error of 0.0263 and outperforms its deterministic counterpart by up to 6.17% under distributional shift induced by additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we leverage the model's uncertainty estimates to enhance its performance significantly, achieving a notable gain - approximately 4% higher accuracy across 84% of the test dataset. A statistically significant negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and prediction error confirms that the learned posterior variance is semantically meaningful. The framework unifies Bayesian uncertainty quantification with the inductive bias of equivariant CNNs.

</details>


### [73] [Model Stealing Through the Lens of Model Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15493)
*Eliott Baltz,Satoshi Hara,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了模型窃取攻击中高保真代理模型与原始目标模型等同的传统假设。通过计算代理模型的Rashomon集（近似等精度模型集合），并在表格、医学影像和NLP任务上评估其多样性，发现尽管代理模型对目标模型的保真度相似，但在其他关键性能指标（如公平性）上存在显著差异，从而否定了高保真代理在部署场景中与原模型等价的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为高保真代理模型能为攻击者提供与原始模型相当的经济价值，但作者质疑这一假设，希望从超越保真度的维度（如公平性、多样性）重新评估模型窃取攻击的实际威胁。

Method: 不执行经典的学习式模型窃取攻击，而是计算代理模型的Rashomon集，并使用多重性指标（模糊性、差异性、Rashomon容量）和群体公平性指标评估其多样性。在表格、医学影像和NLP任务上使用真实数据集进行实验。

Result: 尽管代理模型对目标模型的保真度相似，但在其他关键性能指标上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 高保真代理模型与目标模型在实践部署中并不等价，对模型窃取攻击的传统威胁评估提出了质疑。

Abstract: Model stealing attacks, where adversaries create high-fidelity surrogate models, are a significant threat to the intellectual property of machine learning services. Conventional wisdom suggests these surrogates could provide adversaries with economic leverage comparable to the original service providers. This paper challenges this assumption by evaluating model stealing attacks beyond mere fidelity to the target model. Because query-based extraction provides only partial supervision of the target's input-output behavior, the surrogate is not uniquely identified: many near-optimal surrogates can achieve comparable fidelity while differing in deployment-relevant properties. Instead of performing a classic learning-based model stealing attack, we compute the Rashomon Set (i.e., the set of almost-equally-accurate models) of surrogate models, and evaluate its diversity using multiplicity metrics (ambiguity, discrepancy, and Rashomon Capacity) and group fairness metrics. Across tabular, medical imaging, and NLP tasks, our experiments on real-world datasets reveal that despite exhibiting similar fidelity to the target model, surrogate models can display significant variances in other critical performance metrics. These findings cast doubt on the presumed equivalence between high-fidelity surrogates and the target model in practical deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [74] [Towards Data-Efficient Cross-Device Generalization of Grad-Shafranov Equilibria via Transfer Learning Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15512)
*Jay Phil Yoo,William Howes,Yashika Ghai,Kazuma Kobayashi,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将磁流体平衡重建重新定义为跨装置算子学习问题，开发了领域特定的神经算子框架，将几何和剖面参数直接映射到极向磁通场。在Solov'ev解析族作为测试平台上，对八种几何不同的托卡马克构型进行基准测试。结果表明，多几何预训练可实现数据高效迁移，Wavelet Neural Operator在跨几何性能上最优，在100个标记目标平衡下相对L2误差低于4%，全微调下低于2%，推理时间毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grad-Shafranov平衡计算多为装置特异性且迭代求解，限制了其在延迟敏感的实时控制场景中的应用；现有神经方法可加速单次预测，但无法提供跨等离子体边界或托卡马克几何的可重用模型。

Method: 开发了领域特定的神经算子框架，将平衡重建问题形式化为跨装置算子学习；以解析可解的Solov'ev族作为受控测试平台，生成八种几何不同构型的数据；基准测试五种神经算子架构（包含Wavelet Neural Operator）在四种迁移学习策略下的表现。

Result: 单几何预训练对未见装置迁移性能差，多几何预训练实现数据高效适应；Wavelet Neural Operator跨几何性能最强，100个标记目标平衡时平均相对L2误差低于4%，全微调低于2%；预测磁场满足无散约束至数值精度；四种架构推理时间毫秒或亚毫秒级。

Conclusion: 神经算子预训练是实现跨装置可重用、实时平衡推断的有效路径，为磁约束聚变控制提供新范式。

Abstract: Real-time reconstruction of magnetohydrodynamic equilibria is essential for plasma shaping, stability assessment and feedback control in magnetic confinement fusion. However, Grad-Shafranov equilibrium calculations remain largely device-specific and iterative, limiting their use in latency-constrained control settings. Existing neural approaches can accelerate individual equilibrium predictions, but they do not generally provide reusable models across changing plasma boundaries or tokamak geometries. Here we show that equilibrium reconstruction can be recast as a cross-device operator learning problem. We develop a domain-specific neural operator framework that maps geometry and profile parameters directly to the poloidal flux field, replacing repeated solve-on-demand computation with amortized operator inference. Using the analytically tractable Solov'ev family as a controlled Grad-Shafranov testbed, we generate equilibria across eight geometrically distinct tokamak-like configurations and benchmark five neural operator architectures under four transfer-learning strategies. Single-geometry pretraining gives poor transfer to unseen devices, whereas multi-geometry pretraining enables data-efficient adaptation. The Wavelet Neural Operator gives the strongest cross-geometry performance, reaching mean relative L2 errors below 4% with 100 labelled target equilibria and below 2% with full fine-tuning. The predicted magnetic fields satisfy the divergence-free constraint to numerical precision, and four architectures achieve millisecond or sub-millisecond inference. These results identify neural operator pretraining as a route towards reusable, real-time equilibrium inference across fusion device configurations.

</details>


### [75] [Greedy Coordinate Diffusion: Effective and Semantically Coherent Adversarial Attacks via Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15531)
*Bohdan Turbal,Blossom Metevier,Max Springer,Aleksandra Korolova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何分析框架证明，在对齐语言模型进行良性任务微调时，由于损失函数的曲率导致二阶加速度，即使一阶正交更新看似安全，也会使对齐退化。作者提出对齐不稳定性条件(AIC)，证明对齐退化以四次方速度沿梯度流轨迹发生，并通过Fisher信息矩阵提供安全退化的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个通用的形式化框架来推导微调何时会导致对齐退化的保证，使得该领域缺乏预测或防止对齐崩溃的原则性工具。

Method: 通过参数空间轨迹的几何分析构建局部几何框架，提出对齐不稳定性条件(AIC)，包含三个几何属性，并证明沿梯度流轨迹对齐退化以四次方速度发生。使用Fisher信息矩阵作为安全退化程度的代理指标。

Result: 证明一阶正交更新的安全性是虚幻的，曲率导致的二阶加速度会引发对齐敏感区域的二阶漂移；对齐退化以四次方速度沿梯度流轨迹发生；Fisher信息矩阵可有效代理多种微调场景下的安全退化程度。

Conclusion: 建立了微调导致对齐退化的形式化几何框架，阐明了静态一阶保护在梯度下降下失效的充分条件，为理解和预测对齐崩溃提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Fine-tuning aligned language models on benign tasks (e.g. math tutoring) systematically breaks safety guardrails, even when training data contains no harmful content. While mechanistic approaches have shed light on where alignment resides in model weights, they do not by provide a general formal framework for deriving guarantees about when fine-tuning degrades it -- leaving the field without principled tools for predicting or preventing alignment collapse. We develop a local geometric framework through geometric analysis of parameter-space trajectories and apply it to understand the fragility of alignment in fine-tuning. While first-order analysis suggests orthogonal updates are safe, we prove this is illusory: the curvature of the fine-tuning loss induces second-order acceleration that can induce second-order drift into alignment-sensitive regions. We formalize a construct of our framework as the Alignment Instability Condition (AIC), three geometric properties that, when present, are sufficient to guarantee degradation. Our main result proves quartic onset of alignment degradation along gradient-flow trajectories, determined by how sharply alignment depends on specific parameters and how strongly tasks couple to these parameters. These findings yield formal sufficient conditions under which static first-order protection can fail under gradient descent. We further empirically validate the framework's foundations, showing that the Fisher Information Matrix provides a proxy for the degree of safety degradation across diverse fine-tuning.

</details>


### [76] [A Bifurcation Theory Framework for Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15551)
*Eric Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了梯度下降在"稳定性边缘"（EoS）现象的分岔理论框架，通过将训练动态分解为最小化流形的法向和切向分量，证明了稳定的EoS训练源于法向方向上的翻转分岔，由第一李雅普诺夫系数符号控制，而切向动态则向锐度递减区域漂移。在温和的谱和几何假设下，证明了在EoS阈值处训练收敛到最小化流形，并统一了先前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EoS现象在现代深度学习普遍存在，但先前的严格分析大多局限于标量或低维损失函数且具有特定结构形式，缺乏在现实设定（尤其是过参数化神经网络）下的理论理解。

Method: 将训练动态分解为最小化流形的法向和切向分量，利用翻转分岔理论和第一李雅普诺夫系数分析法向稳定性，同时分析切向动态向锐度递减区域漂移的行为。

Result: 在温和的谱和几何假设下，证明了在EoS阈值处训练收敛到最小化流形；作为推论，恢复并统一了先前结果（如Gan (2026)的乘积稳定性条件是其框架的一个实例）。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于过参数化神经网络的EoS现象分岔理论框架，统一了现有理论结果，为理解深度学习中的EoS现象提供了更一般的理论工具。

Abstract: The Edge of Stability (EoS) phenomenon, where gradient descent operates with sharpness exceeding the classical convergence threshold yet the loss decreases over long timescales, is ubiquitous in modern deep learning but remains poorly understood in realistic settings. Prior rigorous analyses have been largely confined to scalar or low-dimensional losses with specific structural forms. In this work, we develop a bifurcation theory framework for gradient descent on the edge of stability that applies directly to overparameterized neural networks. By decomposing the training dynamics into components normal and tangent to the manifold of minimizers, we show that stable EoS training arises from a flip bifurcation in the normal direction, governed by the sign of the first Lyapunov coefficient, while the tangent dynamics drift toward regions of decreasing sharpness. Under mild spectral and geometric assumptions on the loss landscape, we prove convergence to the minimizing manifold when training at the EoS threshold. As a corollary, we recover and unify prior results: we show that the product-stability condition of Gan (2026) is an instance of our framework.

</details>


### [77] [Distilling Drifting Transformers with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15553)
*Jiawei Zhang,Mengfei Xia,Gen Li,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Drift-RAE方法，将Drifting模型范式应用于表征自编码器（RAE）的潜在空间以蒸馏预训练流模型，通过理论分析和改进训练稳定性，在ImageNet 256上以1.77 FID和仅1万步蒸馏超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAE的潜在空间虽然语义更丰富（得益于DINO特征的强标签聚类），但在蒸馏阶段存在严重的各向异性和大曲率问题，导致基于轨迹的蒸馏不稳定；同时理论揭示Drifting模型在高度分散的空间（如重建VAE）容易失效，因此需要将Drifting范式适配到RAE空间。

Method: 1) 定量研究不同自编码器的曲率和各向同性统计量；2) 理论上揭示Drifting模型在分散空间失败的原因；3) 将Drifting范式直接应用于RAE潜在空间，提出Drift-RAE；4) 通过理论对齐漂移场与其他框架，进行改进以提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在ImageNet 256数据集上，仅用1万步蒸馏步骤即达到1.77 FID，超越现有RAE蒸馏方法，与原始Drifting模型性能相当且无需辅助MAE特征提取器。

Conclusion: RAE潜在空间与Drifting蒸馏范式是兼容的，通过合理的改进可以克服各向异性和大曲率带来的不稳定问题，实现高效高质量蒸馏。

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) have improved diffusion and flow models by semantically richer latent space owing to the strongly label-wise clustered DINO features in the pretrained encoders. Yet in the distillation stage, the severe anisotropy and large curvatures caused by the rich semantic representations would hinder the convergence and performance, making the trajectory-based distillation unstable. In this work, we argue that the RAE latent space is compatible with distillation via the newly proposed Drifting Models. We first quantitatively study the curvatures and isotropy statistics across different autoencoders, and theoretically reveal that Drifting Model itself is highly likely to fail on extremely scattered spaces like reconstruction-based VAEs. These motivate us to apply the drifting paradigm directly to representation autoencoders. Our proposed method, Drift-RAE, distills pretrained flow models in RAE latent spaces using Drifting, together with insightful modifications that improve training stability by thereotically aligning drifting fields with other frameworks. Regarding the experimental evidences, we achieve 1.77 FID on ImageNet 256 dataset using only 10k distillation steps, surpassing state-of-the-art RAE distillation methods and appearing comparative with the original Drifting Model without requiring an auxiliary MAE feature extractor. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [78] [Localizing Credit at the Divergence: Path-Conditioned Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15576)
*Yu Li,Shu Hong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD)方法，通过将教师模型条件设置为当前训练批次中的成功同伴轨迹（而非仅最终答案），为强化学习提供密集的逐token信用分配信号。HSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B的数学与代码基准上优于GRPO变体和在线自蒸馏基线，尤其在简洁答案任务（如AIME）上提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习为每条轨迹分配单一标量，导致长推理链中逐token信用分配不明确。在线自蒸馏虽能通过以特权信息为条件的教师模型生成密集信号，但使用真实答案作为条件仅在终点提供信号，在中间位置缺乏路径级指导。

Method: HSD将教师模型条件设置为当前训练批次中成功的同伴轨迹（即成功条件下的策略样本），无需额外采样。通过提供完整的成功延续而非仅最终答案，信用信号集中在失败轨迹与成功同伴的差异位置。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B的数学与代码基准上，HSD优于GRPO变体和在线自蒸馏基线，在简洁答案任务（如AIME）上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: HSD通过利用成功同伴轨迹作为教师条件，有效解决了在线自蒸馏在简洁答案任务中信用分配稀疏的问题，为长推理链提供了更密集、更准确的逐token学习信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards assigns a single scalar to each rollout, leaving token-level credit assignment underspecified in long reasoning traces. On-policy self-distillation addresses this by letting the same model act as a teacher conditioned on privileged information, producing a dense per-token signal. But the common choice of a ground-truth answer is only an endpoint cue: on terse-answer tasks, the teacher falls silent at the intermediate positions where path-level guidance matters most. We propose Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD), which conditions the teacher on a successful peer rollout drawn from the current training group. Such a peer is an exact sample from the success-conditioned policy, requiring no additional sampled rollouts. By providing a full successful continuation rather than only the final answer, the resulting credit signal concentrates at the divergence position between a failed rollout and a successful peer. Across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B on math and code benchmarks, HSD obtains the best result against GRPO variants and on-policy distillation baselines, with the largest gains on terse-answer tasks such as AIME.

</details>


### [79] [Is Code Better Than Language for Algorithmic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15589)
*Terry Tong,Yu Feng,Surbhi Goel,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分离中间表示与执行机制，发现工具增强型语言模型的性能提升主要来自可靠的外部执行（确定性代码执行比自然语言推理高31.6pp），而非中间表示本身的改变（中间干预仅+0.15pp差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究难以区分工具增强型语言模型中自然语言推理与代码执行管线的性能差异究竟来自中间表示的改变还是执行机制的不同，本文旨在分离这两个因素以明确性能提升的真正来源。

Method: 设计中间干预实验：模型将推理表达为可执行代码，然后让语言模型在上下文中模拟执行。在40个可验证算法基准任务上比较确定性代码执行、自然语言推理和中间干预。同时构建统计决策理论模型刻画执行何时主导端到端风险，并使用代理语言模型从代码表示重建自然语言推理轨迹进行验证。

Result: 确定性代码执行比自然语言推理高31.6pp，而中间干预与自然语言推理差异仅+0.15pp。重建实验恢复的性能与原始自然语言推理管线相当。

Conclusion: 在评估设定下，工具使用的性能增益主要来自可靠的外部执行机制，而非中间表示的改变。分离的轨迹生成/执行机制中，外部执行更能主导端到端风险。

Abstract: For tool-augmented language models, comparing natural-language reasoning with code-execution pipelines is difficult because the comparison changes both the intermediate representation and the execution mechanism. We separate these factors with an intermediate intervention: the model expresses its reasoning as executable code, and the language model simulates that code in context to produce an answer. On a 40-task verifiable algorithmic benchmark, deterministic code execution outperforms natural-language reasoning by +31.6pp. We observe that the intermediate intervention is not meaningfully different from natural-language reasoning (+0.15pp). These results suggest that, in our evaluated setting, changing the intermediate representation alone does not explain the tool-use advantage, providing evidence for the performance gains requiring reliable external execution. We formalize this intuition with a simple statistical decision-theoretic model that characterizes when execution dominates end-to-end risk in our disentangled trace-generation/execution regime. We validate our theory using a reconstruction intervention that leverages a proxy language model to infer natural-language reasoning traces from code representations, recovering performance comparable to the original natural-language reasoning pipeline. All experiments are at https://github.com/TerryTong-Git/ToolProj.

</details>


### [80] [MoECa: Aligning Feature Reuse with Expert Decomposition in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15615)
*Maoliang Li,Haojing Chen,Jiayu Chen,Zihao Zheng,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoECa是一种面向DiT-MoE模型的细粒度缓存框架，通过在专家分支级别进行跨时间步的特征复用，结合专家感知自适应控制和同步缓存更新，实现了最高2.83倍的推理加速，同时保持最小质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Diffusion Transformers with Mixture-of-Experts (DiT-MoE)模型在稀疏激活下提升了模型容量，但扩散推理仍受限于跨时间步的冗余计算。现有缓存方法主要在token级别操作，在DiT-MoE中由于每个token更新内部被分解为多个路由专家分支，token级缓存变得次优。

Method: 提出MoECa框架：1）在专家分支级别进行跨时间步的特征复用（而非token级）；2）引入专家感知自适应控制；3）在MoE和注意力路径之间实现同步缓存更新，以维持稳定的中间状态。

Result: 在多个DiT-MoE模型上，MoECa相比现有缓存方法实现了更好的速度-质量权衡，最高可达2.83倍推理加速，且质量退化极小。

Conclusion: DiT-MoE中的跨时间步冗余在专家分支级别比token级别更易表征，MoECa通过细粒度的分支级缓存有效解决了这一冗余问题，显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers with Mixture-of-Experts (DiT-MoE) improve model capacity under sparse activation, but diffusion inference is still bottlenecked by redundant computation across timesteps. Existing caching methods mainly operate at the token level, which becomes suboptimal in DiT-MoE because each token update is internally decomposed into multiple routed expert branches. Our analysis shows that cross-timestep redundancy in DiT-MoE is better characterized at the expert-branch level than at the whole-token level. Based on this observation, we propose MoECa, a fine-grained caching framework that performs branch-level feature reuse across timesteps. MoECa further introduces expert-aware adaptive control and synchronized cache updates across MoE and attention paths to maintain stable intermediate states. Experiments on multiple DiT-MoE models show that MoECa consistently achieves a better speed-quality trade-off than prior caching methods, with up to 2.83$\times$ inference speedup and minimal quality degradation.

</details>


### [81] [Re-feeding Is Not Replaying: Measuring Replay Noise in Counterfactual Token-Credit Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15621)
*Nils Matteson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了逐Token反事实信用估计中“重新馈送（re-feed）”方法的准确性，发现该方法在低边际决策Token上会导致14-28个百分点的估计偏差（相对于复制基线），建议恢复解码状态或使用批不变内核，并报告复制基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实信用估计方法通过重新馈送前缀文本作为提示来近似模型生成时的状态，但这种近似的代价尚未被量化测量。

Method: 设计三遍实验：从验证的解码时KV状态恢复延续（精确恢复）、完全相同的第二遍精确传递（复制基线）、重新馈送传递。在6种配置、3个模型（含GRPO训练检查点）上测量。

Result: 在低边际决策Token上，重新馈送改变信用估计的比率比复制基线高14-28个百分点（条件独立下7-21pp）；关键Token选择Jaccard系数0.34-0.90（复制上限0.63-0.96）；复制通过本身在9-23%的估计上存在差异。

Conclusion: 反事实信用研究应恢复解码器状态或使用批不变内核，并报告复制基线；单样本信用测量在决策Token上不可靠。

Abstract: Per-token counterfactual credit estimation asks which token in a language-model rollout caused the final answer to be right or wrong: cut the transcript at a pivot, substitute an alternative token, replay continuations, and compare outcomes. Published methods re-feed the transcript prefix as a fresh prompt, assuming this reproduces the state the model passed through during generation. We measure what that assumption costs on a stock inference engine, with a three-pass design: continuations resumed from the verified decode-time KV state, an identical second exact pass (a replica noise floor), and a re-feed pass. Across six configurations and three models (including a GRPO-trained checkpoint), at low-margin decision tokens, re-feeding changes the credit estimate at rates 14-28 percentage points above the replica floor (7-21pp under a treatment-independent conditioning; problem-clustered t = 2.9-6.4). Most changes are zero-boundary crossings of the quantized estimator rather than polarity reversals, and the perturbation is consistent with mean-zero, so averaged quantities are largely safe; but selection is not: a critical-token set chosen by thresholding $|\hat{A}_t|$ under re-feed overlaps the exact-resume selection at Jaccard 0.34-0.90, versus a 0.63-0.96 replica ceiling. A causal confirmation closes the loop: under vLLM's batch-invariant kernels all three passes are identical on every measured channel, with both disagreement rates exactly zero. Replica passes themselves disagree on 9-23% of eligible estimates: single-sample credit measurements at decision tokens are unreliable under any replay. Settings were fixed in advance; exact-pass cache hits in the second campaign are instrumented (100% hit rate, 3,434 pivots); total compute was under 10 USD. We recommend that counterfactual credit studies resume decoder state or use batch-invariant kernels, and report a replica floor.

</details>


### [82] [Surprise-Guided MergeSort: Budget-Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Ranking via Adaptive Comparison Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15623)
*Yujin Park,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Surprise-Guided MergeSort (SGS)框架，通过VLM引导的"惊喜度"指标优先排序需要人工判断的成对比较，在排序任务中仅用O(n log n)次比较即可达到更优效果，在6个基准上Kendall's τ×100提升+6到+12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较是主观排序的黄金标准，但穷举注释需要O(n²)次人工比较；排序方法虽降至O(n log n)，仍需为每次比较支付昂贵人工成本。目标是在保持排序质量的前提下进一步减少人工参与。

Method: 提出SGS框架，包含三个组件：(1) 自底向上归并排序调度器，利用传递性结构化比较；(2) 复合惊喜度评分器，融合去位置偏置的VLM置信度、Elo差距和投票熵，量化比较模糊性；(3) 自适应预算分配器，将高惊喜度比较路由给人类，低惊喜度比较通过传递性推理自动完成。

Result: 在STS-B、BIOSSES、SICKR-STS、KonIQ-10k、TID2013、LIVE Challenge六个基准上验证。SGS每轮会话可识别并跳过最多535个无信息比较，在相同预算下Kendall's τ×100比Active Elo提升+6到+12。

Conclusion: 将VLM引导的惊喜度指标与算法排序相结合，能在多种领域提供一致且高效的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Pairwise comparison is the gold standard for subjective ranking tasks; however, exhaustive annotation requires a massive number of human comparisons ($O(n^2)$). While sorting-based methods have reduced this burden to $O(n\log n)$, they still require expensive human judgment for every single comparison. To further improve annotation efficiency, we propose leveraging a Vision-Language Model (VLM) not as an annotator replacement, but as a \emph{question prioritizer} to identify which comparisons genuinely require human judgment. The proposed \textbf{Surprise-Guided MergeSort (SGS)} framework achieves this through three integrated components: (1) a bottom-up MergeSort scheduler that structures comparisons and exploits transitivity, (2) a composite Surprise Scorer -- combining position-bias-cancelled VLM confidence, Elo gap, and vote entropy -- to quantify comparison ambiguity, and (3) an adaptive budget allocator that routes high-surprise pairs to humans while automating low-surprise pairs via transitivity inference. Validation was conducted on six diverse benchmarks spanning text similarity (STS-B, BIOSSES, SICKR-STS) and image quality assessment (KonIQ-10k, TID2013, LIVE Challenge). SGS effectively identified and skipped up to 535 non-informative comparisons per session. Consequently, it achieved Kendall's $τ{\times}100$ improvements of $+6$ to $+12$ over Active Elo under the same total budget. These results demonstrate that combining VLM-guided surprise metrics with algorithmic sorting provides a generally consistent accuracy-efficiency trade-off across diverse domains.

</details>


### [83] [Conflict-Aware Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15625)
*Yijun Lu,Zihan Fang,Pengpeng Qiao,Zheng Lin,Jing Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Por Lip Yee,Zhe Chen,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中MoE模型因数据异构导致同索引专家优化冲突的问题，本文提出FC-MoE框架，通过重要性加权、梯度共识投影和局部知识保留机制，在非独立同分布环境下加速收敛并提升全局与局部模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的MoE模型在数据异构场景下，客户端特定数据分布导致相同索引的专家在聚合时因特征-标签关联不一致而产生破坏性干扰，使优化轨迹不稳定、模型性能下降。

Method: 提出FC-MoE框架，包括：(1) 重要性感知加权方案，优先使用可靠局部更新；(2) 梯度共识投影，抑制冲突更新以保证稳定全局优化；(3) 局部知识保留机制，通过重新锚定领域特定残差来保留客户端专业知识。

Result: 大量实验表明FC-MoE在非独立同分布联邦环境中加速收敛，并同时提升全局和局部模型性能。

Conclusion: FC-MoE通过冲突感知的聚合策略有效解决了联邦MoE训练中数据异构引起的专家优化冲突问题，为隐私保护的协同优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The continuous scaling of large language models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational costs, making Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) a scalable alternative for efficient fine-tuning via sparse activation. While federated learning (FL) emerges as the paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative optimization, integrating MoE into FL under data heterogeneity may trigger conflicting expert optimizations. Client-specific data distributions force same-indexed experts to optimize under inconsistent or even conflicting feature-label correlations. This mismatch induces destructive interference during aggregation, thus destabilizing the optimization trajectory and degrading model performance. To address this issue, we propose FC-MoE, a federated conflict-aware framework for MoE fine-tuning. It employs an importance aware weighting scheme to prioritize reliable local updates and utilizes gradient consensus projection to suppress conflicting updates, ensuring a stable global optimization path. Moreover, a local knowledge retention mechanism further preserves specialized client expertise by re-anchoring domain-specific residuals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FC-MoE accelerates convergence and enhances both global and local model performance in non-IID federated environments.

</details>


### [84] [Formalizing and Mitigating Structural Distortion in LLM Attention for Zero-Shot Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15633)
*Donald Loveland,Puja Trivedi,Ari Weinstein,Edward W Huang,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了将LLMs应用于文本属性图时，图线性化导致的带宽依赖性注意力衰减问题，并提出了轻量级推理时修改方法GaLA，通过偏置注意力以关注图邻接节点，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究虽观察到图线性化会损害LLMs在图推理中的表现，但常归因于提示设计或模型规模，其根本机制尚不明确。本文旨在阐明该性能下降的底层原因。

Method: 首先理论分析证明了旋转位置编码（RoPE）如何将图线性化转化为带宽依赖的注意力衰减。进而提出GaLA，一种轻量级推理时修改方法，通过偏置注意力权重以关注图邻接节点，同时保留LLM的序列归纳偏置。

Result: 在多个TAG基准测试上，GaLA以极小开销提升了LLM的图推理性能，验证了失真问题是一个可纠正的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 图线性化导致的注意力错位是可纠正的，将LLM图推理的关注点从提示工程和缩放转向注意力校准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for reasoning over Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs). However, applying LLMs to graphs requires linearizing their structure into sequences, introducing distortion rooted in the graph bandwidth problem. While this distortion has been shown to degrade performance, it is often attributed to prompt design or model scale, leaving the underlying mechanism unclear. In this work, we show \textit{how} rotary positional embeddings turn graph linearization into bandwidth-dependent attention decay, suppressing attention between graph-adjacent nodes that are forced far apart in the serialized sequence. This shifts the focus of LLM-based graph reasoning from prompt engineering and scaling toward correcting attention misalignment. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \textbf{G}raph-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{L}anguage \textbf{A}ttention (\textbf{GaLA}), a lightweight, inference-time modification for LLMs. GaLA biases attention toward graph-adjacent nodes while preserving the LLM's sequential inductive biases. Across TAG benchmarks, GaLA improves performance with negligible overhead, demonstrating that distortion is a correctable bottleneck in LLM-based graph reasoning.

</details>


### [85] [HAPI-EP: Towards Hybrid, Adaptive, and Predictive Digital Twins of Cardiac Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15637)
*Sumeet Vadhavkar,Xiajun Jiang,Yubo Ye,Maryam Toloubidokhti,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAPI是一个AI框架，通过构建混合（物理-神经网络）、自适应（元学习快速推理）和可预测的数字孪生模型，解决了心脏数字孪生中动态适应个体实时数据和预测能力不足的核心挑战，并在心脏电生理学中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性心脏数字孪生虽在个性化医疗中潜力巨大，但其快速动态适应个体实时数据及适应后的预测能力是核心挑战。现有方法中，机理模型与数据驱动模型各有优劣，而基于重建目标的优化策略导致模型不可识别，限制了预测性能。

Method: HAPI框架包含三个关键部分：1) 构建物理集成的灰盒模型，以可解释的机理骨干网络为基础，辅以神经网络组件建模残差；2) 通过前馈元学习器实现混合模型参数（机理与神经网络）的摊销推理，从而快速适应小样本实时数据；3) 该自适应过程等价于构建条件生成模型，赋予模型理论可识别性。在心脏电生理学中，使用混合单域模型（机理反应动力学+神经图扩散）进行验证。

Result: 在合成和真实数据研究中，HAPI的机理-神经网络混合与预测性自适应对于获得具有强预测能力和分布外泛化能力的可识别数字孪生模型至关重要。

Conclusion: HAPI通过混合建模与元学习自适应有效解决了心脏数字孪生的动态适应与预测能力难题，为个性化医疗中的可识别、可预测数字孪生提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: A digital twin (DT) of a patient-specific heart offers significant potential in personalized medicine. However, its rapid and dynamic adaptation to an individual's live data and its predictive capability after adaptation remains central challenges. We examine this challenge from its two building blocks: DT formulation where mechanistic and data-driven models show competing merits and limitations, and DT optimization strategies that are largely driven by a reconstruction objective leading to un-identifiable models. We address both bottlenecks via HAPI -- an AI framework for building hybrid, adaptive, and predictive DTs with three key enablers. First, HAPI constructs a physics-integrated gray-box model in which an interpretable mechanistic backbone is augmented by a neural component that models its residual to the observed data. Second, rather than attempting to pre-encode all possible variations in a static hybrid model, HAPI enables rapid on-the-fly adaptation of the hybrid model to few-shot live data, achieved by feedforward meta-learners realizing amortized inference of both mechanistic and neural parameters of the hybrid model trained with predictive objectives. Finally, we show that this adaptivity corresponds to the construction of a conditional generative model (i.e., the hybrid DT) that endows it with theoretical identifiability and thus strong performance in predictive scenarios. We demonstrate the proof-of-concept of HAPI in cardiac electrophysiology using a hybrid monodomain model with mechanistic reaction kinetics and neural graph diffusion. Across synthetic and real-data studies, we show that HAPI's mechanistic-neural hybridization and predictive adaptation are critical for obtaining identifiable DTs with strong predictive and out-of-distribution capabilities.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-Agent Framework for Audit Risk Assessment with Explicit Uncertainty and Evidence Conflict Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15640)
*Yuhan Wang,Manqing Wang,Yixuan Lu,Zhaoyue Peng,Shengda Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UMAR框架，通过三个专业智能体（MD&A文本、财务比率、CAM）独立评估审计风险，并利用Dempster-Shafer证据理论融合风险评分，同时量化智能体间冲突。在3,200份SEC 10-K数据集上，UMAR的AUROC达0.782，预期校准误差最低（0.052），优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计风险评估方法通常产生点预测，无法量化不同证据源之间的一致程度，缺乏对证据冲突的显式建模。

Method: 采用三个专业化智能体（MD&A文本、财务比率、CAM）分别生成带校准不确定性的独立风险评分，再通过基于Dempster-Shafer证据理论的不确定性聚合器融合评分，并显式测量智能体间冲突。

Result: AUROC 0.782, PR-AUC 0.341, ECE 0.052, 均优于LR、XGBoost、FinBERT及单/双智能体LLM基线；证据冲突模式与实际重述风险相关。

Conclusion: UMAR通过显式建模证据冲突和不确定性，提供了更可解释且可操作的审计风险信号，有助于提升审计风险评估的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Audit risk assessment increasingly benefits from combining heterogeneous evidence sources, yet existing approaches typically produce point predictions without quantifying how well different evidence streams agree. We propose UMAR (Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Agent Risk Assessment), a framework that employs three specialized agents: an MD&A Text Agent, a Financial Ratio Agent, and a CAM Agent, each producing independent risk scores with calibrated uncertainty estimates. An Uncertainty Aggregator based on Dempster-Shafer evidence theory fuses these scores while explicitly measuring inter-agent conflict. We evaluate UMAR on a U.S. dataset of 3,200 firm-year observations from SEC 10-K filings (2019-2023), with financial restatement as the target label. Experimental results show that UMAR achieves an AUROC of 0.782 and a PR-AUC of 0.341, outperforming logistic regression, XGBoost, FinBERT, and single-agent and dual-agent LLM baselines. UMAR attains the lowest expected calibration error (ECE = 0.052) among all methods and identifies evidence-conflict patterns that correlate with actual restatement risk, offering auditors potentially actionable and interpretable risk signals.

</details>


### [87] [CIWI-CKT: Chaos-Informed Wave Interference Feature Fusion and Cross-City Knowledge Transfer for Traffic Flow Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15642)
*Abdul Joseph Fofanah,Lian Wen,David Chen,Shaoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIWI-CKT框架，融合混沌感知波生成、元干扰处理和混沌感知元学习，在跨城市数据稀缺场景下显著提升交通流预测精度，并降低训练数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市数据稀缺场景下，有限历史数据导致模型泛化能力不足；现有方法将交通视为纯确定性过程，缺乏对波状干扰模式的建模能力，难以捕捉跨区域交通动态的混沌特性。

Method: 提出CIWI-CKT框架，包含三大创新：1）混沌感知波生成：提取可测量的混沌不变量，将交通建模为自适应波分量；2）元干扰处理：捕获支持集与查询集间的波相互作用，生成预测置信度分数；3）混沌感知元学习：在保持混沌特征的同时实现高效跨城市知识迁移。并提供混沌到波的稳定性、波诱导降维及元学习泛化界等理论保证。

Result: 在四个真实交通数据集上，CIWI-CKT显著优于SOTA时空图学习、迁移学习、提示学习和少样本方法，在提升预测精度的同时大幅降低了所需训练数据量。

Conclusion: 混沌信息与波干扰融合的跨城市知识迁移框架能有效解决数据稀缺下的交通流预测难题，理论与实验共同验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Accurate traffic flow prediction remains challenging in cross-city, data-scarce scenarios where limited historical data hinders model generalisation. The chaotic nature of traffic dynamics, complex spatio-temporal dependencies, and heterogeneous urban networks complicate few-shot learning across cities. Existing deep learning approaches either treat traffic as purely deterministic or lack mechanisms to model wave-like interference patterns essential for cross-regime traffic dynamics. To address these limitations, this paper proposes CIWI-CKT, a novel Chaos-Informed Wave Interference Feature Fusion framework with Cross-City Knowledge Transfer. Our framework introduces three core innovations: chaos-informed wave generation that extracts measurable chaos invariants and models traffic as adaptive wave components; meta-interference processing that captures wave interactions between support and query regimes while producing a predictability score for confidence estimation; and chaos-aware meta-learning that enables efficient cross-city knowledge transfer while preserving chaotic characteristics. We establish theoretical guarantees including chaos-to-wave stability, wave-induced dimension reduction, and meta-learning generalisation bounds. Extensive experiments on four real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that CIWI-CKT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art spatio-temporal graph learning, transfer learning, prompt-based, and few-shot methods, improving prediction accuracy while substantially reducing required training data.

</details>


### [88] [MosaicQuant: Inlier-Outlier Disaggregation for Unified 4-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15652)
*Yangjia Hu,Haodong Wang,Zicong Hong,Qianli Liu,Quanxin Shou,Jian Lin,Song Guo,Xiaowei Shen,Xiangjun Huang,Dian Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MosaicQuant提出了一种统一的4比特LLM量化范式，通过将权重矩阵分解为稠密4比特基分量和稀疏4比特残差分量来实现内点-离群点分离，并配合ZipperEngine将稀疏块计算融合到稠密GEMM核中，在保持近FP16精度的同时实现高达1.24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4比特量化在处理LLM中的内点（密集常见值）和离群点（罕见大值）时精度严重下降，而混合精度方法虽保留离群点高精度但破坏了低比特执行的一致性，引入精度转换和额外数据移动，削弱了实际加速效果。

Method: 提出内点-离群点分离原则，将完整权重矩阵量化为稠密4比特基分量（忠实捕捉内点，离群点被量化）和稀疏4比特残差分量（补偿量化误差，选择性针对误差最大的权重块）。配合ZipperEngine通过重叠流水线将稀疏块计算融合到稠密4比特GEMM核中，实现表示和执行的统一。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3上的实验表明，MosaicQuant保持接近FP16的精度，同时相比W16A16基线实现高达1.24倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过表示和执行的双重统一，MosaicQuant在保持精度和实现加速之间取得了有效平衡，为低比特LLM量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization significantly reduces the memory footprint and accelerates the inference of large language models (LLMs). However, its limited bit-width representation struggles to faithfully capture both dense common values (\emph{inliers}) and rare large-magnitude values (\emph{outliers}), causing substantial accuracy degradation. Existing mixed-precision methods mitigate this by retaining outliers in high precision, but at the cost of breaking the uniformity of low-bit execution, introducing precision conversion and extra data movement that undermine practical speedup. We propose \textbf{MosaicQuant}, a unified 4-bit LLM quantization paradigm built on a novel principle of \emph{inlier--outlier disaggregation}. Rather than elevating outlier precision, MosaicQuant quantizes the full weight matrix into a dense 4-bit base component, where inliers are captured faithfully while outlier are inevitably quantized. A sparse 4-bit residual component is then introduced to compensate for these quantization errors, selectively targeting the most error-critical weight blocks where output distortion is shown to be concentrated. However, a unified representation alone is insufficient, as naïvely executing the sparse residual as a separate kernel still breaks the unified low-bit inference pipeline. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{ZipperEngine}, which fuses sparse block computation into the dense 4-bit GEMM kernel via an overlapped pipeline, unifying not only the representation but also the execution into a single coherent low-bit inference pipeline. Extensive experiments on LLaMA3 and Qwen3 demonstrate that MosaicQuant preserves near-FP16 accuracy while achieving up to $1.24\times$ speedup over the W16A16 baseline.

</details>


### [89] [Z-Plane Neural Networks: Bounded Geometric Activation Replaces ReLU and LayerNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15669)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的二维相量束，使用Radial Bounding几何激活函数替代ReLU和LayerNorm，证明其具有1-Lipschitz连续性且能防止梯度消失。100层纯MLP在MNIST上达到98.34%准确率，验证了几何激活函数足以实现稳定的深层学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度神经网络依赖ReLU和LayerNorm等机制来防止梯度不稳定，但这些机制会导致神经元死亡、丢失方向信息、破坏特征表示的正交性。受生物轴突调频传输启发，作者希望找到一种替代方案，既能保持深度网络稳定性，又能保留相位（方向）信息。

Method: 提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的2D相量束；引入Radial Bounding激活函数（x/max(1, ||x||_2)），通过限制能量幅度同时保留相位（方向）来实现各向同性激活；从数学上证明该激活保持1-Lipschitz连续性，通过保留切向梯度防止梯度消失。

Result: 100层Z-Plane MLP（完全不含ReLU和LayerNorm）在MNIST数据集上成功收敛，达到98.34%准确率，且具有绝对数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 有界几何激活函数本身足以实现稳定的深度学习，无需ReLU和LayerNorm等传统归一化机制，为构建更深层、更稳定的神经网络提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks rely on Euclidean scalar activations (e.g., ReLU) and global normalization techniques (e.g., LayerNorm) to prevent gradient instability in deep architectures. However, these mechanisms inherently cause dead neurons, discard critical directional information, and destroy the orthogonality of feature representations. Inspired by the frequency-modulation transmission of biological axons, we propose the Z-Plane Neural Network, which maps hidden states into 2D phasor bundles on a hypersphere. We introduce a novel geometric activation function, Radial Bounding($\mathbf{x} / \max(1, \|\mathbf{x}\|_2)$), which limits the energy magnitude while preserving the phase (direction). We demonstrate mathematically that this isotropic activation maintains 1-Lipschitz continuity and prevents gradient vanishing by preserving tangential gradients. Empirically, a 100-layer Z-Plane Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-entirely devoid of ReLU and LayerNorm-successfully converges on the MNIST dataset with 98.34% accuracy and absolute numerical stability, proving that bounded geometric activation alone is sufficient for stable deep learning.

</details>


### [90] [The Reservoir Attention Network: Cross-Pass State in Pretrained Transformers via Content-Addressable Reservoir Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15678)
*Emma Leonhart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reservoir Attention Network (RAN)，通过在预训练 Transformer 的中间层注意力中注入固定随机初始化的储层，实现跨前向传播的状态携带，并在 GPT-2 和 Qwen2.5 系列模型上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否仅靠未经训练的循环动态（固定随机储层）就足以在 Transformer 的前向传播间携带可用的跨步状态，为计算受限环境下的 always-alive agent 提供轻量级方案。

Method: 将固定、随机初始化的储层（Reservoir）注入预训练 Transformer 的中间层注意力中，储层未经训练（固定随机），仅依赖其循环动态特性来跨前向传播传递状态。

Result: 在 GPT-2 (124M, 355M) 和 Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) 上，使用最小探针任务验证了固定随机储层能够携带可用的跨步状态。

Conclusion: 未训练的固定随机储层足以实现跨前向传播的状态携带，训练储层是一个互补但更昂贵的方向；always-alive agent 愿景属于计算受限的未来工作。

Abstract: A feasibility and dynamics study of the Reservoir Attention Network (RAN), an architecture that injects a fixed, randomly-initialized reservoir into the mid-layer attention of a pretrained transformer to carry state across forward passes. Experiments span GPT-2 (124M, 355M) to Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) on a single consumer GPU. The tasks are minimal probes chosen to isolate individual mechanisms; the broader always-alive agent vision is treated throughout as compute-limited future work, not a claim of this paper. The reservoir is left untrained (fixed random) by design: this isolates whether untrained recurrent dynamics alone suffice to carry usable cross-pass state, leaving trained recurrence as a complementary, more expensive direction.

</details>


### [91] [ReQAT: Achieving Full-Precision Reasoning Accuracy with 4-bit Floating-Point Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15682)
*Janghwan Lee,Sihwa Lee,Jinseok Kim,Yongjik Kim,Jieun Lim,Jinwook Oh,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReQAT框架，通过面向推理的FP4量化训练（含Trace-Aligned QAT、选择性熵最小化、量化友好初始化），解决大推理模型在W4A4KV4量化下的推理退化问题，在相同训练预算下超越BF16微调精度，并实现最高3.9倍吞吐加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）虽通过长思维链实现强问题求解能力，但其部署受限于全精度推理的高成本和不断增长的KV缓存占用。现有的FP4全量化（W4A4KV4）会导致严重的推理退化，而现有的PTQ和QAT方法无法恢复，亟需一种面向推理特性的量化训练方法。

Method: 提出ReQAT框架，包含三个组件：(i) Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ)，通过回溯相同推理轨迹来聚焦低熵关键决策位置的更新；(ii) Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM)，在低熵位置增强置信度；(iii) Q-FIT，一种量化友好的初始化方法，联合校准与RoPE一致的KV缓存变换以稳定QAT训练。

Result: 在相同训练预算下，ReQAT不仅恢复且超越了BF16微调精度，在NVIDIA DGX Spark上实现最高3.9倍吞吐加速，在B200上实现3.1倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过识别FP4量化失败集中于低熵token（如数字、运算符等精确符号承诺）这一关键洞察，ReQAT有效解决了LRMs在极端量化下的推理退化问题，实现了精度与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong problem-solving through long chain-of-thought, but their deployment is constrained by the high cost of full-precision inference and growing KV cache footprints. Microscaled FP4 formats enable efficient FP4 deployment; however, fully quantizing weights, activations, and KV caches (W4A4KV4) causes severe reasoning degradation that existing PTQ and QAT fail to recover. We identify that FP4 failures concentrate on low-entropy tokens--precise symbolic commitments such as digits and operators--where quantization noise inflates sampling errors that cascade through reasoning traces. Based on this insight, we propose ReQAT, a reasoning-centric FP4 training framework with three components: (i) Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ), which revisits identical reasoning traces to focus updates on critical low-entropy decisions; (ii) Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM), which reinforces confidence at low-entropy positions; and (iii) Q-FIT, a quantization-friendly initialization that jointly calibrates RoPE-consistent KV cache transformations to stabilize QAT. Under the same training budget, ReQAT not only recovers but surpasses BF16 fine-tuning accuracy, while delivering up to 3.9x throughput speedup on NVIDIA DGX Spark and 3.1x on B200.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Fidelity SINDy: Sparse Discovery of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems with Fidelity-Weighted Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15690)
*Filippo Zacchei,Ana Larrañaga,Attilio Frangi,Andrea Manzoni,Steven L. Brunton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加权稀疏辨识方法（加权SINDy），通过整合集成SINDy和弱SINDy，结合广义最小二乘的加权回归策略，解决异方差噪声下非线性动力系统的辨识问题，并在多个基准系统及双摆多保真度数据上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验和仿真数据常含有异方差噪声（噪声水平不均匀），现有SINDy框架无法有效处理此类数据，导致模型发现不准确。因此需要开发一种能适应可变噪声水平的非线性动力系统辨识方法。

Method: 将集成SINDy（Ensemble SINDy）与弱SINDy（Weak SINDy）结合，采用基于广义最小二乘的加权回归公式，为不同噪声水平的数据分配不同权重，并给出了加权策略的统计理论依据。

Result: 在常微分方程和偏微分方程等多个基准系统上验证了方法的有效性；双摆系统的多保真度数据实验表明，低成本低质量的重复测量能够提升模型恢复效果，在某些情况下甚至可与仅使用高质量数据的重建结果相当或更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权SINDy方法能够有效减轻异方差噪声对动力系统辨识的不利影响，且多保真度数据融合策略在实际应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Data from simulations and experiments are rarely noise-free and often exhibit heterogeneous levels of fidelity. Measurement uncertainty may vary across repeated observations, sensing devices, or even within a single experiment. This work addresses the problem of discovering nonlinear dynamical systems from such inhomogeneous data. We extend the Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems (SINDy) framework to account for variable noise levels by combining Ensemble SINDy and Weak SINDy within a weighted regression formulation derived from generalized least squares. A statistical justification for the weighting strategy is also provided. The methodology is validated on several benchmark systems, including ordinary and partial differential equations. In addition, we show the benefit of multi-fidelity integration for forecasting the dynamics of a double pendulum system. The results confirm that the proposed approach mitigates the adverse effects of heteroscedastic noise and that repeated, low-cost, low-quality measurements can improve model recovery, in some cases matching or outperforming reconstructions obtained using only high-fidelity data.

</details>


### [93] [When Generator Replay Degrades: Projected Rehearsal Orchestration for Heterogeneous Federated Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15695)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Khoa D. Doan,Binh T. Nguyen,Danh Le-Phuoc,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRO和PRO-MAX框架通过投影排练编排和邻域加权记忆对齐，解决了异构任务流下联邦类增量学习中的知识遗忘和表示漂移问题，在图像、文本和图基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FCIL方法在异构任务流下脆弱且难以跨模态迁移，因为客户端观察不同标签子集、任务进度不一致、对相同语义概念的监督不均衡，导致输入空间合成回放效果差。

Method: PRO框架用投影排练编排替代合成输入回放：服务器维护紧凑的类级投影记忆，客户端对当前样本和旧投影记忆进行平衡的伪多任务训练。PRO-MAX进一步引入邻域加权记忆对齐来应对更强的表示漂移，同时保持服务器仅聚合模型更新和记忆统计的轻量原则。

Result: 在图像、文本和图基准上，PRO和PRO-MAX在异构流下改善了记忆保持和最终效用，在同类FCIL中保持竞争力。即便基线获得更大回放预算，在监督不平衡和阶段错位下仍会退化。更大的回放不匹配与更大的下游退化相关，而本方法保持投影记忆与演化表示更好地对齐。

Conclusion: 回放数量不足以解决回放质量失败，投影记忆对齐是异构联邦类增量学习的关键。

Abstract: Federated class-incremental learning (FCIL) becomes substantially harder when clients observe different label subsets, progress through tasks at different stages, and provide uneven supervision for the same semantic concepts. Existing FCIL methods often preserve old knowledge through input-space synthesis, but they can be fragile under heterogeneous task streams and difficult to transfer across modalities. To alleviate such issues, we propose PRO, a framework that replaces synthetic input replay with projected rehearsal orchestration. To remove external pretraining, we evaluate all methods under the same warmup. After this, PRO maintains compact class-level projected memories on the server and allows clients perform balanced pseudo multi-task training over current examples and old projected memories. To handle stronger representation drift, we further introduce PRO-MAX, which augments PRO with neighborhood-weighted memory alignment while preserving the same server-light principle that the server only aggregates model updates and memory statistics. Across image, text, and graph benchmarks, PRO and PRO-MAX improve retention and final utility under heterogeneous streams while remaining competitive in homogeneous FCIL. Even when baselines are given expanded replay budgets, they degrade under supervision imbalance and stage misalignment, indicating that replay quantity alone does not resolve replay-quality failures. Additional weak-task diagnostics further show that larger replay mismatch is associated with larger downstream degradation, while our method keeps projected memories better aligned with the evolving representation.

</details>


### [94] [Robust Transformer-Based One-Step Stock Index Forecasting via Shifted Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15701)
*Tien Thanh Thach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Transformer架构用于股票指数单步预测，结合了余弦退火学习率调度和移位数据增强（SDA）技术。在VN30和S&P 500数据集上的实验表明，相比逆幂调度，余弦退火加预热能持续提升预测精度；SDA显著降低了预测误差和运行间方差，并增强了对超参数选择的鲁棒性。两者的组合取得了最佳性能，表明在基于Transformer的金融预测中，数据增强比增加模型复杂度更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在序列建模中表现出色，但直接应用于金融时间序列面临噪声信号、短记忆动态和分布漂移等挑战，现有方法未能充分解决这些问题。

Method: 提出改进的Transformer架构用于单步股票指数预测，结合余弦退火带预热学习率调度，并设计了一种新颖的移位数据增强（SDA）技术。

Result: 余弦退火调度优于逆幂调度；SDA显著降低预测误差和运行间变异性，提升超参数鲁棒性；两者结合在两个数据集上均取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 数据增强比增加模型复杂度在Transformer金融预测中更重要，所提方法提供了一种实用且计算高效的稳健股票指数预测方案。

Abstract: Transformers have shown remarkable success in sequence modeling, yet their direct application to financial time series remains challenging due to noisy signals, short-memory dynamics, and distributional shifts. This paper proposes a modified Transformer architecture for one-step stock index forecasting, combined with advanced learning-rate scheduling and a novel Shifted Data Augmentation (SDA) technique. We evaluate the proposed framework on two benchmark stock index datasets, VN30 and S&P 500. Experimental results demonstrate that cosine annealing with warmup consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the generalized inverse-power scheduler. Furthermore, SDA substantially reduces forecasting errors and run-to-run variability while improving robustness to hyperparameter selection. The combination of cosine annealing scheduling and SDA achieved the best performance on both datasets, indicating that data augmentation can play a more important role than increasing model complexity in Transformer-based financial forecasting. These findings provide a practical and computationally efficient approach for robust stock index forecasting in noisy financial environments.

</details>


### [95] [How to Score Experts for One-Shot MoE Expert Pruning: A Unified Formulation and Selection Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15716)
*Zongfang Liu,Jinghui Zhang,Zijian Ma,Guangyi Chen,Xin Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的一步式MoE专家剪枝公式，基于路由频率、门控权重和激活强度三个因素，推导出剪枝准则选择原则：任务无关剪枝应使用基于激活的准则（如MAN和MSAN），而任务特定剪枝可保留路由频率和门控权重信息。实验证明MAN和MSAN在16个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家剪枝准则多为启发式，缺乏统一原则，且没有单一准则在所有场景下普遍最优，因此需要建立一套原理性框架来指导不同部署目标下剪枝准则的选择。

Method: 提出统一公式，将一步式MoE专家剪枝准则组织为路由频率、门控权重和激活强度三个因素的组合。基于此推导出准则选择原则，并设计两种新准则：Mean Activation Norm (MAN) 和 Mean Squared Activation Norm (MSAN)。

Result: 在四种代表性MoE模型和16个多样化基准上，MAN和MSAN在任务无关剪枝设置中持续表现优异，获得前两名的平均排名，相较于最强基线性能提升最高达8.8个点。

Conclusion: 该统一公式系统化地解释了现有启发式准则，并提供了剪枝准则选择的理论指导，所提出的MAN和MSAN是任务无关剪枝中的有效新准则。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models reduce per-token computation through sparse expert activation, yet deployment still requires storing the full expert pool, making one-shot expert pruning a practical approach for reducing memory usage. Although effective, existing criteria are largely heuristic, and no single criterion is universally optimal. Thus, establishing a principle for selecting pruning criteria suited to different deployment objectives remains an important yet largely underexplored problem in one-shot expert pruning. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation for one-shot MoE expert pruning organized around three factors: routing frequency, gate weighting, and activation strength. The formulation yields a criteria selection principle: task-agnostic pruning should favor routed-token-averaged, gate-free activation-based criteria, whereas task-specific pruning can benefit from retaining routing-frequency and gate-weight information. Beyond this principle, the formulation also provides a systematic view of existing heuristic criteria and gives rise to two new task-agnostic criteria, Mean Activation Norm (MAN) and Mean Squared Activation Norm (MSAN). Across four representative MoE models and 16 diverse benchmarks, MAN and MSAN are consistently strong in the task-agnostic setting, obtain the top-two average ranks, and improve average performance by up to 8.8 points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [96] [InstantForget: Update-Free Backdoor Unlearning with Inference-Time Feature Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15730)
*Zhenyu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InstantForget，一种无需参数更新、仅在推理阶段执行的木马后门遗忘方法，通过干净校准的门控重置机制，利用马氏距离标记异常特征并将其推向中性非目标表征，在CIFAR-10上对四种非自适应触发器平均ASR降至0.071，检测AUROC达0.981，并跨六个骨干网络迁移，同时明确了方法在特定场景下的失败边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门遗忘方法大多需要微调或重新训练模型，这在实际部署中成本高昂且不可行。本文希望在**推理阶段、不更新模型参数**的前提下，有效移除恶意触发行为，同时保持模型在干净样本上的正常性能。

Method: 1. 首先审计了一种常见的投影假设（在干净特征与触发特征的配对特征上执行投影），发现该方法仅在BadNets上有效，在WaNet、Blended、SIG上失效（ASR分别为0.683、0.888、0.941），并通过logit三元组间隙（目标边际、目标logit下降、非目标logit上升）预测其失败。2. 提出**InstantForget**：一种干净校准的门控重置机制，利用马氏距离（Mahalanobis score）标记异常特征（即触发特征），然后仅将这些标记的特征向一个中性的非目标表征方向移动，实现后门遗忘。

Result: 在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18上，对四种非自适应触发器平均ASR降至0.071，检测AUROC达0.981；跨六个骨干网络迁移成功；在WaNet、ModelNet10点混合、两种骨干几何结构以及自适应特征紧凑性攻击下出现失败。

Conclusion: InstantForget证明了在无需触发样本和参数更新的推理阶段实现后门遗忘的可行性，但存在明确的失败边界（特定触发器、骨干网络和自适应攻击），为后续推理时后门防御研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Backdoor unlearning aims to remove a malicious trigger behavior from a deployed model while preserving clean utility. We study the update-free inference-time setting, where model parameters remain frozen. First, we audit a common projection assumption under oracle paired clean and triggered features. Projection succeeds mainly on BadNets and leaves WaNet, Blended, and SIG at 0.683, 0.888, and 0.941 ASR on CIFAR-10 ResNet-18. This failure is not explained by spectral compactness, spatial locality, or subspace misalignment. It is predicted by a logit-triplet gap involving the target margin, target-logit drop, and non-target logit rise. We then introduce InstantForget, a clean-calibrated gated reset that flags anomalous features with a Mahalanobis score and moves only flagged features toward a neutral non-target representation. With one fixed operating point selected on held-out triggered validation, InstantForget reduces average ASR to 0.071 across four non-adaptive CIFAR-10 triggers without triggered samples or parameter updates at deployment. It also reaches 0.981 detection AUROC and transfers to six of eight tested backbones. Reported failures under WaNet, ModelNet10 point blend, two backbone geometries, and adaptive feature-compactness attacks define the method's scope.

</details>


### [97] [Unsupervised Learning for Missing Modalities in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15743)
*Hassan Ismkhan,Hamid Bouchahcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UL4M4，一种用于多模态学习中缺失模态问题的无监督框架。该框架通过模态特定归一化和部分模态距离度量实现缺失样本的公平聚类，并利用聚类中心进行迭代贪婪插补，支持任意数量和任意缺失模式的模态。实验表明，即使超过50%模态缺失，该方法仍能取得F1-Micro > 0.7的稳定结果，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中常面临模态缺失的挑战，现有方法往往依赖特定任务或在固定缺失模式下工作，缺乏灵活性和通用性。本文旨在设计一种任务无关、支持任意缺失模式的通用框架，以解决高度不完整场景下的多模态学习问题。

Method: 提出UL4M4框架，包含两个阶段：1) 无监督阶段：使用模态特定归一化消除不同模态的尺度差异，并设计部分模态距离度量实现缺失样本的公平聚类；2) 插补阶段：利用聚类中心进行迭代贪婪插补，填充缺失模态的嵌入。插补模块轻量级，使用冻结编码器，与下游任务解耦。

Result: 在多种高度不完整配置下进行广泛实验，UL4M4在超过50%模态缺失时仍能一致地达到F1-Micro > 0.7，这是首次在此类挑战性场景下取得该成绩；结果在聚类大小变化时保持稳定，并显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: UL4M4是一个灵活、鲁棒且任务无关的缺失模态处理框架，能够有效应对任意缺失模式和任意数量模态的场景，易于集成到任何融合/预测架构中。

Abstract: This paper addresses the missing-modality challenge in multi-modal learning by introducing Unsupervised Learning for Missing Modalities in Multi-Modal Learning (UL4M4), a flexible framework that imputes missing feature embeddings in a task-independent manner before supervised prediction. We propose modality-specific
  normalization and a novel partial-modality distance metric to enable fair clustering of incomplete observations, capturing cross-modal structures while preserving scale-invariance across varying dimensionalities and modality counts. Cluster centers from this unsupervised stage guide an iterative greedy imputation process for any
  missing modalities during training or inference, supporting arbitrary numbers of modalities and arbitrary missing patterns per sample. The imputation module is lightweight, uses frozen encoders, and decouples from the downstream task, allowing easy integration with any fusion/prediction architecture. Extensive experiments under diverse and highly incomplete regimes demonstrate UL4M4's robustness, achieving, to
  the best of our knowledge, the first consistent F1-Micro scores above 0.7 on challenging missing configurations even when more than 50\% of modality slots are missing. Results are also stable across cluster sizes and significantly outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available here: https://github.com/h-ismkhan/Multimodal-Learning-with-Missing-Modalities-via-Unsupervised-Learning.

</details>


### [98] [From Correlation to Causation in Lane Change Prediction for Automated Driving: A Causal Explanation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15756)
*Mohamed Manzour,Aditya Kumar,Augusto Luis Ballardini,Miguel Ángel Sotelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推理的车道变换预测与解释框架，结合专家约束因果发现、深度结构因果建模（DECI）、干预效应分析等方法，在预测准确率（F1>95%）之外，实现了对预测变量的因果链解释和直接/间接效应区分，推动从相关性预测向因果推理的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车道变换预测方法主要学习驾驶变量与未来操作之间的统计关联，忽略了输入变量之间的因果依赖关系，导致可解释性受限，尤其是将纵向间隙、相对纵向速度、碰撞时间（TTC）等物理上相关的变量视为独立的扁平输入。

Method: 结合语言特征构建、专家约束因果发现、深度端到端因果推理（DECI）、基于干预的效应分析、反事实检验和递归因果链解释。

Result: 在车道标记穿越事件前3秒内，平均F1分数超过95%；通过干预效应分析区分了有影响变量与弱影响变量，区分了直接贡献变量与中介效应，并生成了对比性因果链解释。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是构建了一个机制感知的车道变换预测流水线，超越了基于相关性的分类方法，实现了更具可解释性的因果推理型操作预测。

Abstract: Lane-change prediction is a central task in intelligent vehicles, where early maneuver anticipation can support safer decision-making. However, many existing approaches mainly learn statistical associations between observed driving variables and future maneuvers, while overlooking the causal dependencies among the input variables themselves. This limits interpretability, especially when physically related variables such as longitudinal gap, relative longitudinal velocity, and Time-To-Collision (TTC) are treated as independent flat inputs. This article presents a causal-inference-based framework for lane-change prediction and explanation. The proposed approach combines linguistic feature construction, expert-constrained causal discovery, deep structural causal modeling with Deep End-to-end Causal Inference (DECI), intervention-based effect analysis, refutation testing, and recursive causal-chain explanation. The objective is not only to predict the future maneuver, but also to identify candidate variables that directly contribute to the prediction, the upstream factors influencing them, and the causal chains through which these effects propagate. The framework achieves average F1-scores above 95% during the first three seconds before the lane-marking crossing event. Beyond prediction accuracy, the framework uses intervention-based effect analysis to distinguish influential from weakly influential variables under the learned causal structure. It further distinguishes candidate direct contributors from mediated effects and generates contrastive causal-chain explanations that clarify why the predicted maneuver is favored and why the alternative maneuvers are less supported. The main contribution is therefore a mechanism-aware lane-change prediction pipeline that moves beyond correlation-based classification toward more interpretable causal reasoning for maneuver prediction.

</details>


### [99] [Visualizing Uncertainty: Spatial Maps of Missing and Conflicting Evidence in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15767)
*Dong Hyun Jeong,Feng Chen,Jin-Hee Cho,Lance M. Kaplan,Audun Jøsang,Soo-Yeon Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性激活图(UAM)框架，结合证据深度学习(EDL)与FullGrad，生成可解释的空间不确定性激活图，区分缺乏证据的空虚(vacuity)和证据冲突的不和谐(dissonance)两种不确定性类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法仅提供标量置信度度量，无法揭示输入中哪些空间区域贡献了不同类型的不确定性，缺乏不确定性量化与可解释性之间的桥梁。

Method: 将证据深度学习(EDL)与Full-Gradient类激活映射(FullGrad)相结合，利用主观逻辑的完整梯度分解属性和信念加权归因，分别生成空虚和不和谐两种不确定性的空间激活图。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，该框架有效解决了不确定性量化与可解释性之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: UAM框架能够提供直观的视觉反馈，帮助评估复杂视觉识别任务中的模型可靠性，识别模型缺乏知识的位置与遇到模棱两可证据的位置。

Abstract: Understanding when and why deep neural networks are uncertain is crucial for deploying reliable machine learning systems in safety-critical domains. While existing uncertainty quantification methods provide scalar measures of model confidence, they offer limited insight into which spatial regions of an input contribute to different types of uncertainty. We propose a novel visualization framework, Uncertainty Activation Map (UAM), that combines Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) with Full-Gradient Class Activation Mapping (FullGrad) to generate interpretable spatial uncertainty activation maps. Our approach distinguishes between two fundamental types of uncertainty: vacuity, representing lack of evidence, and dissonance, capturing conflicting evidence between competing hypotheses. By leveraging the complete gradient decomposition property of FullGrad and the principled uncertainty quantification of Subjective Logic, our method produces theoretically grounded visualizations that highlight specific image regions responsible for model uncertainty. With this framework, vacuity and dissonance activation maps are generated by computing belief-weighted attributions, enabling identification of where models lack knowledge versus where they encounter ambiguous evidence. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively addresses the critical gap between uncertainty quantification and explainability, providing intuitive visual feedback to assess model reliability in complex visual recognition tasks.

</details>


### [100] [Bayesian Networks with Latent Time Embedding for Stage-Aware Causal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15784)
*Nguyen Linh Dan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BN-LTE，一个贝叶斯结构框架，通过潜在时间嵌入和AT(N)约束的有向依赖，在估计疾病伪时间的同时预测tau-PET区域进展，并在ADNI数据上展示了优于基线方法的空间重建能力，同时揭示了中伪时间窗口的淀粉样蛋白敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纵向模型要么将AD生物标志物级联视为固定序列，要么将其视为黑箱预测任务，难以判断生物引导的生物标志物关系何时影响未来区域病理。

Method: 提出BN-LTE：从基线生物标志物谱估计疾病伪时间，根据AT(N)顺序约束有向依赖，使用后验样条变化结构方程将初始多模态测量与未来年度区域tau-PET变化关联。

Result: 在ADNI数据上，BN-LTE在tau进展空间重建方面优于预测基线；恢复出后验阶段变化的AT(N)约束效应；识别出中伪时间窗口的淀粉样蛋白敏感性，并通过g公式对比、AIPW、消融和鲁棒性分析验证。

Conclusion: BN-LTE是一个能够同时预测tau进展并检查阶段依赖的AT(N)级联机制的贝叶斯结构框架，适用于观测性纵向神经影像数据。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is often described through the amyloid-tau-neurodegeneration, or AT(N), cascade. However, most longitudinal models represent this cascade either as a fixed sequence of biomarkers or as a black-box forecasting task. This makes it difficult to determine when biologically guided biomarker relationships influence future regional pathology. In this study, we introduce Bayesian Networks with Latent Time Embedding (BN-LTE), a Bayesian structural framework for stage-aware modeling of AD progression. BN-LTE estimates disease pseudotime from baseline biomarker profiles and constrains directed dependencies according to biologically plausible AT(N) ordering. Posterior spline-varying structural equations are then used to link initial multimodal measurements with future annualized regional tau-PET change. Across repeated subject-disjoint evaluations using ADNI data, BN-LTE shows strong spatial reconstruction of tau progression compared with the included forecasting baselines. Beyond spatial reconstruction, BN-LTE recovers posterior stage-varying AT(N)-constrained effects and identifies a mid-pseudotime window of amyloid sensitivity. This window is supported by model-implied g-formula contrasts, root-adjusted AIPW, mechanism-sensitive ablations, and robustness analyses across spline and prior specifications. Overall, these findings position BN-LTE as a Bayesian structural framework for forecasting tau progression while examining stage-dependent AT(N)-cascade mechanisms in observational longitudinal neuroimaging data. Our code is available at https://github.com/danleneurocom/BN-LTE.

</details>


### [101] [Mean-Field Parallel Decoding for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15805)
*Tamim Zoabi,Ameen Ali,Liran Ringel,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的平行解码框架，通过在每次前向传播中引入基于成对交互的commit分数调整机制，协调离散扩散语言模型的并行token生成，在保持生成质量的同时提升并行提交token数量，改善质量-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型虽然能实现并行token生成以降低解码延迟，但基于边际置信度的独立token选择会导致兼容性冲突——单个看起来可靠的token组合起来可能形成不兼容的配置，限制了并行性的有效利用。

Method: 提出无需训练的解码框架：每次前向传播中，为每个掩码位置分配一个commit分数，然后利用模型预测分布中的成对交互信息来细化这些分数。通过变分松弛方法，得到简单的定点更新公式，在单次前向传播内抑制冲突的并行commit决策。

Result: 在推理和代码生成基准测试中，该方法在质量-延迟权衡方面取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量级、无需辅助模型或重新训练，可直接嵌入现有扩散解码流程，通过协调并行更新有效提升了离散扩散语言模型的解码效率与生成质量之间的平衡。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models enable parallel token generation, offering a pathway to low-latency decoding. However, selecting tokens independently by marginal confidence limits effective parallelism: tokens that appear reliable in isolation can form incompatible configurations when several positions are updated at once. We introduce a training-free decoding framework that coordinates these parallel updates. At each forward pass, the method assigns a commit score to each masked position and refines these scores using pairwise interactions derived from the model's predictive distributions. A variational relaxation yields a simple fixed-point update that suppresses conflicting simultaneous commitments within a single forward pass. This mechanism allows the decoder to commit more tokens in parallel while maintaining competitive generation quality. The method is lightweight, requires no auxiliary model or retraining, and drops into existing diffusion decoding pipelines without modification. Experiments on reasoning and code-generation benchmarks show consistent improvements in the quality-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [102] [Continuous Cross-Domain Traffic State Prediction via Memory-Augmented Graph Liquid Time-Constant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15807)
*Jinrong Xiang,Ming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆增强图液态时间常数网络（MA-GLTC），用于解决跨域交通预测中源-目标域粗粒度适应、未见模式处理能力不足及连续时间动态建模困难等问题，在五个公开数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域交通预测方法存在三个主要局限：源-目标域的粗粒度适应、对目标域未见模式的泛化能力不足、以及难以建模不规则或异质时间条件下的连续交通动态。

Method: 1）构建时空单元（STU）将交通网络分解为可迁移的局部单元，实现细粒度知识对齐；2）提出图液态时间常数网络（GLTC），引入图耦合递归电导，使节点状态可随泄漏、自适应时间常数和邻域感知反馈演化；3）设计基于记忆的迁移存储（MTS）机制，保存源域知识、检索匹配交通模式、更新目标域未见状态模式。

Result: 在五个公开交通数据集上，MA-GLTC在短时和长时预测任务中均优于代表性域内和跨域基线方法，与第二名相比平均预测误差分别降低3.02%、0.33%、8.92%、10.09%和2.11%。

Conclusion: MA-GLTC通过细粒度时空单元分解、连续时间图液态动力学建模和记忆迁移存储机制，有效解决了跨域交通预测中的关键挑战，显著提升了数据稀缺场景下的预测性能。

Abstract: Traffic state prediction is a fundamental task in intelligent transportation systems. In practical applications, some regions suffer from limited traffic observations due to insufficient sensing infrastructure, making cross-domain knowledge transfer an important solution for data-scarce traffic prediction. However, existing cross-domain traffic prediction methods still face several limitations, including coarse-grained source-target adaptation, limited capability in handling unseen target-domain patterns, and insufficient modeling of continuous traffic dynamics under irregular or heterogeneous temporal conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a continuous cross-domain traffic prediction framework, termed Memory-Augmented Graph Liquid Time-Constant Network (MA-GLTC). Specifically, we first construct spatio-temporal units (STUs) to decompose traffic networks into transferable local units, enabling fine-grained knowledge alignment across domains. Then, a graph liquid time-constant network (GLTC) is developed to model graph-coupled traffic evolution in continuous time. Different from generic graph neural ODE-based models, GLTC introduces graph-coupled recurrent conductance into liquid time-constant dynamics, allowing node states to evolve with leakage, adaptive time constants, and neighborhood-aware feedback. Furthermore, a Memory-based Transfer Storage (MTS) mechanism is designed to preserve source-domain knowledge, retrieve matched traffic patterns, and update reliable target-domain patterns when unseen states emerge. Experiments on five public traffic datasets demonstrate that MA-GLTC consistently outperforms representative innerdomain and cross-domain baselines in both short-term and longterm prediction tasks. Compared with the second-best method, MA-GLTC reduces the average prediction errors by 3.02%, 0.33%, 8.92%, 10.09%, and 2.11%, respectively.

</details>


### [103] [Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15812)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Giuseppe Di Fatta,Giuseppe Nicosia,Panos M Pardalos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出布朗核梯子（BKL），一种通过布朗核积分构造递归再生核希尔伯特空间层次结构的方法，证明了其拟巴拿赫空间性质、深度相关Hölder正则性、严格单调性，并给出了正则化经验风险最小化的存在性结果和均匀控制的高斯复杂度界，最终实现了近参数阶的过剩风险保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计学习理论中，构建既能数学上易于处理又能捕捉层次化组合表示的函数空间仍然是一个核心挑战。

Method: 通过布朗核积分构造递归定义的积分再生核希尔伯特空间层次结构（BKL），从线性泛函出发，每一层通过对前一层子集上的概率测度积分布朗核获得；结合递归子集分解和布朗核阈值表示的组合证明技术进行分析。

Result: BKL空间构成拟巴拿赫空间，满足深度依赖的Hölder正则性估计，具有关于深度的严格单调性；正则化经验风险最小化解存在；高斯复杂度界随维度和深度一致有界；过剩风险达到近参数阶。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数学上易于处理的层次化函数空间框架，可用于研究深度学习中的组合表示问题。

Abstract: Constructing mathematically tractable function spaces that capture hierarchical compositional representations remains a central challenge in statistical learning theory. We introduce Brownian kernel ladders (BKLs), a recursively defined hierarchy of integral reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces generated through Brownian-kernel integral constructions. Starting from linear functionals, each layer is obtained by integrating Brownian kernels over probability measures supported on subsets of the previous layer, yielding a recursive function-space model in which depth is encoded directly through the hierarchy.
  Based on this framework, we define canonical BKL spaces together with an associated complexity functional. We establish several analytical and statistical properties of these spaces.
