Building on TD-MPC2 [Hansen et al., 2024], I introduce FlowMPC, a framework that combines an imitation-learned FM policy with a learned world model for test-time planning in ManiSkill manipulation tasks [Tao et al., 2025]. Across PickCube and PickSingleYCB, adding the world model improved performance over the FM policy alone, with especially clear gains in end-of-episode success. These results suggest that world-model-based planning can effectively complement flow-based imitation policies without modifying the FM training objective.

</details>


### [140] [An affordable hardware-aware neural architecture search for deploying convolutional neural networks on ultra-low-power computing platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16290)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Antonio Frisoli,Paolo Gastaldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向超低功耗微控制器的硬件感知神经架构搜索（HW-NAS）方法，能够生成可在嵌入式设备上运行的轻量级CNN，在保持分类精度的同时满足极低功耗约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HW-NAS方法针对高性能微控制器设计，功耗无法满足传感节点等超低功耗设备的需求，亟需一种能够在超低功耗微控制器上生成紧凑CNN的搜索方法。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级HW-NAS方法，搜索过程本身可在嵌入式设备上执行，生成适配超低功耗微控制器的微小CNN。

Result: 在三个微型计算机视觉基准测试上，生成的微小CNN在保持先进分类准确率的同时满足了超低功耗硬件约束。

Conclusion: 提出的HW-NAS方法能够有效生成适合超低功耗微控制器的微小CNN，在准确率和硬件约束之间取得良好平衡，且搜索过程轻量化，可部署于嵌入式设备。

Abstract: Hardware-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS) allows the integration of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in microcontrollers devices by automatically designing neural architectures that can fit prearranged hardware constraints. However, state-of-the-art HW-NAS target high-performance microcontrollers, whose power consumption does not meet sensing nodes requirements. This work presents a HW-NAS generating tiny CNNs that can run on ultra-low-power microcontrollers, featuring a lightweight search procedure enabling its execution even on embedded devices. Empirical results on three well-known benchmarks for tiny computer vision proved that the proposed HW-NAS was able to generate tiny CNNs while preserving state-of-the-art classification accuracy.

</details>


### [141] [pFedUL: Layer-Aware Federated Unlearning for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16304)
*Zhuodong Liu,Xiangyu Li,Zhihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pFedUL，一种面向个性化联邦学习（pFL）的去学习框架，通过层感知的选择性遗忘机制，在保证去学习效果的同时，有效保留剩余客户端个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦去学习（FU）方法主要针对FedAvg范式设计，忽略了实际中广泛使用的个性化联邦学习（pFL）方法。pFL将模型分解为共享全局层和客户端个性化层，这改变了去学习的语义，现有方法无法同时兼顾去学习的完整性和剩余客户端的个性化保留。

Method: 提出pFedUL框架，包含三个组件：(1) 基于梯度的层贡献归因，分别量化目标客户端对共享层和个性化层的影响；(2) 自适应选择性去学习，对不同层类型采用差异化遗忘策略；(3) 轻量级重校准协议，使剩余客户端以最小开销恢复个性化。同时引入两个新指标：个性化保留分数（PPS）和跨客户端公平性指数（CFI）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和FEMNIST上，pFedUL在去学习效果上媲美完整重训练，同时剩余客户端个性化准确率平均保持97.3%。与六种SOTA FU方法相比，pFedUL在个性化保留上持续最优。

Conclusion: pFedUL有效解决了pFL场景下去学习完整性与个性化保留之间的冲突，为个性化联邦学习的合规数据删除提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) enables the removal of specific data contributions from federated learning (FL) models to comply with regulations such as the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). However, most existing FU methods are designed for the FedAvg paradigm, where all clients share a single global model. In practice, personalized federated learning (pFL) methods such as FedPer, FedRep, Ditto, and FedBN have become widely adopted due to their superior handling of non-IID data. These methods decompose the model into shared global layers and client-specific personalized layers, fundamentally altering the semantics of unlearning, yet this setting has received little attention. We formalize FU under the pFL paradigm, identifying a tension between unlearning completeness on shared layers and personalization preservation for remaining clients. We then propose pFedUL, a layer-aware selective unlearning framework comprising three components: (1) gradient-based layer-wise contribution attribution that separately quantifies the target client's influence on shared and personalized parameters, (2) adaptive selective unlearning that applies differentiated forgetting strategies across layer types, and (3) a lightweight recalibration protocol enabling remaining clients to restore personalization with minimal overhead. We further introduce two new metrics, Personalization Preservation Score (PPS) and Cross-client Fairness Index (CFI), to evaluate pFL-specific unlearning quality. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and FEMNIST under varying non-IID settings indicate that pFedUL achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while maintaining an average of 97.3\% personalized accuracy for remaining clients. Compared with six state-of-the-art FU methods adapted to the pFL setting, pFedUL consistently achieves superior personalization preservation.

</details>


### [142] [QK-Normed MLA: QK normalization without full key caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16310)
*Yizhou Han,Yao Zhao,Jun Zhou,Longfei Li,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明QK归一化与多头潜注意力（MLA）兼容，通过将RMSNorm分解为静态仿射权重和动态标量统计量，前者可吸收到查询投影中，后者简化为每个token和KV组的逆RMS标量，从而在不缓存完整键的情况下实现QK归一化。400M参数模型训练至100B token的实验表明，QK归一化MLA相比QK裁剪训练损失更低、下游准确率更好，且H800解码基准测试中256k上下文长度下延迟开销不到2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QK归一化通过控制查询和键的规模来稳定注意力，但与MLA存在表面上的不兼容性，因为MLA通过缓存低维潜状态而非完整键来实现高效解码，而投影后的QK RMSNorm似乎需要每个缓存token的完整投影键。作者试图解决这一矛盾。

Method: 将RMSNorm分解为静态仿射权重和动态标量RMS统计量。静态键侧权重可吸收到MLA的查询侧投影中；动态键统计量简化为每个token和KV组的一个逆RMS标量。得到的公式在精确算术下等价于显式投影后QK RMSNorm，并保持MLA的潜解码路径。

Result: 400M参数模型训练至100B token的实验：QK归一化MLA相比QK裁剪训练损失更低、下游准确率更好。H800解码基准测试显示，在高达256k上下文长度下延迟开销不到2%。

Conclusion: QK归一化可以成为MLA模型的一种实用的稳定化选择，无需缓存完整键。表面上的不兼容性只是实现伪影而非架构约束。

Abstract: Query-key (QK) normalization stabilizes attention by controlling the scale of queries and keys before the dot product, but is not immediately compatible with Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA). MLA achieves efficient decoding by caching low-dimensional latent states instead of full keys, whereas post-projection QK RMSNorm appears to require the fully projected key for every cached token. We show this apparent incompatibility is an implementation artifact, not an architectural constraint. RMSNorm decomposes into a static affine weight and a dynamic scalar RMS statistic. The static key-side weight can be absorbed into the MLA query-side projection; the dynamic key statistic reduces to one inverse-RMS scalar per token and KV group. The resulting formulation is exactly equivalent to explicit post-projection QK RMSNorm in exact arithmetic and preserves MLA's latent decode path. In our 400M runs trained for up to 100B tokens, QK-Normed MLA achieves lower training loss and better downstream accuracy than QK clipping, while H800 decode benchmarks show less than 2% latency overhead up to 256k context. These results make QK normalization a practical stabilization option for MLA models without requiring full-key caching.

</details>


### [143] [Diffusion Offline Reinforcement Learning for Fair and Energy-Efficient UAV-Assisted Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16331)
*Eslam Eldeeb,Hirley Alves*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种扩散软演员-评论家（Diffusion-SAC）方法，将扩散概率模型与离线强化学习相结合，用于无人机网络中的轨迹与调度控制，在有限数据集下实现了更优的能量效率、公平性和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL方法（如CQL）在低数据或动态条件下泛化能力不足，难以应对未来6G网络中智能决策的需求。

Method: 将保守Q学习（CQL）的鲁棒性与去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的生成能力相结合，提出Diffusion-SAC算法，实现具有信号感知能力的策略学习。

Result: 相比标准离线RL基线，Diffusion-SAC收敛更稳定、奖励更高；能耗降低，吞吐量提升超过35%。

Conclusion: 该方法在数据效率、能量消耗和吞吐量方面均优于现有算法，展现了对下一代无线控制系统鲁棒策略学习的潜力。

Abstract: The integration of generative artificial intelligence with wireless communication and signal processing systems has opened new avenues for intelligent, data-driven decision-making in future 6G networks. This work proposes a diffusion soft actor-critic (Diffusion-SAC) approach that leverages offline reinforcement learning (RL) enhanced by denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs) to optimize trajectory and scheduling control in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) networks. While offline RL methods, such as conservative Q-learning (CQL), can learn from static datasets, they often struggle to generalize in low-data or dynamic conditions. To address this, we combine the robustness of CQL with the generative power of diffusion models, enabling expressive and signal-aware policy learning that generalizes beyond behavior policies. Applied to a UAV-assisted wireless network, the proposed framework minimizes transmission energy and improves fairness among devices. Simulations show that Diffusion-SAC outperforms standard offline RL baselines, achieving more stable convergence and higher rewards even with limited datasets. The method enhances data efficiency, reduces energy consumption, and increases throughput by more than 35 % compared to existing algorithms, demonstrating its potential for robust policy learning in next-generation wireless control systems.

</details>


### [144] [Filtered ANN as a Phase Transition: When Selectivity-Estimation Error Causes Plan Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16341)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了带过滤条件的近似最近邻（ANN）查询中，选择性估计误差导致的不同执行策略（预过滤、后过滤、内过滤）之间的性能边界与后悔值（regret），揭示了后悔值仅在策略边界附近以对数宽度楔形出现，并通过序统计和渗流理论确定了两个关键相变边界，实验验证了后悔曲线的有限尺寸标度坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带过滤条件的ANN查询中，最优执行策略（pre-filter, post-filter, in-filter）随选择性 s 动态变化，现有系统需要估计 s 来选择策略，但估计误差可能导致性能损失。作者希望量化这种损失并刻画其几何结构，从而为系统设计提供理论指导。

Method: 将策略选择建模为关于选择性的argmax景观问题，识别出三个策略对应的相及相边界；利用序统计（order statistics）推导后过滤边界 s ~ k/K，利用位点渗流（site percolation）推导内过滤边界 s_c ~ 0.83/M（M为图度数）；定义后悔值为与最优策略相比丢失的召回率，推导出后悔楔形（wedge）的宽度与高度公式；在合成数据和真实SIFT1M数据集上，通过预注册决策规则进行实验验证，并展示有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）坍塌。

Result: 后悔值在边界附近集中约290倍；后悔曲线在两个数量级的语料库规模上呈现一致的有限尺寸标度坍塌现象；真实近似索引不会错位边界，但有偏的成本模型会引入持续性的校准偏差带，无法通过估计误差鲁棒性修复。

Conclusion: 本文提供了对过滤ANN查询中策略选择问题的理论刻画，而非提出新的索引算法；关键贡献是揭示了选择性估计误差的后悔值仅在相边界附近显著，并给出了边界位置的解析预测。

Abstract: A filtered approximate-nearest-neighbor (ANN) query returns the k nearest vectors among those satisfying an attribute predicate P of selectivity s. The best execution strategy -- pre-filter, post-filter, or in-filter -- changes with s, so a system must estimate s and choose. We model this as an argmax over a landscape with phases (regions where each strategy wins) separated by boundaries, and show that selectivity-estimation error produces plan regret -- recall lost versus the oracle strategy -- only in the critical regions around those boundaries. The regret is a wedge of log-width equal to the multiplicative estimation error epsilon and height equal to the local cliff |V'(s*)| epsilon; the flip-margin 1/|V'(s*)| is the condition number of a sibling cardinality-estimation study reappearing as the local boundary theory. The two phase boundaries follow from independent mathematics: order statistics place the post-filter cliff at s ~ k/K, and site percolation places the in-filter cliff at s_c ~ 0.83/M for graph degree M (corpus-size independent). Criticality exists only under a constrained budget B < sqrt(k n). Under pre-registered decision rules we confirm, on synthetic sweeps and real SIFT1M, that regret concentrates ~290x at the boundary and that the regret curves obey a finite-size scaling collapse onto one universal wedge across two decades of corpus size. A real approximate index does not mis-locate the boundary, but a biased cost model opens a persistent miscalibration band that estimation-error robustness cannot fix. The contribution is a characterization, not a new index. Code and the full pre-registration are public.

</details>


### [145] [Communication-Efficient Verifiable Attention for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16352)
*Ziqun Chen,Ming Wu,Michael Heinrich,Jason Zeng,Huiying Lan,Tianwei Zhang,Rui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriAttn，一种面向Transformer大语言模型的通信高效TEE-GPU注意力验证方案，通过将注意力计算全部卸载到GPU并由TEE执行验证，以及采用两级流水线预填充和智能密钥值缓存分区解码策略，相比传统TSDP方法实现2.60-5.42倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程大语言模型服务的计算完整性存在疑问。现有TEE屏蔽DNN分区（TSDP）方法直接应用于Transformer架构LLM会导致显著的TEE计算开销和TEE-GPU通信开销。

Method: VeriAttn将注意力的线性和非线性计算均卸载到GPU，TEE仅执行验证；预填充阶段采用两级流水线重叠数据传输、TEE预处理/后处理和GPU计算；解码阶段当KV缓存超限时，在TEE和GPU间分区注意力以减少重复的键值传输。

Result: 在Intel TDX平台上，VeriAttn在预填充阶段对6k令牌提示实现2.60-3.38倍加速，在解码阶段对10k令牌输出实现3.86-5.42倍加速，相较于TSDP。

Conclusion: VeriAttn通过创新的注意力卸载与验证架构及通信优化策略，有效解决了TSDP在LLM场景下的性能瓶颈，显著加速了可验证的LLM推理过程。

Abstract: Computation integrity of remote large language model (LLM) serving can be questionable. For conventional deep neural networks (DNNs), the existing TEE-shielded DNN partitioning (TSDP) approach uses Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) to compute non-linear components and verify the integrity of linear components offloaded to an untrusted GPU. However, directly applying TSDP to Transformer-based LLMs incurs significant TEE computation and TEE-GPU communication overhead. This paper presents Communication-efficient TEE-GPU Attention (\textsc{VeriAttn}) for accelerating verifiable LLM inference. \textsc{VeriAttn} offloads both linear and non-linear computations of attention to the GPU, while TEE performs verification. Moreover, for prefill, \textsc{VeriAttn} uses a two-level pipeline to overlap data movement, TEE pre-/post-processing, and GPU computation. For decoding, when the key-value cache exceeds available GPU memory, \textsc{VeriAttn} partitions attention across TEE and GPU to reduce repeated key-value transfers. Evaluation on an Intel TDX platform shows that \textsc{VeriAttn} achieves 2.60-3.38$\times$ and 3.86-5.42$\times$ acceleration over TSDP for 6k-token prompts and 10k-token outputs during prefill and decoding, respectively.

</details>


### [146] [Simulation-Augmented Multi-Step Split Conformal Prediction for Aggregated Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16356)
*Andro Sabashvili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-MSCP方法，一种通过块自助法模拟增强的多步分裂共形方法，用于聚合时序预测（如年度总量、同比增长率）的不确定性量化。实验表明该方法在经验覆盖率和增长目标上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在聚合预测任务（如年度总量、同比增长率）的不确定性量化方面存在不足，需要一种有效且通用的框架来提供可靠的预测区间。

Method: 提出SA-MSCP（模拟增强的多步分裂共形方法），通过块自助法从交叉验证残差中生成未来路径，并基于经验分位数构建预测区间。

Result: 实验表明SA-MSCP在聚合目标和增长率目标的经验覆盖率上优于模拟路径基线方法。

Conclusion: 模拟增强的共形校准是一种有效且通用的聚合时序预测不确定性量化框架。

Abstract: We study uncertainty quantification for aggregated forecasting tasks such as annual totals and year-over-year growth rates. We propose SA-MSCP, a simulation-augmented multi-step split conformal method that generates future paths from cross-validated residuals using a block bootstrap and constructs prediction intervals from empirical quantiles. Experiments show that SA-MSCP improves empirical coverage over a simulated-path baseline for aggregated and growth-rate targets. Our results demonstrate that simulation-enhanced conformal calibration is an effective and general framework for uncertainty quantification in aggregated time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [147] [CacheMuon: Using Temporal Preconditioning To Approximate Polar Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16371)
*Bishnu Dev,Sushil Bohara,Martin Takáč,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CacheMuon通过利用动量矩阵极因子在优化步骤间的时间相关性，缓存历史信息来近似当前极因子，从而减少Muon优化器中昂贵的Newton-Schulz正交化计算，在可控范围内实现质量与效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在每个优化步骤都需执行Newton-Schulz迭代来计算动量矩阵的极因子，计算成本高昂。观察到动量矩阵在训练过程中平滑变化，其极因子具有强时间相关性，因此希望利用这一结构特性来减少冗余的正交化计算。

Method: 提出CacheMuon，一种时间预条件方法，通过缓存并复用先前优化步骤的信息来近似当前步骤的极因子。该方法被分析为一种非精确Muon更新，其误差由新鲜求解器误差和缓存陈旧度共同控制。

Result: 在语言模型和视觉训练任务上，保守阈值设置下CacheMuon的结果与原始Muon高度一致，同时减少了正交化FLOPs；激进阈值设置下可获得更大的算术节省，但以适度的验证质量下降为代价。

Conclusion: CacheMuon提供了可控的质量-效率前沿，能够有效减少Muon优化器的计算开销，适用于对训练效率有要求的场景。

Abstract: Muon is an optimizer that computes updates using the polar factor of the momentum matrix and has shown strong empirical performance across a range of training settings. A key component of Muon is the Newton-Schulz iteration used to compute this polar factor. Although this avoids the cost of an exact singular value decomposition, it remains expensive in practice because it is applied at every optimization step. At the same time, the momentum matrix changes smoothly over training, suggesting strong temporal correlation in the corresponding polar factors. In this paper, we exploit this structure and propose CacheMuon, a temporal preconditioning method that reuses information from previous optimization steps to approximate the polar factor at the current step. This reduces redundant orthogonalization computation across iterations. We analyze CacheMuon as an inexact Muon update, with error controlled by fresh-solver error and cache staleness. Empirically, CacheMuon provides a controllable quality-efficiency frontier: conservative thresholds closely match fresh Muon on language-model and vision training while reducing orthogonalization FLOPs, whereas more aggressive thresholds yield larger arithmetic savings at the cost of modest validation-quality degradation.

</details>


### [148] [Mixtures of Subspaces for Bandwidth Efficient Context Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16384)
*Sameera Ramasinghe,Ajanthan Thalaiyasingam,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Yan Zuo,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对去中心化训练的通信高效上下文并行压缩方法，通过利用激活输出的低秩结构实现95%以上压缩率，使百亿参数模型在300Mbps慢速网络上也能达到与100Gbps高速互联集中式训练相当的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文并行方法将输入序列分块后跨设备广播并逐块计算注意力，在高速集群中可行，但不适用于低带宽去中心化训练场景，通信开销过大。

Method: 利用激活输出的内在低秩结构，通过高效重参数化将激活动态约束到学习到的子空间混合上，实现通信压缩。

Result: 压缩率超过95%，开销可忽略且无收敛损失；在300Mbps网络上可将百亿参数模型扩展到超过10万token的上下文长度，墙钟收敛速度与100Gbps互联的集中式模型匹配。

Conclusion: 所提方法使低带宽去中心化训练能够支持超长上下文，弥合了其与高速集中式训练在效率上的差距。

Abstract: Pretraining language models with extended context windows enhances their ability to leverage rich information during generation. Existing methods split input sequences into chunks, broadcast them across multiple devices, and compute attention block by block which incurs significant communication overhead. While feasible in high-speed clusters, these methods are impractical for decentralized training over low-bandwidth connections. We propose a compression method for communication-efficient context parallelism in decentralized settings, achieving a remarkable compression rate of over 95\% with negligible overhead and no loss in convergence. Our key insight is to exploit the intrinsic low-rank structure of activation outputs by dynamically constraining them to learned mixtures of subspaces via efficient reparameterizations. We demonstrate scaling billion-parameter decentralized models to context lengths exceeding 100K tokens on networks as slow as 300Mbps, matching the wall-clock convergence speed of centralized models on 100Gbps interconnects.

</details>


### [149] [Robust Neural Tucker Factorization with Bias Correction and Adaptive Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16388)
*Yuchao Su,Yixin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高维不完整张量补全中非线性动态和多模态非平稳变化导致的难题，本文提出KaBiN模型，通过Kaiming均匀初始化嵌入和Tucker线性参数，并在输出映射中加入简单偏置校正，有效解耦全局均值偏移与局部结构表征，在三个真实数据集上以极低计算开销超越原始NeuTucF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性重建框架难以处理HDI张量中的非线性动态和非平稳多模态变化，而神经张量分解虽能缓解低秩约束，但参数初始化和输出偏置配置等实现级选择会严重影响性能：次优初始化导致方差爆炸和梯度饱和，缺乏平移参数迫使交互权重隐式吸收全局统计偏差。

Method: 提出KaBiN模型：(1) 对嵌入参数和Tucker线性参数采用Kaiming均匀初始化，避免方差爆炸和梯度饱和；(2) 在输出映射中引入简单的偏置校正项，将全局均值偏移从局部结构表示中解耦出来，从而提供稳定且良态的优化景观。

Result: 在三个真实世界HDI张量数据集上的实验表明，KaBiN在补全性能上优于原始NeuTucF，同时仅引入极小的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过Kaiming初始化与偏置校正这两个简单而有效的改进，KaBiN显著提升了神经Tucker分解在HDI张量补全任务中的稳定性和精度，为高维稀疏张量分析提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-dimensional incomplete (HDI) tensors are widely used in traffic and climate applications, but sparse observations make accurate completion difficult. The intrinsic non-linear dynamics and non-stationary variations across distinct multi-modal fields severely hinder the efficacy of conventional linear reconstruction frameworks. Neural Tucker factorization provides an effective framework for modeling high-order interactions among tensor modes. By parameterizing underlying structural characteristics into continuous latent spaces, neural representations circumvent the rigid low-rank constraints of classical algebra. However, its performance can still be affected by implementation-level choices, especially parameter initialization and the bias configuration of the final output mapping. Suboptimal initializations frequently lead to variance explosion across the cubically expanded interaction spaces, driving the subsequent non-linear activation boundaries into severe gradient saturation zones, while the omission of a dedicated translation parameter forces interaction weights to implicitly absorb global statistical deviations. This paper proposes a simple yet effective neural Tucker factorization model with Kaiming initialization and bias correction (KaBiN) for HDI tensor completion. The proposed model utilizes Kaiming uniform initialization for the embedding and Tucker linear parameters, and adopts a simple bias correction in output mapping. By elegantly decoupling global mean shifts from local structural representations, the framework provides a highly stable and well-conditioned optimization landscape. Experiments on three real-world HDI tensor datasets show that KaBiN achieves better performance than the original NeuTucF, while introducing minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [150] [MUNI: Multimodal Unified Latent Diffusion for Coherent Any-to-Any Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16408)
*Kyeongmin Yeo,Yunhong Min,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUNI是一个端到端的多模态潜在扩散框架，通过共享随机潜变量实现任意模态间的条件生成与无条件联合采样，在PolyMNIST和图像-文本-音频基准上达到或超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成模型大多基于LLM，受限于文本配对数据和模态专用生成器的利用；而扩散/流模型的扩展仍依赖文本对齐嵌入、全配对数据或等维确定性映射。MUNI旨在解决这些问题，实现真正的任意模态生成。

Method: (1) 架构上扩展潜在扩散至端到端多模态任意生成，联合训练模态专用编码器、表达性解码器和单一共享流式先验；(2) 提出路由训练目标，解决多模态变分推理标准聚合规则与学习先验+表达性解码器耦合时的不足，确保潜变量的跨模态一致性、子集预测充分性和内容最小充分性。

Result: 在PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels和大规模图像-文本-音频基准上，MUNI在条件生成任务中匹配或超越最强基线，在无条件生成一致性上取得最大优势。

Conclusion: MUNI通过端到端联合训练和路由训练目标，有效解决了多模态任意生成中的潜变量一致性与充分性问题，为多模态生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce MUNI, an end-to-end multimodal latent diffusion framework for any-to-any generation that unifies subset-conditioned cross-modal generation and unconditional joint sampling through a shared stochastic latent. Existing multimodal generative models are largely LLM-based, which limits leveraging modality-specific generators and requires text-paired data for training. Recent diffusion- and flow-based any-to-any extensions take a different direction but still rely on text-aligned embeddings, fully-paired training, or matched-dimensionality deterministic mappings. MUNI rests on two complementary contributions, one architectural and one in the training objective. First, we extend latent diffusion to multimodal any-to-any generation end-to-end: instead of the standard two-stage recipe that precomputes a frozen latent space and then fits a prior over it, MUNI jointly trains modality-specific encoders, expressive decoders, and a single shared flow-based prior under one objective. Second, we identify that the standard aggregation rules of multimodal variational inference are insufficient once coupled with a learned prior and expressive decoders. A suitable shared latent must simultaneously satisfy coherence across generated modalities, predictive sufficiency of subset latents, and minimality of the latent content. We propose a routed training objective whose structural choices align the latent with these criteria and admit a minimal-sufficiency characterization in the realizable setting. Experiments on PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels and a large-scale image-text-audio benchmark show MUNI matching or exceeding the strongest baselines on conditional generation while opening its largest margins on unconditional coherence. Project page: https://muni-proj.github.io/.

</details>


### [151] [Not all Jensen-Shannon Divergence Estimators are Equal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16411)
*Alba Garrido,Alejandro Almodóvar,Mar Elizo,Patricia A. Apellániz,Santiago Zazo,Juan Parras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了JS散度在合成表格数据保真度评估中的测量问题：不同估计协议（边际估计器、分类器估计器）会产生不可比较的结果，并提出了针对分类器估计的先验偏移后验校正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JS散度被广泛用于合成表格数据的保真度评估，但其实际估计中使用的采样协议往往定义不明确，导致经验值依赖于估计器族、采样协议、校准方式、维度和类别平衡等因素，产生测量问题。

Method: 通过在受控环境（参考散度已知）和真实合成表格基准上进行系统实验，分析边际估计器和分类器估计器的行为差异；推导了分类器JS估计的先验偏移后验校正闭式解。

Result: 边际估计器存在依赖盲视（忽略联合分布中的依赖关系，严重低估散度），分类器估计器能捕捉联合结构但存在强估计器依赖性，且类别不平衡导致先验偏移偏差；在高维场景下估计器敏感性加剧。

Conclusion: 经验JS散度值本质上是协议依赖的，必须明确指定估计程序才能进行有意义的比较；提供了实践指南和开源评估工具。

Abstract: The Jensen-Shannon divergence is widely reported as a scalar measure of fidelity for synthetic tabular data. Yet, in practice, it is estimated from finite samples using protocols that are often underspecified. This creates a measurement problem. Although the population divergence is well defined, the empirical value depends on the estimator family, sampling protocol, calibration, dimensionality, and class balance. We show that different protocols can yield non-comparable values: marginal-based estimators ignore dependencies in the joint distribution and can severely underestimate divergence, while classifier-based estimators capture joint structure but exhibit strong estimator dependence. We systematically study this behavior across controlled settings with reference divergences and real-world synthetic tabular benchmarks. Our analysis reveals dependence blindness in marginal estimators, prior-shift bias under class imbalance, and estimator sensitivity in high dimensions. To address prior shift, we derive a closed-form posterior correction for classifier-based Jensen-Shannon estimation. Our results show that empirical Jensen-Shannon divergence values are inherently protocol-dependent, making explicit specification of the estimation procedure necessary for meaningful comparison. We provide practical guidelines and an open-source tool for estimator-aware Jensen-Shannon evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [Taylor-Calibrate: Principled Initialization for Hybrid Linear Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16429)
*Zhongzhu Zhou,Qingyang Wu,Junxiong Wang,Mayank Mishra,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Ben Athiwaratkun,Chenfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Taylor-Calibrate方法，利用泰勒引导的教师注意力统计信息来初始化混合Gated DeltaNet学生模型，解决了预训练Transformer转换为线性注意力模型时初始化脆弱的问题，显著减少了蒸馏所需的训练token数量（4.9倍至9.2倍），并在零样本性能上实现高达88倍的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练Transformer转换为混合线性注意力模型（如Gated DeltaNet）时，直接复制教师注意力投影无法指定新的循环衰减、写入和输出门控动态，导致转换后模型处于糟糕的动态区域，需要大量蒸馏token来修复初始化问题而非学习教师行为，这严重影响了转换效率。

Method: Taylor-Calibrate方法包含两步：(1) 使用泰勒引导的教师注意力统计信息来设置值投影、记忆时间尺度、写入门和输出门；(2) 应用短期的逐层对齐步骤，使每个转换层与教师输出匹配。

Result: 在四种教师设置和三种保留层策略下，Taylor-Calibrate生成了显著更强的零样本学生模型；在代表性消融实验中实现了高达88倍的改进；达到相同的恢复目标所需训练token减少了4.9倍至9.2倍。

Conclusion: Taylor-Calibrate是一种轻量级但高效的初始化方法，能够改善从Transformer到混合Gated DeltaNet的转换过程，大幅减少蒸馏成本。

Abstract: Hybrid linear attention models offer an appealing path to faster long-context inference: they reduce the quadratic cost and KV-cache burden of full softmax attention while retaining much of the quality of Transformer models. A practical way to obtain such models is to convert a pretrained Transformer instead of pretraining a new architecture from scratch, but this conversion is still brittle. Simply copying the teacher attention projections into a Gated DeltaNet (GDN) student does not specify the new recurrent decay, write, and output-gating dynamics. As a result, the converted model often starts in a poor dynamical regime and must spend many distillation tokens repairing initialization rather than learning the remaining teacher behavior. We propose Taylor-Calibrate, a lightweight initialization method for hybrid GDN students. The method uses Taylor-guided teacher attention statistics to set the value projection, memory timescale, write gates, and output gate, then applies a short per-layer alignment step to match each converted layer to the teacher output. Across four teacher settings and three retained-layer policies, Taylor-Calibrate gives substantially stronger zero-shot students, with up to an 88x improvement in a representative ablation, and reaches matched recovery targets with 4.9x--9.2x fewer training tokens than naive conversion.

</details>


### [153] [Autonomous End-to-End SOH Prediction Services for Battery Systems via Temporal-Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16434)
*Junting Wen,Dan Li,Qihao Quan,Xiwen Wang,Hang Yang,Zhaohong Meng,Zigui Jiang,Changlin Yang,Tianle Liu,Diego Muñoz-Carpintero,Jian Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TC-SOH，一种面向锂离子电池健康状态（SOH）估计的模块化、即插即用服务架构，采用时序对比机制与跨窗口预测预任务，直接从原始运维数据中端到端提取退化表征，并通过多项可解释性分析验证特征有效性，在四个公开数据集上MAPE和RMSE分别降低1.91倍和2.13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SOH估计依赖人工特征工程和黑箱模型，难以实现可扩展的工业部署，亟需一种自主、端到端且可解释的解决方案。

Method: 提出TC-SOH架构，采用时序对比机制（temporal-contrastive mechanism）和跨窗口预测预任务（cross-window prediction pretext task），直接从原始运维数据中提取退化相关表征，并结合可视化、敏感性分析、冗余分析、双向探针、未来SOH探针及时序打乱等表征诊断工具提升透明性。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，TC-SOH相比物理信息驱动和数据驱动基线，MAPE降低1.91倍，RMSE降低2.13倍。

Conclusion: TC-SOH作为一种模块化、即插即用的服务架构，能够在不依赖人工特征工程的前提下实现高性能、可解释的SOH估计，具备良好的工业部署潜力。

Abstract: Accurate state of health (SOH) estimation is a critical diagnostic service for lithium-ion battery management. However, reliance on labor-intensive manual feature engineering and opaque black-box models hinders scalable industrial deployment. To address this, we introduce TC-SOH: a modular, plug-and-play service architecture for autonomous, end-to-end SOH prediction. TC-SOH employs a temporal-contrastive mechanism and a cross-window prediction pretext task to extract degradation-relevant representations directly from raw operational data. To improve transparency, we connect model efficacy with representation diagnostics: visualization, sensitivity analysis, redundancy analysis, bidirectional probing, future-SOH probing, and temporal shuffling show that learned features overlap with selected expert descriptors while retaining additional SOH-relevant variation, and that ordered temporal context improves subsequent-SOH prediction. Across four public datasets, TC-SOH outperforms the considered physics-informed and data-driven baselines, reducing MAPE by 1.91 times and RMSE by 2.13 times.

</details>


### [154] [SDS-LoRA: Overcoming Anisotropic Gradient Scaling in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16454)
*Junghun Oh,Sungyong Baik,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度揭示了LoRA中低秩矩阵梯度因奇异值驱动而产生各向异性缩放的问题，导致梯度有效秩降低和与全微调的对齐变差；为此提出SDS-LoRA，通过结构解耦奇异值与反向传播，使梯度仅通过正交基传播，从而消除条件数对收敛的影响，在NLP和视觉任务上缩小了与全微调的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽能高效适配大模型，但其低秩参数化存在几何缺陷：全微调梯度反向传播到低秩矩阵时，受奇异值驱动产生各向异性缩放（偏向主导奇异方向而抑制其他方向），导致梯度有效秩降低、与全微调梯度的对齐变差，从而拉大与全微调的差距。本文旨在分析该问题并提出改进方案。

Method: 提出SDS-LoRA，一种结构解耦奇异值的低秩参数化方法。其核心思想是让全微调梯度仅通过低秩矩阵子空间的正交基反向传播，与矩阵尺度（奇异值）解耦，从而避免各向异性梯度缩放。

Result: 理论分析表明：LoRA的收敛率随低秩矩阵条件数增大而退化，而SDS-LoRA的收敛率与条件数无关。实验上，在自然语言和视觉基准任务中，SDS-LoRA改进了损失收敛，缩小了与全微调的差距，显著提升了适配性能。

Conclusion: LoRA中的各向异性梯度缩放是限制其性能的关键因素；SDS-LoRA通过解耦奇异值有效解决了该问题，实现了更优的收敛性和与全微调更接近的适配效果。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient adaptation of large pre-trained models to downstream tasks by parameterizing weight updates with low-rank matrices. In this paper, we investigate the limitations of the LoRA parameterization from a geometric perspective. Specifically, we show that when a full fine-tuning gradient is backpropagated to the low-rank matrices, it undergoes anisotropic scaling driven by their singular values. We argue that this phenomenon is undesirable because it distorts the full fine-tuning gradient by skewing it toward dominant singular directions while suppressing others. Our analyses demonstrate that anisotropic gradient scaling reduces the effective rank of the low-rank matrices' gradients and results in suboptimal alignment between the full fine-tuning gradient and its low-rank approximation in LoRA, thereby exacerbating the gap to full fine-tuning. To address these limitations, we propose a new low-rank parameterization, SDS-LoRA, which structurally decouples singular values from the backward pass. Our method ensures that the full fine-tuning gradient backpropagates only through the orthonormal bases of the low-rank matrices' subspaces, independent of their scales. Convergence analysis demonstrates that while LoRA's convergence rate degrades with the condition number of the low-rank matrices, SDS-LoRA remains independent of it. Experimental results across natural language and vision benchmarks show that SDS-LoRA improves loss convergence and reduces the gap to full fine-tuning, significantly enhancing adaptation performance.

</details>


### [155] [SPRI: SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization for Data-Constrained MoE Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16456)
*Weiqiao Shan,Ruixiang Mao,Yuang Li,Yuhao Zhang,Yingfeng Luo,Tong Zheng,Chen Xu,Yucheng Qiao,Chunxiang Jin,Yi Yuan,Jingdong Chen,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization (SPRI)方法，利用预训练前馈网络权重的SVD分解残差来初始化MoE专家，并配合两阶段训练策略，在数据受限的多语言语音到文本翻译任务中显著优于现有MoE upcycling方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE upcycling方法在数据受限的有监督适应场景下表现不佳，原因在于要么专家同质性过高，要么对预训练参数的扰动过大。需要一种既能利用预训练权重结构又能引入足够专家多样性的方法。

Method: 1) SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization (SPRI)：将预训练FFN权重的SVD分解残差分配到各个路由专家中，基于预训练谱结构引入受控的专家多样性。2) 两阶段训练策略：提升适应稳定性。

Result: 在CoVoST2的15个En-to-XX方向上，SPRI相比全微调稠密模型平均BLEU提升2.58、COMET提升3.32；相比最佳MoE upcycling基线，BLEU提升3.39、COMET提升4.34。

Conclusion: SPRI通过基于谱结构的残差初始化有效解决了数据受限下MoE upcycling的专家多样性与参数稳定性问题，在多语言语音翻译任务上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable efficient scaling, but training them from scratch remains prohibitively expensive. MoE upcycling mitigates this cost by converting pretrained dense models into sparse MoE models. However, existing upcycling methods typically rely on large-scale continued training and often perform poorly under data-constrained supervised adaptation, due to either homogeneous experts or overly disruptive perturbations to pretrained parameters. In this setting, effective upcycling must leverage pretrained weight structure while introducing sufficient diversity among routed experts. To this end, we propose SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization (SPRI), which distributes SVD-partitioned residuals derived from pretrained feed-forward network (FFN) weights across routed experts, introducing controlled expert diversity grounded in pretrained spectral structure. We further introduce a two-stage training strategy to improve adaptation stability. We evaluate SPRI on multilingual speech-to-text translation, where limited supervised data challenges MoE upcycling and multiple target languages provide natural routing heterogeneity. On CoVoST2 across 15 En-to-XX directions, SPRI improves average BLEU and COMET over fully fine-tuned dense models by 2.58 and 3.32 points, respectively, and outperforms the prior best MoE upcycling baseline by 3.39 BLEU and 4.34 COMET points.

</details>


### [156] [Privacy from Symmetry: Orthogonally Equivariant Transformers for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16461)
*Alexander Yukhimchuk,Andrey Shulga,Mladen Kolar,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过正交混淆（将嵌入乘以秘密正交矩阵）和ConjFormer（O(d)-等变Transformer架构）来保护LLM推理隐私，无需噪声注入或密码学，即可将令牌恢复率从35%降至1.3%，困惑度仅增加0.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地运行大语言模型不切实际，将敏感文本推理委托给第三方提供商存在隐私泄露风险；现有的分割推理虽将令牌保留在客户端，但隐藏表示仍可通过最近邻搜索从公开的嵌入表中恢复。

Method: 提出正交混淆和ConjFormer：(1) 客户端在发送前将嵌入乘以一个秘密正交矩阵；(2) ConjFormer通过标量RMSNorm归一化和对所有权重进行块状正交共轭，实现精确的O(d)-等变性，使服务器在旋转后的基中执行完整前向传播，无法观察到未旋转的隐藏状态。

Result: 在GPT-2和Llama 3.2 1B模型（PubMed微调）上，正交混淆消除了直接余弦最近邻反转，令牌恢复率从超过35% top-10降至最多1.3%，微调后困惑度仅增加0.4%。

Conclusion: 在架构层面强制执行对称性（O(d)-等变性）可以为隐私保护的LLM推理提供一种实用的防御手段，无需噪声注入或繁重的密码学机制。

Abstract: Running large language models locally is often impractical, pushing inference on sensitive text to third-party providers. Split inference partially mitigates this by keeping tokens on the client and sending only hidden representations, but these representations can still be recovered via nearest-neighbor search against the public embedding table. We propose an orthogonal obfuscation procedure in which the client multiplies embeddings by a secret orthogonal matrix before transmission. To enable correct inference under arbitrary rotations, we introduce ConjFormer, a transformer variant that is exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant via a lightweight normalization change (scalar RMSNorm) together with blockwise orthogonal conjugation of all linear weights. As a result, the server performs the full forward pass entirely in the rotated basis and never observes unrotated hidden states. Experiments on GPT-2 and Llama 3.2 1B models fine-tuned on PubMed show that orthogonal obfuscation eliminates direct cosine nearest-neighbor inversion and reduces token recovery from over 35% top-10 to at most 1.3%, while increasing perplexity by only 0.4% after fine-tuning. These results indicate that enforcing symmetry at the architectural level can provide a practical defense for privacy-preserving LLM inference without noise injection or heavy cryptographic machinery.

</details>


### [157] [Learning aligned EEG representations with subject-specific encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16462)
*Bruna J. Lopes,Gabriel Schwartz,Sylvain Chevallier,Raphael Y. de Camargo,Bruno Aristimunha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 跨被试EEG解码中，使用被试专用编码器+共享分类器的混合模型能有效学习被试对齐表征，甚至替代欧几里得对齐（EA）的作用；被试专用头增强了类别区分性并将每个被试映射到其潜在流形上，但未见被试的头部选择仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仅靠任务监督和架构设计能否学习到被试对齐的表征，以解决跨被试EEG解码中存在的强分布漂移问题。

Method: 将共享EEG编码器替换为被试专用编码器后接共享分类器（混合模型），在四个运动想象数据集上与EEGNet、AttentionBaseNet、CTNet基线（含欧几里得对齐EA）进行对比分析。

Result: EA能提升共享编码器性能，但混合编码器在很大程度上内化了EA的对齐功能（移除EA后验证损失曲线和潜在距离分析变化很小）；被试专用头增强了类别区分性并将每个被试映射到自己的潜在流形上，改善了多数被试性能，但存在一个对方法敏感的被试子集。

Conclusion: 被试专用编码器可作为EEG解码中的学习对齐机制，未见被试的头部选择是当前主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG decoding promises more training data, but it also exposes neural networks to strong inter-subject distribution shifts. We study whether task supervision and architecture alone can learn subject-aligned representations. We replace a shared EEG encoder with subject-specific encoders followed by a common classifier, and compare this hybrid model with standard EEGNet, AttentionBaseNet, and CTNet baselines with Euclidean Alignment (EA) on four motor-imagery datasets. EA improves shared encoders by recentering subject covariances, but the hybrid encoder largely internalises this role: validation-loss curves and latent-distance analyses change little when EA is removed. Subject-specific heads increase class distinctiveness and place each subject close to its own latent manifold, improving most subjects while leaving a method-sensitive subset. These results support subject-specific encoders as a learned alignment mechanism for EEG decoding and identify head selection for unseen subjects as the remaining bottleneck.

</details>


### [158] [BRICKS-WM: Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16489)
*Shaowei Zhang,Jiahan Cao,Xunlan Zhou,Shenghua Wan,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRICKS-WM提出了一种模块化组装结构化世界模型的方法，通过将潜在状态空间分解为Agent模块和Background模块，并学习潜在接口连接两者，实现了背景动力学的跨智能体复用，在保持控制性能的同时解决了传统MBRL中整体潜在动力学难以复用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型强化学习（MBRL）方法依赖整体潜在动力学，将环境动态耦合在一起，导致一旦改变智能体就需要从头重新训练整个世界模型，严重限制了可重用性。

Method: 基于物理世界由独立实体组成的洞察，将全局动力学建模为通过潜在接口交互的独立动力学模块的组合。将潜在状态空间分解为驱动的Agent模块和外部Background模块，两者通过学习得到的潜在接口桥接，实现功能分离——Background动力学对Agent动力学保持不可知。

Result: BRICKS-WM在从头训练时能达到与强整体基线相当的控制性能，并且能够实现冻结的背景动力学跨智能体复用。

Conclusion: 通过功能分离的模块化世界模型设计（而非传统的视觉分割方式），BRICKS-WM有效提升了世界模型的可重用性，为MBRL中结构化潜在动态建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Model-based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable success in continuous control by leveraging latent world models. However, prevailing approaches typically rely on monolithic latent dynamics, entangling environment dynamics into a coupled process. This coupling severely limits reusability: altering the agent necessitates retraining the entire world from scratch, even if the environment remains constant. To address this, we introduce BRICKS-WM (Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models), a framework for the modular assembly of structured world models. Driven by the insight that the physical world is composed of independent entities, we posit that global dynamics can be modeled as a composition of distinct dynamical modules interacting via latent interfaces. As a minimal instantiation, we factorize the latent state space into an actuated Agent module and an external Background module, bridged by a learned latent interface. Unlike prior object-centric methods that prioritize visual segmentation, BRICKS-WM enforces a functional separation in transition dynamics, ensuring that background dynamics remains agnostic to the agent's dynamics. Empirically, BRICKS-WM achieves control performance comparable to strong monolithic baselines when trained from scratch, and enables the reuse of frozen background dynamics across agents.

</details>


### [159] [daVinci-kernel: Co-Evolving Skill Selection, Summarization, and Utilization via RL for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16497)
*Dayuan Fu,Mohan Jiang,Tongyu Wang,Dian Yang,Jiarui Hu,Liming Liu,Jinlong Hou,Pengfei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出daVinci-kernel，一个基于强化学习的GPU内核优化框架，通过动态演化的技能库实现技能发现与利用的耦合。三个智能体（技能选择、策略生成、技能总结）共享一个LLM骨干网络，通过执行验证、SFT冷启动和多轮REINFORCE联合优化。在KernelBench基准上，daVinci-kernel-14B在Level 1/2/3分别达到37.2%、70.6%和32.2%的Fast₁阈值成功率，超越此前最强的RL训练模型Dr.Kernel-14B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化的目标是执行效率而非功能正确性，现有方法缺乏系统性的技能发现与利用机制，难以高效复用优化经验。

Method: 提出daVinci-kernel框架，使用一个LLM骨干网络联合训练三个智能体：技能选择（BM25+LLM重排序检索）、策略生成（生成多轮CUDA/Triton内核）、技能总结（将成功轨迹转化为可复用技能）。技能经执行验证确认可复现加速后才加入技能库。采用多样性过滤数据的SFT冷启动初始化，再通过多轮REINFORCE和每个智能体的优势估计进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 在KernelBench上，daVinci-kernel-14B在Level 1、Level 2、Level 3的Fast₁阈值下分别达到37.2%、70.6%和32.2%，全面超越Dr.Kernel-14B。

Conclusion: 动态技能库与多智能体联合强化学习能有效提升GPU内核优化性能，验证了技能发现与利用相结合范式的有效性。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization represents a paradigm where functional correctness is assumed and execution efficiency is the objective. We present daVinci-kernel, a reinforcement learning framework that couples skill discovery with skill exploitation through a dynamically evolving skill library. daVinci-kernel jointly trains three agents sharing one LLM backbone: a Skill Selection Agent that retrieves relevant techniques via BM25 and LLM reranking, a Policy Agent that generates multi-turn CUDA/Triton kernels conditioned on selected skills, and a Skill Summary Agent that distills successful rollouts into reusable skills. Candidate skills are added only after execution-based verification confirms reproducible speedups. All three agents share a single LLM backbone, are initialized via a structured SFT cold start on diversity-filtered data, and are then jointly optimized end-to-end with multi-turn REINFORCE and per-agent advantage estimation. On KernelBench, daVinci-kernel-14B achieves 37.2%, 70.6%, and 32.2% on Level 1, Level 2, and Level 3 under the Fast$_1$ threshold, outperforming the strongest prior RL-trained model, Dr.Kernel-14B.

</details>


### [160] [Tail-Shape Estimation in LLM Evaluation Is Fragile: A Protocol for Diagnosing False Positives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16511)
*Luca Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于验证LLM评估中尾部指数（tail-index）声明有效性的协议，并通过毒性评估实验证明，该协议能有效过滤掉三类假阳性，推翻了近期文献中关于尾部形状的关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究主张将LLM评估从基于均值转向尾部感知指标（如CVaR和奖励模型误差的尾部指数估计）。本文质疑：极值理论中的尾部指数参数（区分尾部厚度与尾部质量）是否能在均值和标准尾部幅度统计量之外提供额外的判别信息。

Method: 预注册协议，涵盖四项要求：可接受性（admissibility）、拟合优度（goodness-of-fit）、阈值稳定性（threshold-stability）和效应量（effect-size）。在标准LLM毒性评估设置下，使用两种结构不同的评分器族进行实证演示。

Result: 协议捕获了三种不同的假阳性模式（naive分析本会发表），并在两个评分器上都拒绝了核心的尾部形状声明。

Conclusion: 在研究的LLM毒性评估设置中，尾部形状估计比近期文献表明的更加脆弱；建议将该协议作为类似设置中尾部指数声明的起点。

Abstract: Recent work motivates moving large language model (LLM) evaluation from mean-based to tail-aware metrics, including conditional value-at-risk and tail-index estimates of reward-model error. We ask whether the canonical extreme-value-theory tail-index parameter, which isolates how heavy a tail is from how large the tail mass is, adds discriminative information beyond the mean and a standard tail-magnitude statistic in LLM evaluation. We pre-register a protocol covering admissibility, goodness-of-fit, threshold-stability, and effect-size requirements for any positive tail-shape claim. The protocol is the contribution of this paper; the empirical study below is a demonstration of what its gates catch. Applied to a standard LLM toxicity-evaluation setup under two structurally different scorer families, the protocol catches three distinct modes of false positives that a naive analysis would have published, and rejects the headline tail-shape claim on both scorers. We conclude that tail-shape estimation in the LLM toxicity-evaluation setups we examined is more fragile than the recent literature suggests, and recommend the protocol as a starting point for tail-index claims in similar setups.

</details>


### [161] [Direction-Conditioned Policies via Compositional Subgoal Scoring for Online Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16515)
*Swaminathan S K,Damiya Gondha,Theyanesh Eswaramoorthy Rajahkrishnan,Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方向条件策略(DCP)，将目标条件强化学习分解为子目标评分和方向条件动作两个组件，共享InfoNCE表示ψ。通过HJB理论证明方向充分性、训练与部署一致性，以及方向条件失败的可控子空间表征。在九个环境中相比Contrastive RL取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）将原始目标作为策略条件，当目标远离数据分布时，这一信号在几何上是非信息性的。HJB理论表明最优动作仅依赖于目标距离的梯度，而非原始目标本身，因此需要更高效的条件方式。

Method: 提出DCP方法，包含两个共享InfoNCE表示ψ的组件：(1) 子目标评分步骤，选择与最终目标g在ψ空间中对齐的已访问状态z_t；(2) 方向条件策略，以从ψ(s_t)到ψ(z_t)的单位方向d_t和幅度r_t为条件。训练时联合优化，部署时移除子目标评分，以g替代z_t。提供了理论证明包括方向充分性、训练-部署一致性边界和失败条件表征。

Result: 在九个环境中，DCP在大多数最终指标上优于Contrastive RL，在操控和障碍交互任务上提升最大。定性分析显示对比学习表示的ψ距离在线行为类似准度量，编码了环境拓扑结构。唯一失败案例AntSoccer中，学习到的梯度出现病理现象，与理论预期一致。

Conclusion: 方向条件是比原始目标条件更高效的目标条件强化学习范式，具有理论保障和实证优势。InfoNCE表示可作为在线准度量编码环境拓扑。方向条件在可控子空间缺失时失败的理论刻画为方法适用性提供了边界条件。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman theory implies that the optimal goal-conditioned action depends on the goal only through the gradient of the goal-reaching distance at the current state, yet standard online GCRL still conditions the actor on the raw goal -- a signal that is geometrically uninformative when the goal is far from the data distribution. We propose Direction-Conditioned Policies (DCP), a fully online method that decomposes goal-reaching into two components sharing one InfoNCE representation $ψ$: a subgoal-scoring step that selects a visited state $z_t$ aligned with the final goal $g$ in $ψ_g$, and a direction-conditioned actor that consumes the unit direction $d_t$ and magnitude $r_t$ from $ψ(s_t)$ to $ψ(z_t)$. The two components train jointly, factor cleanly at deployment (subgoal scoring is removed, while direction conditioning remains with $g$ in place of $z_t$), and admit independent modification at the same $(d_t,r_t)$ interface. We prove three results. First, direction sufficiency under HJB: the optimal action under control-affine dynamics depends on the goal only through the value gradient. Second, a quantitative bound showing that, under mild conditions on the learned representation and assuming the scoring rule returns an on-path $z_t$, the actor's conditioning input at training and at deployment coincide up to representation error and geodesic slack. Third, a controllable-subspace characterization of when directional conditioning fails. Across nine environments, DCP improves over Contrastive RL on most final metrics, with the largest gains on manipulation and obstacle-interaction tasks; a qualitative analysis of the learned $ψ$-distance landscape shows the contrastive representation behaves as an online quasimetric encoding environment topology, and the single failure case (AntSoccer) localizes to a learned-gradient pathology that the theory anticipates.

</details>


### [162] [How Post-Training Shapes Biological Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16517)
*Lukas Fesser,Hanlin Zhang,Michelle M. Li,Eric Wang,Bryan Perozzi,Shekoofeh Azizi,Sham M. Kakade,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在基因组学、转录组学和蛋白质领域训练100多个生物推理模型，系统研究了后训练各阶段（CPT、SFT、RL）对模型在领域内（ID）和领域外（OOD）性能的影响，发现各阶段以不同方式重塑泛化能力：CPT通过对齐生物语言提升下游性能，SFT提升ID但导致OOD性能先升后降，RL在强SFT检查点上可部分恢复OOD泛化；最优的ID-OOD权衡来自短SFT+大RL分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生物推理模型后训练各阶段（CPT、SFT、RL）如何影响模型的推理能力和泛化性能，以及何时会引发过专业化（over-specialization），从而指导更优的训练策略设计。

Method: 在基因组学、转录组学和蛋白质三个领域，系统变化骨干模型、CPT、SFT和RL等后训练阶段，训练并评估了100多个生物推理模型，同时测量领域内（ID）和领域外（OOD）性能。

Result: CPT通过对齐生物语言提升下游性能；SFT持续提升ID性能但OOD性能先升后降；RL应用于强SFT检查点时可改善OOD性能并部分恢复泛化；在固定后训练预算下，最优ID-OOD权衡来自短时SFT、更大的RL分配以及各阶段非对称的适应能力。

Conclusion: 生物推理能力并不会随着更多的监督或计算资源而单调提升，性能高度依赖于各训练阶段的组合方式。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning models for biology combine language models with foundation models trained on multimodal biological data, including DNA, RNA, and proteins. These models are built through post-training, yet how each stage shapes reasoning and generalization remains poorly understood. We study when post-training improves performance and when it induces over-specialization. Across genomics, transcriptomics, and proteins, we train and evaluate more than 100 biological reasoning models under controlled variation in backbone, continued pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL), measuring both in-domain (ID) and out-of-domain (OOD) performance. We find that each post-training stage reshapes generalization in a distinct way rather than contributing uniform gains. CPT improves downstream performance by aligning models with biological language. SFT consistently increases ID performance but causes OOD performance to peak early and decline as models fit the training distribution. RL, when applied to strong SFT checkpoints with aligned rewards, improves OOD performance and partially recovers generalization. These results show that biological reasoning does not improve monotonically with additional supervision or compute. Instead, performance depends on how training stages are composed. Under fixed post-training budgets, the strongest ID-OOD trade-off comes from brief SFT, larger RL allocations, and asymmetric adaptation capacity across stages.

</details>


### [163] [Assessing Reliability of Symbol Detection in Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16535)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）中概念检测的可靠性问题，发现高任务准确率并不保证概念被忠实检测；通过交换独立训练的概念检测器和分类头来评估可靠性，并提出了一种可靠性感知的训练策略，在概念监督减弱时显著缓解信息泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBMs通过人类可解释符号进行预测，但高任务准确率不能保证符号被忠实检测——联合训练的CBMs可能在瓶颈中编码任务特定捷径，导致解释不可靠。因此需要研究概念检测的可靠性问题。

Method: 1) 通过交换独立训练的概念检测器和分类头（共享同一符号词汇表）来评估性能退化；2) 使用概念级指标和符号级不确定性估计识别易受虚假激活影响的概念；3) 提出可靠性感知训练策略：共享概念检测器与多个分类头联合优化，并对依赖全局或实例级不可靠符号的行为施加惩罚。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011全概念监督下，检测器和头几乎可自由互换（交换掉点低于1个准确率点，相对保留率超过99%，无概念低于随机水平检测）。在受控合成任务中，当概念监督权重降低时，模型保持近乎完美的任务准确率，但交换准确率和与真实概念的一致性骤降至随机水平。可靠性感知训练可显著缓解此泄漏，在泄漏环境下将交换准确率大致翻倍。

Conclusion: CBMs中的概念检测可靠性会随概念监督减弱而急剧恶化，简单的任务准确率不足以衡量解释可靠性；所提出的可靠性感知训练策略能有效抑制信息泄漏，提升交换准确率。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are a relevant tool for explainable Artificial Intelligence because they make their predictions through human-interpretable symbols. However, high task accuracy does not guarantee that these symbols are detected faithfully: jointly trained CBMs may encode task-specific shortcuts in the bottleneck, making their explanations unreliable. In this paper, we study concept-detection reliability by swapping independently trained concept detectors and classification heads that share the same symbolic vocabulary. We use the resulting performance degradation, concept-level metrics, and symbol-wise uncertainty estimates to identify concepts that are especially prone to spurious firing. Finally, we propose a reliability-aware training strategy in which a shared concept detector is optimized with multiple classification heads and penalized for relying on globally or instance-wise unreliable symbols. On CUB-200-2011 with full concept supervision, detectors and heads are almost freely interchangeable (swap drop below one accuracy point, relative retention above $99\%$, and no concept detected below chance), whereas on a controlled synthetic task we show that, as the concept-supervision weight is reduced, models keep near-perfect task accuracy while swapped accuracy and agreement with the ground-truth concepts collapse to chance. Our reliability-aware training substantially mitigates this leakage, roughly doubling swap accuracy in the leaky regime.

</details>


### [164] [MIRAGE: Auditing Anti-Muslim Bias in Frontier LLMs Across Reasoning, Agentic, and Time-Coupled Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16562)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Tamim Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MIRAGE基准测试（1200个提示），在三种部署场景（直接补全、思维链推理、模拟智能体决策）下评估前沿LLM对穆斯林的偏见，发现思维链会放大暴力关联（12-34%），智能体决策存在显著不对称性（9-22%），且偏见与检索到的新闻情境高度耦合（18-27%）。现有缓解措施在不同条件下迁移效果差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去五年对LLM中反穆斯林偏见的评估大多停留在单轮提示补全，这种设置已无法反映前沿LLM的实际部署方式，需要更现实的评估框架。

Method: 构建MIRAGE基准，包含1200个提示，覆盖直接补全、思维链推理、模拟智能体决策（内容审核、贷款分类、难民申请摘要、招聘筛选）三种部署条件，在六个前沿模型上进行系统性评估。

Result: (i) 思维链推理相比直接补全将穆斯林-暴力关联放大12-34%；(ii) 智能体决策中穆斯林与非穆斯林案例存在9-22百分点的对称性差异；(iii) 偏见与近期冲突检索情境耦合，增加18-27%；现有缓解措施在直接补全上有效但无法降低智能体不对称性。

Conclusion: 现有偏见缓解方法在更现实的部署场景中泛化能力不足，需要针对智能体决策等复杂条件开发专门的缓解策略。MIRAGE开源以支持相关研究。

Abstract: Five years after the discovery of persistent anti-Muslim bias in large language models, most evaluations remain confined to single-turn prompt completion, a setting that no longer reflects how frontier LLMs are deployed. We introduce \textbf{MIRAGE} (Muslim-Identity Reasoning and Agentic Generation Evaluation), a benchmark of 1{,}200 prompts spanning three deployment-realistic conditions: direct completion, chain-of-thought reasoning, and simulated agentic decision-making across content moderation, lending triage, refugee claim summarization, and hiring screens. Across six frontier models, we find that (i) chain-of-thought reasoning \emph{amplifies} rather than suppresses Muslim-violence associations by 12--34\% relative to direct completion, (ii) agentic decisions exhibit a 9--22 percentage-point asymmetry between Muslim and matched non-Muslim cases on identical evidence, and (iii) bias is sharply time-coupled to retrieved news context, increasing 18--27\% under recent-conflict retrieval. Existing prompt-based mitigations transfer poorly across our three conditions, suppressing direct-completion bias while leaving agentic asymmetry largely intact. We release MIRAGE and an open evaluation harness to support targeted mitigation research.

</details>


### [165] [RepNet: Tackling spectral bias in deep neural networks via parameter reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16575)
*Yong Wang,Tao Zhou,Xuhui Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RepNet，通过重新参数化第一隐藏层的权重和偏置，有效控制初始斜率尺度和分割点分布，从而克服深度神经网络在拟合高频和多尺度问题时的谱偏差，数值实验表明该方法能以微小额外计算成本显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在科学计算中取得显著成功，但在捕捉振荡和多尺度行为时存在谱偏差问题，尤其是浅层ReLU网络难以拟合高频函数。

Method: 提出RepNet模型，对ReLU和tanh网络的第一隐藏层的权重和偏置进行重新参数化，以控制初始斜率尺度和初始分割点分布；将重新参数化的权重和偏置作为可训练参数，实现训练过程中的自适应频率缩放；并给出重新参数化DNN输出和斜率幅度的定量估计以指导初始化。

Result: 在多尺度一维和四维函数逼近、正向和反向PDE问题（结合PINN）以及算子学习等数值实验中，RepNet以略微增加的额外计算成本，显著提升了传统DNN在捕捉高度振荡特征时的预测精度。

Conclusion: RepNet提供了一种有效且灵活的方法来克服谱偏差，使DNN能够更好地应用于多尺度问题。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable success in scientific computing, yet they often suffer from spectral bias in capturing oscillatory and multiscale behaviors. In this study, we investigate this limitation by examining the failure of shallow ReLU neural networks in fitting high-frequency functions. This observation identifies two important factors in resolving rapid oscillations: the initial slope scale and the distribution of partition points induced by the networks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose RepNet, a reparameterized DNN model for ReLU and tanh networks designed for high-frequency and multiscale problems. The key idea is to reparameterize the weights and biases in the first hidden layer, which enables effective control of the initial slope scale and provides an appropriate distribution of the initial partition points. Furthermore, treating the reparameterized weights and biases as trainable parameters allows the DNN to achieve adaptive frequency scaling during training. In addition, we derive quantitative estimates for the output and slope magnitudes of the reparameterized DNN to guide the initialization of the proposed method. Numerical experiments, including multiscale one- and four-dimensional function approximation, forward and inverse PDE problems in combination with physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), and operator learning, demonstrate that RepNet improves the predicted accuracy of vanilla DNNs in capturing highly oscillatory features with slightly additional computational cost. These results indicate that RepNet provides an effective and flexible approach for overcoming spectral bias and applying DNNs to multiscale problems.

</details>


### [166] [On the Entropy Formula for Real, Complex, and Quaternionic Deep Linear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16579)
*Luis Contreras,Marco Nahas,Tejas Kotwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Menon和Yu的实深度线性网络熵公式扩展到了复数和四元数情形，得到了在实数域ℝ、复数域ℂ和四元数域ℍ上统一的DLN熵公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将实深度线性网络的熵公式推广到复数和四元数情形，寻求一个能够统一描述三类实数、复数和四元数深度线性网络熵的公式。

Method: 基于Menon和Yu对实DLN的熵公式，通过数学推导将其扩展到复数和四元数DLN，并整合为统一形式。

Result: 得到了一个适用于ℝ、ℂ和ℍ上深度线性网络的统一熵公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为不同数域下的深度线性网络熵提供了统一的理论框架，有助于理解DLN在不同代数结构下的行为。

Abstract: We extend the entropy formula of Menon and Yu for the real Deep Linear Network (DLN) to its complex and quaternionic analogues, obtaining a unified formula for DLNs over $\mathbb{R}$, $\mathbb{C}$, and $\mathbb{H}$.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-Modal Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Network with Mixture of Experts for Soil Organic Carbon Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16580)
*Daniele Mos,Felipe Drummond,Anton Bossenbroek,Soufiane el Khinifri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpTGNN，一个多模态时空图神经网络，用于土壤有机碳（SOC）预测。它通过异构图（空间邻近、光谱相似、高程三种边关系）表示土壤样本，利用微调的TerraMind编码器从多模态遥感数据提取特征，结合稀疏MoE融合与异质回归+深度集成的双重不确定性量化，在非洲测试集上达到R²=0.762，RMSE=3.51±0.48 g/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOC预测方法存在两大局限：一是使用手工特征+传统ML或单模态深度模型，无法充分利用遥感数据的丰富光谱和时间信息；二是基于网格的架构忽略了田间测量点的不规则空间结构。因此需要一种能够同时处理多模态遥感数据和不规则空间结构的统一框架。

Method: SpTGNN包含四个核心组件：1）异构图构建：将土壤测量点作为节点，定义三种边类型（空间邻近、光谱相似性、高程差异），使用关系图注意力机制分别学习每种关系的模式；2）多模态特征提取：微调的TerraMind编码器从Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1和DEM信号提取节点特征，结合环境协变量、位置嵌入和时间嵌入；3）稀疏MoE融合：通过top-k路由机制融合四个特征流；4）不确定性量化：结合异质回归（aleatoric不确定性）和深度集成（epistemic不确定性），并引入Moran's I惩罚项正则化空间自相关。

Result: 在三个区域实例（全球~49k、非洲~26k、欧洲~14k样本）上评估。5成员深度集成在非洲测试集上R²=0.762，RMSE=3.51±0.48 g/kg，MAPE=22.9%，优于表格XGBoost基线；最佳单检查点在验证集上R²=0.864。消融实验验证了异构图、MoE融合和微调骨干网络各自的重要贡献。集成UQ在校准后ECE达到0.031（混合）和0.026（β-NLL）。

Conclusion: SpTGNN是首个统一了基础模型特征提取、异构图注意力机制和分解式不确定性量化的SOC估计框架，在多模态融合和空间结构建模上显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Top-soil organic carbon (SOC) prediction is fundamental to agricultural sustainability, land use policy and fertilization planning. Existing approaches face two limitations: they pair hand-crafted covariates with classical ML or single-modal deep models that miss rich spectral and temporal information, and grid-based architectures ignore the irregular spatial structure of field measurements. We introduce SpTGNN, a multi-modal spatio-temporal graph neural network addressing both. SpTGNN represents soil measurements as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with three edge types (spatial proximity, spectral similarity, elevation), and applies relational graph attention to learn separate patterns per relation. A fine-tuned TerraMind encoder extracts node features from Sentinel-2, Sentinel-1 and DEM signals, combined with per-sample environmental covariates and learned positional and temporal embeddings. A sparse Mixture-of-Experts module fuses the four streams via top-$k$ routing. Uncertainty is captured by pairing heteroscedastic regression (aleatoric) with deep ensembles (epistemic), and a Moran's $I$ penalty regularizes spatial autocorrelation. We evaluate on a global SOC corpus split into three regional instances ($\sim$49k samples globally, Africa $\sim$26k, Europe $\sim$14k). Our 5-member deep ensemble reports $R^2=0.762$, RMSE $=3.51\pm0.48$ g/kg and MAPE $=22.9\%$ on the Africa test split, improving over a tabular XGBoost baseline; the best single checkpoint reaches validation $R^2=0.864$. Ablations confirm the heterogeneous graph, MoE fusion and fine-tuned backbone each contribute substantively, and the ensemble UQ stack achieves post-calibration ECE of $0.031$ (hybrid) and $0.026$ ($β$-NLL). To our knowledge, this is the first framework to unify foundation-model feature extraction, heterogeneous graph attention and decomposed uncertainty quantification for SOC estimation.

</details>


### [168] [Infant Spontaneous Movement Noise Improves Exploration in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16590)
*Francisco M. López,Markus R. Ernst,Francisco Cruz,Matej Hoffmann,and Jochen Triesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文受婴儿自发运动噪声的启发，提出一种在深度强化学习训练过程中逐步增加探索噪声时间自相关性的机制，实验表明该方法能产生结构化探索行为并提高学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习使用时间不相关的白噪声进行探索，而近期研究表明时间相关的有色噪声能提升探索效率。作者进一步探究婴儿自发运动中的噪声模式是否能启发更好的深度RL探索策略。

Method: 通过分析婴儿末端执行器速度的功率谱密度，发现其服从有色噪声过程且谱指数随年龄增长。据此设计一种在RL训练过程中逐步增加探索噪声时间自相关性的机制，匹配婴儿的统计特征。

Result: 在多个RL环境中，婴儿启发的噪声能产生结构化探索行为，相比传统探索策略提升了学习效率。

Conclusion: 人类运动和认知发展可为人工智能体的学习机制设计提供有价值的指导。

Abstract: Exploration in deep reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly implemented as temporally uncorrelated white noise. However, recent works show that temporally correlated colored noise can improve exploration efficiency by producing smooth trajectories with better coverage of the state space. We inquire whether action noise inspired by infant spontaneous movements can also improve exploration in deep RL. We find that the power spectral densities of babies' end-effector velocities follow a colored noise process where the spectral exponent increases with age. Inspired by this developmental pattern, we introduce a mechanism that progressively increases the temporal auto-correlation of exploration noise during RL training, matching the infant statistics. Experiments across several RL environments show that infant-inspired noise produces structured exploratory behavior and can improve learning efficiency compared to conventional exploration strategies. These findings suggest that human motor and cognitive development can provide useful guidance for designing learning mechanisms in artificial agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/trieschlab/baby-noise-rl.

</details>


### [169] [PhysGuard: Fisher-Guided Gradient Projection for Sim-to-Real Neural PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16602)
*Changjian Zhou,Junfeng Fang,Negin Yousefpour,Peng Wu,Bin Yan,Guillermo A Narsilio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhysGuard是一个面向神经算子的物理保持微调框架，通过Fisher信息矩阵识别物理关键参数方向，在域迁移下保护低频物理结构，最高降低32%的低频误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在仿真数据上训练后迁移到实验数据时存在sim-to-real gap，标准微调会破坏预训练中学到的核心物理表征。现有知识保持方法多针对视觉或语言任务，不适用于需要保护尺度物理结构的神经算子。

Method: 利用仿真数据的经验Fisher信息矩阵识别物理关键参数方向，在微调时限制更新不干扰这些方向；采用逐层Gram矩阵实现高效计算，自适应阈值确定保护子空间大小。

Result: 在四种神经算子架构和不同物理系统上，PhysGuard在大多数评估指标上优于基线方法。在严重域迁移下，相比标准微调可降低32%的低频误差，同时保持适应性。

Conclusion: PhysGuard有效解决了神经算子在sim-to-real迁移中的物理知识保护问题，尤其适用于域差异较大的场景。

Abstract: Neural operator models trained on simulation data often lose accuracy when applied to experimental measurements due to the sim-to-real gap. Standard fine-tuning with limited real data can reduce this gap, but it may also damage the core physics-relevant representations learned during pretraining. Although knowledge-preserving adaptation has been widely investigated in vision or language tasks, it remains unclear whether these methods are suitable for neural operators whose architectures and protected knowledge are fundamentally different. Neural operators need to preserve core-scale physical structures rather than semantic or visual features. We propose PhysGuard, a physics-preserving framework for accurate sim-to-real adaptation of neural operators. Specifically, PhysGuard uses the empirical Fisher Information Matrix computed on simulation data to identify physics-critical parameter directions, then restricts fine-tuning updates to directions that do not interfere with them. A layer-wise Gram-matrix formulation makes this efficient for models with millions of parameters, while an adaptive threshold automatically determines the protected subspace size. A spectral probe experiment shows that the dominant Fisher directions are strongly associated with low-frequency output structures. Experiments on benchmark across four neural operator architectures and different physical systems show that PhysGuard performs strongly on most evaluation metrics compared to baselines. The benefits are most evident under severe domain shift, where it reduces low-frequency error by up to 32\% compared to standard fine-tuning while maintaining adaptability. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZhouChaunge/PhysGuard.

</details>


### [170] [TCHG: Tri-Trust Conditioned Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Reliable Dynamic Trust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16611)
*Bohao Liao,Boyu Deng,Qipeng Song,Jieling Wang,Jingchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCHG框架，将信任证据分解为实体可靠性、交互行为可靠性和上下文信任三个通道，分别赋予不同的图传播控制功能，并引入非均匀衰减的独立时间状态和概率校准机制，在多个公开数据集上实现了有效且可靠的信任预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将信任信号统一表示，未解耦异质信任证据为独立通道，未能利用不同证据通道在信任建模中的不同角色。

Method: 提出TCHG（三信任条件异质图学习框架）：(1) 将信任证据分解为实体可靠性、交互行为可靠性和上下文信任三个通道；(2) 实体可靠性控制消息准入，交互行为可靠性调节传播强度，上下文信任通过条件算子选择调整传播模式；(3) 三个通道以非均匀衰减速率维护独立时间状态；(4) 预测信任概率并校准输出概率。

Result: 在多个公开信任数据集上，TCHG相比代表性信任预测方法和异质图基线方法实现了有效且可靠的信任预测。

Conclusion: 信任证据应被分解为功能各异的控制因子而非统一输入，三通道异质图学习框架能提升信任预测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Trust prediction infers latent user-user trust relations and provides important support for social recommendation, fake-review and manipulation detection, and risk identification. Graph neural networks have become a prominent approach to trust prediction because of their ability to learn network structures and complex trust dependencies. However, existing methods often rely on a unified representation of trust signals and do not disentangle heterogeneous trust evidence into separate evidence channels, failing to exploit the distinct roles that different evidence channels should play during trust modeling. To address this gap, this paper argues that trust evidence should not be treated as an undifferentiated input, but should be decomposed and used as functional control factors over graph propagation. We propose TCHG, a tri-trust conditioned heterogeneous graph learning framework that decomposes trust evidence into three channels and assigns them distinct functional roles in propagation: entity reliability governs message admission, interaction-behavior reliability modulates propagation strength, and contextual trust adjusts the propagation mode through context-conditioned operator selection. Since the three evidence channels evolve at different temporal scales, TCHG maintains independent temporal states with non-uniform decay rates to prevent rapidly changing contextual signals from overwriting slowly accumulated entity reliability. It further predicts trust probability and calibrates the output probability, improving predictive confidence under sparse or conflicting evidence. Extensive experiments on multiple public trust datasets show that TCHG achieves effective and reliable trust prediction compared with representative trust prediction and heterogeneous graph baselines.

</details>


### [171] [Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16620)
*Soham Bhattacharjee,Dushyant Singh Chauhan,Salem Lahlou,Martin Takac,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EGLR的训练无关解码方法，通过在不同层跨度L上递归应用冻结模型的高层解码层来生成多样化推理轨迹，与温度采样T构成L×T笛卡尔采样空间，在MATH-500上将oracle准确率从单一温度采样的83.4%提升至91.6%，为推理时扩展提供了不依赖随机噪声的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法仅依赖单轴（随机token级采样）获取多样性，作者认为这种单轴采样空间存在根本性局限，需要探索互补的确定性轴。

Method: 提出Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion (EGLR)：在不确定性高的token处，递归重新应用冻结模型顶部L层最多K_max次，直到下一个token分布收敛。与温度采样T结合后，构成L×T的笛卡尔采样空间。

Result: 在MATH-500上使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型，联合L×T oracle达到91.6%，比纯温度oracle（83.4%）高8.2个百分点，比纯层oracle（81.2%）高10.4个百分点。在8个指令微调模型和6个数学推理基准上验证了互补性。

Conclusion: L轴与温度轴捕捉到真正互补的问题分布，扩展的rollout池可为self-consistency、best-of-N、GRPO等下游流程提供更丰富的候选，开辟了不依赖随机噪声的推理时扩展新方向。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has become the dominant lever for improving language-model reasoning, but existing methods derive rollout diversity from a single source: stochastic token-level sampling. We argue that this single-axis sampling space is fundamentally limiting, and identify a second, fully deterministic and complementary axis: the layer span $L$ at which a frozen model's top decoder layers are recursively re-applied at high-uncertainty tokens. Different choices of $L$ produce distinct rollouts that solve different subsets of problems, with no stochasticity. We instantiate this axis through Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion (EGLR), a training-free decoding procedure that re-applies the top-$L$ layers for at most $K_{\max}$ iterations until the next-token distribution converges. Combined with $T$ temperature samples, EGLR turns a single-axis stochastic rollout pool into an $L\times T$ Cartesian sampling space at almost the same per-rollout cost. We characterize this space across $8$ instruction-tuned models and $6$ math reasoning benchmarks, and show that the $L$-axis is genuinely complementary to temperature: on MATH-500 with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, the joint $L\times T$ oracle reaches $91.6\%$, $+8.2$ percentage points beyond the temperature-only oracle ($83.4\%$) and $+10.4$ points beyond the layer-only oracle ($81.2\%$), confirming that the two axes capture genuinely complementary problems. The expanded rollout pool provides richer per-prompt candidates for any downstream procedure that consumes rollouts, including self-consistency, best-of-$N$ with verifiers, and group-relative RL training (GRPO), opening a new direction for inference-time scaling that does not rely on stochastic noise.

</details>


### [172] [SPICE: Synergy and Partial Information Based Curriculum Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16639)
*Ankush Pratap Singh,Houwei Cao,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPICE框架，利用部分信息分解（PID）理论将多模态交互分解为冗余、独特和协同信息成分，并据此设计渐进式课程学习策略，实现自适应样本排序，在多个多模态基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有课程学习策略通常假设样本相对复杂度在训练过程中不变，无法适应模型演化，缺乏对多模态交互动态特性的建模。

Method: 基于PID理论将多模态交互分解为冗余、独特和协同信息；设计渐进式课程，引导模型从共享跨模态线索学到模态特定模式再到复杂协同交互；利用PID信息估计实时更新样本排序以适配模型演化。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，SPICE优于传统训练方法和现有最优基线，验证了PID信息分解和自适应样本排序的有效性。

Conclusion: PID理论驱动的渐进式课程框架能够动态表征样本复杂度并自适应模型演化，为多模态课程学习提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal learning exploits complementary information across heterogeneous modalities. The informativeness of each modality can vary widely across samples and training stages. Existing multimodal curriculum learning strategies often assume that the relative complexity of samples remains unchanged throughout training and therefore cannot adapt to model evolution. We propose SPICE (Synergy and Partial Information based Curriculum Evolution), a novel progressive curriculum framework for multimodal interaction learning. Guided by Partial Information Decomposition (PID) theory, our approach decomposes multimodal interactions into redundant, unique, and synergistic information components, enabling an interpretable and dynamic characterization of sample complexity. Building on this decomposition, we design a progressive curriculum that evolves throughout training, allowing the model to transition from learning shared cross-modal cues to modality-specific patterns and, finally, to complex synergistic interactions. Adapting to model evolution, sample ordering is refined in real-time using PID information estimates derived from unimodal and multimodal predictions. Experiments across multiple multimodal benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over conventional training and state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of PID information decomposition and adaptive sample ordering for multimodal curriculum learning.

</details>


### [173] [Distribution Alignment for One-Shot Federated Learning via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16655)
*Daniele Berardini,Vito Paolo Pastore,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLOT-Align，一种无需额外训练的最优传输特征对齐框架，通过共享冻结编码器、Bures-Wasserstein重心和闭式测地线最优传输映射，在单轮联邦学习中同时处理域偏移和标签偏移，显著提升异构数据下的分类精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮联邦学习（OSFL）中，客户端与服务器仅通信一次，无法通过迭代优化纠正域偏移和标签偏移导致的特征表示错位，现有方法分别处理两种偏移且假设表示已对齐，缺乏统一高效的解决方案。

Method: SLOT-Align采用共享冻结编码器提取紧凑特征统计量，通过Bures-Wasserstein重心构建全局参考分布，并利用闭式测地线最优传输映射对齐局部客户端特征表示，整个过程无需训练且计算高效。

Result: 在多个基准数据集、不同预训练骨干网络和多种OSFL方法上，SLOT-Align在联合域偏移与标签偏移场景下持续提升了分类准确率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SLOT-Align是一种几何感知、即插即用的特征对齐方案，能有效解决OSFL中异构数据分布带来的表示错位问题，且易于集成到现有基于冻结编码器的OSFL管道中。

Abstract: One-Shot Federated Learning (OSFL) addresses extreme communication regimes in which clients interact with the server only once, amplifying the impact of heterogeneous client data distributions. In particular, the interaction of domain shift and label shift across clients induces misaligned feature representations that cannot be corrected through iterative optimization. Existing OSFL methods rely on distillation, server-side generation or ensemble-based aggregation, but assume aligned representations or address domain and label shift separately. We introduce SLOT-Align (Single-round, Learning-free Optimal Transport Alignment), a geometry-aware feature harmonization framework for OSFL. SLOT-Align uses a shared frozen encoder to extract compact feature statistics, constructs a global reference via Bures-Wasserstein barycenters, and aligns local representations using closed-form geodesic optimal transport maps. The method is computationally efficient and can be combined with existing OSFL pipelines relying on frozen encoders without modifying their training procedures. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, pretrained backbones, and OSFL methods show that SLOT-Align consistently improves accuracy and robustness under joint domain and label shift.

</details>


### [174] [Near-Optimal Stochastic Linear Bandits with Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16656)
*Ofir Schlisselberg,Mengxiao Zhang,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了延迟反馈下的随机线性赌博机问题，建立了近乎最优的遗憾界。对于损失无关延迟，延迟仅带来可加性惩罚（与期望延迟或最大未观察数相关，且维度无关）；对于损失相关延迟，线性赌博机的延迟惩罚依赖于维度的平方根，显著难于多臂赌博机；对于延迟即收益模型，多臂赌博机的最优保证在线性赌博机中无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对延迟反馈下多臂赌博机（MAB）的理解较为充分，但线性结构带来的新挑战尚未被清晰刻画。本文旨在系统识别延迟线性赌博机何时与MAB行为一致，何时因线性结构产生根本性新难题。

Method: 针对三种延迟模型分别设计算法并推导理论界：(1) 损失无关延迟下，采用基于乐观估计的线性上置信界算法，分析随机延迟与对抗延迟的惩罚项；(2) 损失相关延迟下，提出匹配（至多对数因子）的上下界证明；(3) 延迟即收益模型中，通过反例构造证明线性情况下最优MAB保证不可达。

Result: (1) 损失无关延迟：随机延迟惩罚与期望延迟成正比，对抗延迟惩罚与最大未完成观察数成正比，且两者均与维度无关；(2) 损失相关延迟：延迟惩罚与维度平方根成正比，与MAB形成本质差异；(3) 延迟即收益模型：线性赌博机无法实现仅依赖最优臂延迟的MAB最优保证。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了延迟反馈与线性泛化能力之间的交互机制，揭示了线性结构在损失相关延迟下带来根本性挑战，为延迟线性赌博机提供了近乎最优的理论保证。

Abstract: We study stochastic linear bandits with delayed feedback under several delay models and establish near-optimal regret guarantees. Our results identify when delayed linear bandits exhibit the same qualitative behavior as multi-armed bandits (MAB), and when the linear structure creates fundamentally new challenges. Specifically, (1) for \emph{loss-independent delays}, where the delay does not depend on the realized loss (but potentially depends on the arm), we show that delays incur only an additive regret penalty. Under stochastic delays, this penalty scales with the expected delay, while under adversarial delays, it scales with the maximum number of outstanding observations. Notably, both delay penalties are dimension-free, improving upon the state-of-the-art results; (2) for \emph{loss-dependent delays}, we show that linear bandits are substantially harder than MAB: unlike in MAB, we prove matching (up to log factors) upper and lower bounds in linear bandits, whose delay penalty depends on the square root of the dimension. (3) for the \emph{delay-as-payoff model}, a special case of loss-dependent delay, we show that the optimal MAB guarantee, which depends only on the delay of the optimal arm, is also unattainable in linear bandits. Together, these results provide a sharp characterization of how delayed feedback interacts with linear generalization.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Defensive Reporting: Machine Learning for Active Anti-Money Laundering Control in Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16663)
*Dara Goldar,Geir Kjetil Ferkingstad Sandve,Martin Jullum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究机器学习在保险索赔反洗钱中的应用。利用挪威一家大型保险公司的生产数据，训练梯度提升决策树模型，并通过引入欺诈调查标签作为辅助信号，显著提升洗钱检测效果。最佳模型在仅审查前2%-6%的高风险索赔时，即可捕获近三分之二的洗钱案件。同时提出了新的评估指标Budget-Weighted Capture Rate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险索赔洗钱行为对保险公司构成欺诈赔付、声誉及监管风险，但现有研究极少关注如何主动预防此类洗钱。本文旨在探索机器学习能否帮助保险公司在赔付前识别可疑索赔，将关注点从被动报告转向主动预防。

Method: 使用挪威大型保险公司的生产数据，训练梯度提升决策树模型。利用欺诈标签作为辅助训练信号，比较不同学习设置下的检测效果，并引入Budget-Weighted Capture Rate作为评估指标，衡量在仅能人工审查少量索赔时捕获洗钱案件的能力。

Result: 引入欺诈相关调查标签显著提升了洗钱检测效果。最佳模型在排名前2%-6%的索赔中，捕获了近三分之二的洗钱案件。

Conclusion: 这是首次关于机器学习在保险索赔洗钱检测中的实证研究，证明了利用欺诈标签作为辅助信号的有效性，为保险公司主动预防洗钱提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Money laundering through insurance claims poses a threat to insurers both through fraudulent payouts and reputational and regulatory risk. Despite this, little research has examined how such laundering can be prevented. This paper examines whether machine learning can help insurers flag suspicious claims before payout, shifting the focus from passive reporting to active prevention. Using production data from a major Norwegian insurer, we train gradient-boosted decision tree models to detect claims later reported to authorities for suspected money laundering. Because fraud and laundering may share behavioural patterns, we also examine whether insurance fraud labels can serve as an auxiliary training signal. We compare different learning setups using the Budget-Weighted Capture Rate, a metric introduced in this paper to measure how many laundering cases are captured when only a small share of claims can be manually reviewed. The results show that incorporating fraud-related investigation labels substantially improves laundering detection. The best-performing model captures nearly two-thirds of laundering cases within the top-ranked 2 to 6 percent of claims selected for investigation. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of machine learning for money laundering detection in insurance claims.

</details>


### [176] [Multimodal Evaluator Preference Collapse: Cross-Modal Contagion in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16682)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态AI评估中存在偏好坍缩(EPC)和跨模态传染现象：step_by_step策略吸收48.4%权重（是纯文本的3.2倍），跨模型评估产生双向传染，而自我评估几乎完全免疫（97%零传染）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注AI自我评估中的偏好坍缩现象，但缺乏对多模态场景下该问题的系统性分析，且未探索不同模态间偏好传染的可能性。

Method: 使用GPT-4o评估DeepSeek-chat的文本和视觉任务，通过四阶段隔离训练范式测量传染系数，对比四种评估配置（共53次独立重复、15,592次API调用）。

Result: step_by_step策略在多模态中权重占比48.4%（文本-only的3.2倍），三个视觉策略仅占9.1%；跨模型评估产生强双向传染（gamma_T->V=1.176, gamma_V->T=1.089），自我评估97%零传染。

Conclusion: 跨模型评估架构是偏好传染的主要风险因素，自我评估具有近乎完全的免疫力，研究发布MM-EPC框架和传染矩阵。
