Abstract: When AI agents use language models to evaluate their own outputs in a feedback loop, systematic biases emerge. We show that Evaluator Preference Collapse (EPC) is dramatically amplified in multimodal settings. Using GPT-4o to evaluate DeepSeek-chat across text and visual tasks, we find that a single strategy (step_by_step) absorbs 48.4% of all weight -- 3.2x the collapse observed in text-only self-evaluation -- while three visual-domain strategies receive only 9.1% combined weight. We then demonstrate a novel phenomenon we term cross-modal contagion: evaluator preferences acquired on one modality transfer to and corrupt strategy selection on another. Through a four-phase isolation training paradigm, we measure contagion coefficients and document strategy inversion -- the optimal strategy for a modality reverses after cross-modal exposure. A Phase 3 statistical validation across four evaluator configurations (N=53 total independent repetitions, 15,592 API calls) reveals a clear hierarchy: cross-model evaluation (GPT-4o, N=8) produces strong but symmetric bidirectional contagion (mean gamma_{T->V}=1.176, gamma_{V->T}=1.089, Delta=-0.088, p=0.575, Cohen's d=0.29); high round counts (DashScope, 50 rounds) cause collapse to single-strategy dominance (70% zero contagion); and self-evaluation provides near-complete immunity -- 97% of runs (N=30, DeepSeek-chat) yield exactly zero contagion (mean gamma=0.033, 95% CI [-0.031, 0.010], p=0.642, d=0.07). No evaluator condition shows statistically significant directional asymmetry. We introduce the contagion matrix indexed by evaluator identity, release the MM-EPC experimental framework, and identify cross-model evaluator architecture as the primary risk factor for preference contagion.

</details>


### [177] [Adaptive inference and function vectors in deep transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16694)
*Ravin Raj,Gautam Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度Transformer可作为平均场相互作用系统实现分布式推理，通过内部状态表征（函数向量）逐层推断潜在上下文变量，并预测了非高斯层次结构、上下文变量与Transformer深度之间的非平凡关系。在受约束线性注意力Transformer上的实验验证了理论预测，表明前馈模块和深度使Transformer能够实现比以往描述更丰富的上下文学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer内部机制尚不明确，亟需理论框架解释其如何学习复杂变量间关联以及实现上下文学习。

Method: 将深度Transformer建模为平均场相互作用系统，引入通信、局部性和深度约束下的分布式推理框架，利用函数向量表示作为内部状态进行分层推理。

Result: 理论预测了非高斯层次结构与Transformer深度之间的定量关系，并通过约束线性注意力Transformer实验验证了自适应推理能力。

Conclusion: 前馈模块和深度显著扩展了Transformer的上下文学习能力，使其能够实现更丰富的学习算法。

Abstract: Transformers are widely used as a general-purpose substrate for learning complex correlations between a large collection of coupled variables, but their internal mechanisms have remained mysterious. We introduce a theory of a deep transformer as a mean-field interacting system that implements distributed inference, subject to constraints on communication, locality and depth. We show that such a system can exploit internal state representations ('function vectors') to infer a latent context variable at increasingly finer scales over its layers. In an in-context regression task, the theory predicts a non-trivial relationship between non-Gaussian, hierarchical structure in the latent context variable, and transformer depth. Predictions are tested using constrained linear attention transformers and demonstrate adaptive inference in deep architectures. Feedforward blocks and depth enable transformers to implement a much richer class of in-context learning algorithms than previously described.

</details>


### [178] [MyPCBench: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16748)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Andrew Keunwoo Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MyPCBench是一个测试计算机使用智能体作为个人助理的基准，在部署了17个模拟真实Web应用和完整桌面栈的Linux桌面上，基于Michael Scott这一标准化角色定义了184个任务。最佳模型Claude Opus 4.6仅完全解决了55.4%的任务，模型失败集中在跨多应用和长轨迹任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体基准在非个人化环境中评估模型，与实际部署中个人助理需要处理用户上下文、历史数据和登录账户的场景存在差距，尤其在需要登录或个人信息的Web任务上缺口最大。

Method: 构建一个部署了17个模拟真实Web应用和完整桌面栈的Linux桌面环境，基于《办公室》中Michael Scott这一标准化角色，从OpenClaw社区收集真实请求并定义184个任务，使用统一的computer+bash工具表面评估六个闭源和开源模型。

Result: 最佳模型Claude Opus 4.6仅完全解决55.4%的任务，是唯一超过50%的模型。模型失败主要集中在跨多个应用的任务和长轨迹上，个性化对助理的挑战最大。

Conclusion: 现有模型在个人化计算机使用任务上表现有限，跨应用和长轨迹场景是主要瓶颈，MyPCBench为弥合评估与部署之间的差距提供了有效基准。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for computer-use agents evaluate models in impersonal environments. This leaves a gap between evaluation and deployment where personal assistants are expected to work across a user's whole digital life, including their context, historical data, and logged-in accounts. This gap is widest on web tasks, where live web evaluations cannot exercise sites that require logging in or personal information, the kind of site a real personal assistant has to drive. We introduce MyPCBench, which tests computer-use agents as personal assistants on a Linux desktop populated with 17 simulated real-world web applications and a full desktop stack, all seeded for one canonical persona, Michael Scott from The Office. We define 184 tasks in this environment, each inspired by a real request drawn from the OpenClaw community, and benchmark six closed and open-weight models with a uniform computer+bash tool surface. We find that the best model, Claude Opus 4.6, fully solves 55.4\% of the tasks, the only model above 50\%. Model failures cluster on tasks that span many applications and on long trajectories, where personalization stresses an assistant the most. We release the environment, task set, and agent harness at https://mypcbench.com.

</details>


### [179] [Maximum Entropy Inverse Reinforcement Learning for Mean-Field Games with Average Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16759)
*Şevket Kaan Alkır,Naci Saldı,Berkay Anahtarcı,Can Deha Karıksız*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平均奖励准则下离散时间、无限时域平均场博弈（MFG）的逆强化学习问题，通过最大因果熵原理从专家演示中恢复解释性策略，分别针对有限维线性奖励和无限维RKHS奖励提出了凸对偶重构和拉格朗日松弛方法，并给出了收敛保证和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家演示来源于未知奖励下的平稳平均场均衡，目标是在缺乏折扣因子收缩的条件下，恢复能够解释观察到的集体行为的策略，从而弥合逆强化学习与平均场博弈之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 采用最大因果熵原理，将逆问题通过平均场项和长程特征期望的一致性约束进行建模。对于有限维线性奖励，引入凸对偶重构和显式对数配分目标，证明光滑性和曲率性质以支持常步长梯度下降；对于无限维RKHS奖励，提出拉格朗日松弛，利用基于极小化的次随机核构造软贝尔曼算子的严格压缩，并建立Fröchet可微性和Lipschitz光滑性，进而设计梯度上升算法。

Result: 理论层面证明了线性奖励情形下凸对偶目标的光滑性与曲率，以及RKHS奖励情形下对数似然得分的Fröchet可微性和Lipschitz光滑性；数值实验在恶意软件传播MFG和基于RKHS的消费者选择模型上显示，恢复的策略与专家行为高度吻合。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了无折扣因子收缩条件下平均场博弈逆强化学习的核心困难，为从聚合观测数据中学习群体决策机制提供了理论框架和可执行算法。

Abstract: We study inverse reinforcement learning for discrete-time, infinite-horizon mean-field games (MFGs) under an average-reward criterion. Expert demonstrations are assumed to arise from a stationary mean-field equilibrium under an unknown reward, and the goal is to recover a policy explaining the observed behaviour via the maximum causal entropy principle. We formulate the inverse problem by enforcing consistency with the expert mean-field term and long-run feature expectations, treating two reward classes within a unified occupation-measure framework. For finite-dimensional linear rewards, we give a convex dual reformulation with an explicit log-partition objective, and prove smoothness and curvature properties justifying constant-step-size gradient descent. For infinite-dimensional RKHS rewards, we develop a Lagrangian relaxation whose inner-maximising policy is characterised by a soft Bellman equation. The main obstacle is the absence of a discount-factor contraction. We resolve this by introducing a minorisation-based sub-stochastic kernel that yields a strict contraction of the soft Bellman operator. We establish Fréchet differentiability and Lipschitz smoothness of the log-likelihood score, leading to a gradient ascent algorithm with convergence guarantees. Two numerical examples, a malware-spread MFG and an RKHS-based consumer-choice model, show that the recovered policies closely match expert behaviour.

</details>


### [180] [A Validated LBM Dataset and Pipeline for Surrogate Modeling of Turbulent 3D Obstructed Channel Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16765)
*Lukas Schröder,Shubham Kavane,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的管道，用于生成3D湍流通道流动的训练数据，并验证了基于格子玻尔兹曼方法的求解器，计划系统评估傅里叶神经算子与U-Net在预测、超分辨率和误差校正任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估神经算子用于3D湍流需要经过验证的数据集和物理基准，目前缺乏标准化的比较框架。

Method: 使用具有累积碰撞算子的格子玻尔兹曼求解器，在Re=1,000-10,000范围内生成3D通道绕流数据，通过斯特劳哈尔数、阻力系数、湍流波动等实验测量进行严格验证，并进行1024x512x512分辨率下的网格收敛性研究。

Result: 求解器通过了实验验证和网格收敛性测试，建立了标准化的代理模型比较管道。

Conclusion: 该验证管道可实现神经算子与数值求解器在湍流问题上的公平比较，未来将关注计算效率对比和实际应用探索。

Abstract: Evaluating neural operators for 3D turbulent flow requires validated datasets with physical benchmarks. We present a reproducible pipeline generating training data for 3D channel flows around generated geometries at Re=1,000-10,000. Our lattice Boltzmann solver with cumulant collision operators is rigorously verified against experimental measurements (Strouhal number, drag coefficients, turbulent fluctuations) with comprehensive grid convergence studies at resolution 1024x512x512. Building upon an established framework, this validated pipeline enables standardized surrogate model comparison. We outline planned systematic evaluation of Fourier Neural Operator and U-Net variants on forecasting, super-resolution, and error correction tasks, using physics-informed metrics to assess turbulent energy cascade representation. Future work will compare computational efficiency between numerical solvers and neural surrogates, exploring practical application. We seek community feedback on our validation approach, planned benchmark methodology, and evaluation priorities for neural operators in turbulent flows.

</details>


### [181] [Taming Curvature: Architecture Warm-Up for Stable Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16768)
*Sameera Ramasinghe,Ajanthan Thalaiyasingam,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Yan Zuo,Karol Pajak,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速在线估计预条件Hessian最大特征值（曲率）的方法，并基于曲率观测发现训练不稳定性与曲率激增相关，进而提出架构热身（architecture warm-up）策略——逐步增加网络深度以控制曲率，从而稳定大型Transformer训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练十亿参数规模的Transformer常出现损失尖峰和发散，浪费大量计算资源。虽然Edge of Stability理论可通过（预条件）曲率理解和控制优化稳定性，但这些曲率控制方法因曲率估计复杂而在大规模Transformer训练中不流行。作者旨在解决这一矛盾，提供高效曲率估计工具并据此设计稳定训练方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于热启动的幂迭代变体快速在线估计预条件Hessian最大特征值（曲率），通过Hessian-向量积实现。2) 利用该工具观测到训练不稳定性与预条件曲率激增相关，且曲率随深度增加。3) 提出架构热身（architecture warm-up）策略：逐步增加网络深度以精细控制预条件Hessian，稳定训练。

Result: 理论证明和实验验证了所提曲率估计方法在十亿参数规模下每迭代跟踪曲率的可行性，且比现有方法更准确。在大型Transformer上的实验表明，该方法能实现高效曲率跟踪，并在不减缓收敛速度的情况下，相比现有最先进的稳定技术减少了训练不稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文的工作表明，通过高效的曲率估计工具（热启动幂迭代）可以实时监控训练稳定性，而架构热身（逐步加深网络）是一种有效的曲率控制策略，能够在不牺牲收敛速度的前提下显著提升大型Transformer训练的稳定性。

Abstract: Training billion-parameter Transformers is often brittle, with transient loss spikes and divergence that waste compute. Even though the recently developed Edge of Stability (EoS) theory provides a powerful tool to understand and control the stability of optimization methods via the (preconditioned) curvature, these curvature-controlling methods are not popular in large-scale Transformer training due to the complexity of curvature estimation. To this end, we first introduce a fast online estimator of the largest (preconditioned) Hessian eigenvalue (i.e., curvature) based on a warm-started variant for power iteration with Hessian-vector products. We show theoretically, and verify empirically, that the proposed method makes per-iteration curvature tracking feasible at billion parameter scale while being more accurate. Using this tool, we find that training instabilities coincide with surges in preconditioned curvature and that curvature grows with depth. Motivated by these observations, we propose architecture warm-up: progressively growing network depth to carefully control the preconditioned Hessian and stabilize training. Experiments on large Transformers validate that our approach enables efficient curvature tracking and reduces instabilities compared to existing state-of-the-art stabilization techniques without slowing down convergence.

</details>


### [182] [GD$^2$PO: Mitigating Multi-Reward Conflicts via Group-Dynamic reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16771)
*Haotian Liu,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Siyuan Huang,Xinpeng Liu,Pengyu Cheng,Jiajun Song,Ruijin Ding,Junfeng Li,Zhechao Yu,Mengyu Zhou,Hongteng Xu,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GD²PO通过冲突感知过滤机制和查询级重加权，解决多奖励强化学习中的奖励冲突问题，显著提升学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如GDPO）在多奖励场景下仍面临奖励冲突：单个rollout在不同奖励维度上可能同时产生正负优势，导致信号相互抵消，阻碍训练效率。

Method: 提出GD²PO，包含两个核心组件：(1) 冲突感知过滤机制，掩码掉存在严重奖励维度不一致的rollouts，避免冲突信号抵消；(2) 查询级重加权，根据查询的整体奖励共识动态调整更新强度。

Result: 在工具调用和人类偏好对齐等多奖励场景中，GD²PO一致且显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: GD²PO通过有效应对多奖励冲突，加速了多维度奖励强化学习的训练效率，为LLM后训练提供了更优方案。

Abstract: As LLMs advance, post-training reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly relies on multi-dimensional rewards to cultivate comprehensive capabilities. This shift demands new algorithms capable of optimizing diverse and potentially competing objectives simultaneously. To address this, existing methods such as Group reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GDPO) decompose the overall score into independent reward groups, then compute the RL loss separately within each group. However, this strategy still encounters multi-reward conflicts: a single rollout can yield positive advantages on certain reward dimensions but negative ones on others, causing opposing signals to cancel each other out during aggregation, further hindering RL training efficiency. Inspired by Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO), which improves RL training efficiency by filtering out ineffective rollouts with near-zero advantages, we propose Group-Dynamic reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GD$^2$PO). Specifically, GD$^2$PO employs a conflict-aware filtering mechanism to mask out rollouts suffering from severe reward-wise disagreement. By preventing conflicting signals from canceling each other out, this masking strategy preserves and enhances the magnitude of effective RL advantages, thereby significantly accelerating learning efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce query-level reweighting to dynamically adjust the update intensity of each query based on its overall reward consensus. Experiments on various multi-reward scenarios, including tool calling and human preference alignment, demonstrate that GD$^2$PO consistently and significantly outperforms existing baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/GD2PO.

</details>


### [183] [We Need Explanation Cards to Connect Explanation Algorithms to the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16786)
*Eric Günther,Balázs Szabados,Kristof Meding,Gunnar König,Sebastian Bordt,Ulrike von Luxburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"解释卡"（Explanation Cards）框架，通过为算法解释补充稳健性、有效性信息及明确解释指南，弥合解释表面含义与实际信息之间的鸿沟，并将解释责任从用户转移到提供者，实验证明该方法能提升解释的实用性与可靠性，且符合欧盟AI法案的可解释性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法解释存在两大问题：(1) 解释的实际含义与直觉不符，需要专业知识才能正确解读；(2) 流行解释算法对复杂决策函数的行为缺乏信息量。这导致解释的表面信息与实际提供的信息之间存在鸿沟，用户难以正确理解和使用解释。

Method: 提出"解释卡"（Explanation Cards）框架，为标准解释补充三类信息：(1) 稳健性信息（robustness）；(2) 有效性信息（validity）；(3) 明确的解释指南（interpretation instructions）。以反事实解释和SHAP为例，演示如何构建解释卡。

Result: 解释卡能够使原本无信息量的解释变得实际有用，同时帮助检测解释不适用的情况。解释卡中的指南将解释责任从用户转移到提供者，用户获得了解释所需的指导，能够进行正确的解读。

Conclusion: 解释卡为算法解释在实际场景中的应用提供了可行方案，可操作化实现欧盟AI法案中的可解释性条款，是推动解释算法走向真实世界应用的重要一步。

Abstract: Algorithmic explanations are intended to help stakeholders understand opaque algorithmic decisions, but in practice, they often fall short. First, the meaning of algorithmic explanations is often not what one might intuitively expect, so expert knowledge is required to interpret them correctly. Second, recent work has shown that popular explanation algorithms are uninformative about the behavior of complex decision functions. Together, these issues create a gap between what explanations appear to convey and what they actually provide. In this work, we propose Explanation Cards for Explanation Algorithms, which augment standard explanations with complementary information about robustness and validity, as well as clear instructions for interpretation. The complementary information can render otherwise uninformative explanations practically useful, while also helping to detect cases where they are not. Importantly, the interpretation instructions in explanation cards shift responsibility from users to providers: Rather than expecting users to recognize what can and cannot be concluded from an explanation, providers must make this explicit upfront. Using counterfactual explanations and SHAP as examples, we demonstrate how providers can construct explanation cards and that these cards provide users with the guidance needed for sound interpretation. We further argue that explanation cards offer a practical means of operationalising the explainability provisions of the EU AI Act. Overall, explanation cards are a significant step toward making explanation algorithms fit for real-world use cases.

</details>


### [184] [Decision-Weighted Flow Matching for Contextual Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16790)
*Jize Xie,Haomiao Wu,Qiang Chen,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策加权流匹配（DW-FM）框架，通过重加权速度回归目标来对齐生成模型与下游决策遗憾，在三个CVaR随机优化基准上优于标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型用于随机优化的场景生成时，标准训练目标强调均匀分布拟合，忽视了生成场景对下游决策的影响，导致目标错配：统计常见区域的误差对决策遗憾影响小，而决策敏感区域的误差会显著改变最优动作。

Method: 提出决策加权流匹配（DW-FM），保持标准流匹配的简洁性，使用决策敏感的端点信息对速度回归目标进行重加权。理论上通过损失诱导的决策差异和伴随传输论证连接下游遗憾与路径速度错配。

Result: 在合成投资组合、半真实金融和交通CVaR三项上下文随机优化基准上，DW-FM相比标准基线显著改善了下游决策遗憾。

Conclusion: 决策加权流匹配通过将下游决策遗憾融入训练目标，有效解决了生成模型在随机优化中的目标错配问题，是一种遗憾对齐的训练框架。

Abstract: Conditional generative models are increasingly used as scenario generators for stochastic optimization, but standard training objectives emphasize uniform distributional fit rather than the downstream decisions induced by generated scenarios. This creates an objective mismatch: errors in statistically common regions may have little effect on decision regret, whereas errors in decision-sensitive regions can substantially change the optimal action. We propose Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM), a regret-aligned training framework that preserves the simplicity of standard flow matching while reweighting its velocity-regression objective using decision-sensitive endpoint information. Theoretically, we connect downstream regret to pathwise velocity mismatch through a loss-induced decision discrepancy and an adjoint transport argument, yielding an ideal regret-aligned surrogate and practical endpoint-weighted objectives with regret guarantees. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of DW-FM on three CVaR-based contextual stochastic optimization benchmarks spanning synthetic portfolio, semi-real financial, and traffic-CVaR tasks, where DW-FM improves downstream regret over standard baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Deep Q-Learning on Hölder Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16846)
*Qian Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从算子理论角度研究连续时间连续状态-动作随机控制中的Q学习核心。在均匀椭圆性和Hölder正则系数下，证明了Bellman更新将有界输入映射到各向异性正则类，状态变量光滑而动作变量仅Lipschitz依赖，由此导出紧的Bellman迭代族，并提出适配混合正则性的张量积DeepONet架构，同时给出了显式逼近与资源界以及时间步长δ→0时的刚度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q学习理论在离散环境中有较好发展，但连续时间连续状态-动作的随机控制场景中，Bellman最优性目标的正则性和逼近复杂性缺乏严格分析，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用算子理论分析Bellman更新映射的正则性提升性质；利用均匀椭圆性和Hölder正则系数建立各向异性正则类；设计张量积DeepONet架构以匹配状态与动作变量的混合正则性。

Result: 证明了Bellman更新将有界输入映射到状态变量光滑、动作变量仅Lipschitz依赖的各向异性正则类；得到Bellman迭代的紧性；给出了显式逼近误差与资源复杂度界；揭示了时间步长趋零时刚度与复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文为连续随机控制中的Q学习理论提供了Bellman目标正则性与逼近的严格基础，但未给出实际采样Q学习（含探索、经验回放和随机梯度更新）的完整收敛定理。

Abstract: We study the operator-theoretic core of Q-learning in continuous-time stochastic control with continuous states and actions. In value-based reinforcement learning, each Q-learning or DQN update is built from a Bellman optimality target; our analysis isolates this target in a diffusion setting and studies its regularity and approximation complexity. Under uniform ellipticity and Hölder-regular coefficients, we show that a Bellman update maps bounded inputs into an anisotropic regularity class, smoothing the state variable while leaving only Lipschitz dependence on the action variable. This yields a compact family of Bellman iterates and motivates a tensor-product DeepONet architecture adapted to the mixed regularity of the problem. We then derive explicit approximation and resource bounds, together with a stiffness--complexity trade-off as the time step $δ\to 0$. The resulting theory makes a direct contribution to Q-learning theory at the level of Bellman target regularity and approximation in continuous stochastic control. At the same time, we do not claim a full convergence theorem for practical sampled Q-learning with exploration, replay, and stochastic gradient updates.

</details>


### [186] [HawkesNest: A Multi-Axis Synthetic Benchmark for Spatiotemporal Pattern Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16863)
*Yahya Aalaila,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HawkesNest是一个基于多元Hawkes过程的可控时空点过程基准测试框架，通过四个复杂度轴（时空纠缠、背景异质性、跨类型交互、域拓扑）定义确定性指标，用于诊断性压力测试STPP模型，发现Hawkes基线模型在联合异质性-纠缠复杂度下性能退化，而AutoSTPP等神经模型对独立的时空纠缠增加仍然敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STPP模型评估依赖不透明的真实数据集，其潜在生成结构未知，模型失败难以归因，缺乏可控的基准测试环境。

Method: 基于多元Hawkes过程构建生成器对齐的基准，定义四个复杂度轴并关联确定性指标，在固定全局速率、稳定性和模拟预算的条件下变化各轴以进行诊断性压力测试。

Result: 验证了指标在受控扫描下具有单调性和近似正交性；Hawkes基线模型在联合异质性-纠缠复杂度下性能下降；AutoSTPP在独立的时空纠缠增加下仍然脆弱。

Conclusion: HawkesNest提供了一个可控、可归因的STPP模型评估框架，能够揭示不同模型在已知结构难度下的失效模式，有助于模型诊断和改进。

Abstract: Evaluation of spatiotemporal point process (STPP) models relies heavily on opaque real-world datasets, where latent generative structure is unknown and model failures are difficult to attribute. We introduce HawkesNest, a generator-aligned benchmark for controlled spatiotemporal pattern complexity built on a multivariate Hawkes backbone. HawkesNest defines four complexity axes: space--time entanglement, background heterogeneity, cross-type interaction, and domain topology. Each axis is associated with a deterministic index computed from the latent data-generating mechanism. By varying these axes while holding global rate, stability, and simulation budget fixed, HawkesNest enables diagnostic stress tests of STPP models under known structural difficulty. We verify that the indices are monotone and nearly orthogonal under controlled sweeps. We illustrate its use by showing that Hawkes-family baselines degrade under joint heterogeneity--entanglement complexity, even though they are structurally aligned with the Hawkes data-generating backbone. We further show that HawkesNest exposes neural-model sensitivity: AutoSTPP remains vulnerable under isolated increases in space--time entanglement. Code. Available at https://github.com/YahyaAalaila/HawkesNest

</details>


### [187] [Integrated Marketing Attribution: A Bayesian Framework for Privacy-Safe Granular Measurement Anchored in MMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16878)
*Meghana R. Bhat,Ankit Umare,Utsav Aggarwal,Richard Vecsler,Arunkumar Mani,Karthik Nair,Chandhu Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集成营销归因（IMA）框架，结合营销组合建模（MMM）与贝叶斯归因模型，从聚合数据中推导出细粒度的、隐私安全的广告活动级效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售营销测量中，MMM和MTA两种主流方法存在碎片化问题：MMM隐私安全但粒度太粗，MTA细粒度但在隐私限制下可靠性下降。亟需一种既能提供细粒度洞察又保持隐私安全且与MMM一致性的统一框架。

Method: 提出集成营销归因（IMA）框架，将MMM与渠道特定的贝叶斯归因模型相结合，利用MMM信息先验，从聚合数据中推导出广告活动级效果。

Result: IMA能够提供细粒度、隐私安全的归因，同时保持与MMM的一致性，克服了传统方法在粒度与隐私安全性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: IMA统一了MMM和MTA的优势，在不依赖用户级追踪的前提下实现了细粒度广告活动级洞察，为隐私受限环境下的营销测量提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Retail marketing measurement increasingly requires granular campaign-level insights without relying on user-level tracking. However, the two dominant approaches, Marketing Mix Modeling (MMM) and Multi-Touch Attribution (MTA), often produce fragmented insights. MMM is privacy-safe and robust for channel-level planning but is too coarse for campaign optimization, while MTA provides granular attribution but has become less reliable under increasing privacy restrictions. We propose Integrated Marketing Attribution (IMA), a unified framework that combines MMM with channel specific Bayesian attribution models to derive campaign-level effects from aggregated data. By leveraging MMM-informed priors, IMA delivers granular, privacy-safe attribution while preserving consistency with MMM.

</details>


### [188] [Upper Bounds on the Generalization Error of Deep Learning Models via Local Robustness and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16883)
*Abdul-Rauf Nuhu,Parham M. Kebria,Vahid Hemmati,Mahmoud N. Mahmoud,Edward Tunstel,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的泛化界，通过根据输入空间子区域内的稳定与不稳定样本数量来缩放鲁棒性项，从而解决了现有鲁棒性泛化界在实践中的空洞性问题。在ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法能持续产生非空洞的紧致上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于鲁棒性的泛化界在实际设置中往往产生空洞上界（远高于真实错误率），传统方法将鲁棒性项作为全局度量，忽略了其在输入空间不同子区域间的变化，这是导致界过于松弛的重要原因。

Method: 提出一种新的泛化界，通过根据每个子区域中稳定和不稳定样本的数量来缩放鲁棒性项，同时整合数据和模型依赖因素，使得上界更紧致且具有实际相关性。

Result: 在ImageNet数据集上训练的多种鲁棒深度神经网络中，所提出的边界持续保持非空洞（non-vacuous），并且在现有方法中达到了最紧致的估计，与经验性能高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过考虑鲁棒性在输入空间子区域中的局部变化，可以显著提升泛化界的紧致性和实用性，为安全关键应用中的深度学习模型评估提供了更有意义的理论工具。

Abstract: Generalization is a critical property of data-driven models, particularly deep learning models deployed in safety-critical applications. Robustness-based generalization bounds have gained attention as a principled way to link robustness properties to generalization performance, often in a data-dependent manner. However, most existing bounds suffer from vacuousness in practical settings, yielding loose upper bounds that greatly exceed the actual error rates and limiting their usefulness for real-world evaluation. While this issue is often attributed to the uncertainty term, a substantial part of the problem originates from the robustness term itself, particularly for the 0-1 loss. Existing approaches typically treat the robustness term as a global measure, ignoring its variation across different sub-regions of the input space. In this work, we propose a generalization bound that addresses this limitation by scaling the robustness term according to the number of stable and unstable samples within each sub-region. Our bounds incorporate both data- and model-dependent factors while maintaining practical relevance (yielding tighter upper bounds on true error). Experiments on models trained on the ImageNet dataset show that our bounds remain consistently non-vacuous and achieve the tightest estimates among existing methods, closely aligning with empirical performance across a range of robust deep neural networks.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Weights and Gradients: A Taxonomy of Federated Learning Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16891)
*Alvaro Javier Vargas Guerrero,Xinguang Wang,Quang Manh Doan,Guy Nagels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中消息交换的定义过于狭隘的问题，提出了一种涵盖效用和隐私的正式数学定义，将联邦消息分为三类（模型结构、统计摘要、数据条件表示），并通过计算需求、通信成本和隐私风险进行评估。对202篇论文的综述显示，自2021年以来联邦消息范式已发生显著转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习定义仅局限于传统模型权重和梯度的交换，无法涵盖合成数据、联邦分析等现代负载，存在定义缺口。

Method: 提出联邦消息的形式化数学定义，建立三分法分类体系（模型结构、统计摘要、数据条件表示），从计算需求、通信成本和隐私风险三个维度评估，并对202篇近期出版物进行系统综述。

Result: 自2021年以来，联邦学习研究出现了向多样化消息范式的显著转变，从标准深度学习更新转向更专业化的信息共享方式。

Conclusion: 该分类框架为未来研究提供了结构化路径，有助于针对不同硬件和安全需求优化联邦系统。

Abstract: Federated Learning is rapidly evolving beyond the exchange of traditional model weights and gradients, yet existing definitions fail to capture the full scope of modern payloads like synthetic data and federated analytics. This paper addresses the gap by proposing a formal mathematical definition of a federated message that accounts for both utility and privacy. We introduce a taxonomy that organizes these exchanges into three categories: model structures, statistical summaries, and data-conditioned representations. By evaluating these groups based on computational demands, communication costs, and privacy risks, we provide a clearer understanding of the trade-offs involved in decentralized training. Our review of 202 recent publications highlights a significant shift since 2021 toward diverse messaging paradigms, signaling a move away from standard deep learning updates toward more specialized information sharing. This framework provides a structured path for future research to optimize federated systems for varying hardware and security requirements.

</details>


### [190] [Fantastic Pretraining Optimizers and Where to Find Them II: Hyperball Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16899)
*Kaiyue Wen,Xingyu Dang,Kaifeng Lyu,Tengyu Ma,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hyperball是一个简单的优化器包装器，通过将权重矩阵及其对应优化器更新的Frobenius范数固定为常数，解决了现有矩阵优化器（如Muon）在大模型和数据规模下相比AdamW优势减弱的问题。在Qwen3风格模型上（最高1.2B参数），Muon Hyperball相比权重衰减基线实现了20-30%的token等效加速，并改进了学习率在不同宽度和深度间的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于矩阵的优化器（如Muon）在语言模型预训练中能显著加速，但实验观察到当模型规模和数据规模增大时，使用标准常数解耦权重衰减的Muon相比AdamW的优势会缩小。作者希望解决这一可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出Hyperball优化器包装器：对于给定的基础优化器（如Adam或Muon），将权重矩阵的Frobenius范数以及对应的优化器更新量均固定为常数。该方法受先前理论启发——权重衰减会导致权重范数达到仅依赖于训练超参数的平衡态，进而权重衰减决定了角度学习率（权重矩阵方向变化的速度）。

Result: 在Qwen3风格模型上（最高1.2B参数），Muon Hyperball相比权重衰减基线实现了20-30%的token等效加速。Hyperball还改进了学习率在不同宽度和深度间的迁移性。

Conclusion: Hyperball通过固定权重矩阵及其更新的Frobenius范数，有效提升了Muon等矩阵优化器在大规模场景下的性能，并通过理论联系权重衰减与角度学习率的关系解释了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Matrix based optimizers such as Muon can substantially speed up language model pretraining, but their gains over AdamW are observed to shrink as model size and data scale grow when using standard constant decoupled weight decay. We propose Hyperball, a simple optimizer wrapper that addresses this issue. Given a base optimizer such as Adam or Muon, Hyperball sets the Frobenius norms of weight matrices and their corresponding optimizer updates to fixed constants. On Qwen3 style models up to 1.2B parameters, Muon Hyperball achieves 20--30% token equivalent speedup over weight decay baselines. Hyperball also improves learning rate transfer across widths and depths compared to decoupled weight decay. This method is motivated by prior theory showing that training with weight decay leads to an equilibrium weight norm that only depends on the training hyperparameters. Through this mechanism, the weight decay then decides the angular learning rate, i.e. how fast the direction of the weight matrix changes.

</details>


### [191] [Factorized Neural Operators Decompose Dynamic and Persistent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16900)
*Hao Tang,Yuechen Duan,Jiongyu Zhu,Zimeng Feng,Hao Li,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化神经算子（FaNO），通过将谱分解为等变动态响应和不变持久响应，实现对多尺度物理系统的解耦建模，在预测精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化上超越现有神经算子方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子依赖单一归纳偏置，难以刻画物理系统中快速演化动力学与持久结构共存的多尺度异质行为，导致不同物理响应被耦合到共享表示中。

Method: 提出统一格林函数框架，并设计FaNO将谱表示分解为等变分支（捕获快速瞬态动力学）和不变分支（提取相干持久结构），两个算子分支自发特化为不同的物理角色。

Result: FaNO在长程自回归滚动预测、跨分辨率外推和物理机制迁移场景下均保持一致的预测性能，在预测精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化方面显著提升。

Conclusion: 可扩展物理建模应从单一归纳偏置转向因子化算子表示，以更好反映物理系统的异质组织特性，加速机器学习在科学计算与发现中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Physical systems often exhibit heterogeneous mechanisms, where rapidly evolving dynamics coexist with persistent structures. Capturing such multiscale physical behavior remains challenging for existing neural operators, which typically rely on single dominant inductive bias and therefore couple distinct physical responses into a shared representation. We introduce the Unified Green's Function Framework across domains and propose the Factorized Neural Operators (FaNO), which decompose spectral representations into equivariant dynamic responses and invariant persistent responses, leading to better interpretability and generalization. Mechanistically, we show that the two operator branches spontaneously specialize into distinct physical roles that remain consistent across scales and domains: the equivariant branch captures rapidly varying transient dynamics, whereas the invariant branch extracts coherent persistent structures. This factorized mechanism of FaNO improves prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency and cross-scale generalization across physical systems and domains. In particular, it maintains consistent predictions under long-horizon autoregressive rollout, cross-resolution extrapolation and physical-regime shifts. These findings suggest that scalable physical modeling may benefit from moving beyond single-inductive-bias formulations toward factorized operator representations that better reflect the heterogeneous organization of physical systems, accelerating the reliable deployment of machine learning for scientific computing and discovery.

</details>


### [192] [Demystifying Variance in Circuit Discovery of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16920)
*Frank Zhengqing Wu,Francesco Tonin,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了电路发现中存在的三类方差问题（重采样方差、重述方差、样本方差），提出改进方法CEAP以降低重采样方差，并揭示了重述方差源于不同模板激活不同电路，而样本方差主要由不忠实度定义本身导致而非电路缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最佳电路发现方法EAP-IG在忠实度指标上表现良好，但存在显著的三类方差问题，影响电路发现的稳定性和可靠性，本文旨在探究这些方差的根源并提出改进方案。

Method: 提出CEAP方法，在EAP-IG基础上进行改进并具有理论保证；通过实验分析重采样方差、重述方差和样本方差的成因；引入选择性贡献缩放机制解释样本方差的异常值。

Result: CEAP显著降低了重采样方差；重述方差源于不同提示模板激活不同电路，且稀疏化无法解决该问题；样本方差中极端不忠实分数主要由不忠实度定义和选择性贡献缩放机制导致。

Conclusion: 揭示了电路发现中方差问题的根源，指出由于不同模板激活不同电路，寻找能够解释和控制模型行为的综合电路具有挑战性，LLMs可能本质上难以被操控。

Abstract: Circuit discovery is a key technique in mechanistic interpretability to pinpoint the model components that are crucial for performing a given task. Although the current state-of-the-art method (EAP-IG) performs well on the metric of (un)faithfulness, it suffers from substantial variability. This includes resampling variance, where the circuit changes when we probe with a new batch of data from the same distribution; rephrasing variance, where the discovered circuit shifts when the prompts are rephrased; and sample-wise variance, where a circuit with low population unfaithfulness exhibits large fluctuations in unfaithfulness across individual samples.
  This paper studies the roots of these variances. We demonstrate that CEAP, our new circuit discovery method that improves upon EAP-IG with a theoretical guarantee, can substantially lessen resampling variance. We further show that rephrasing variance arises because prompts with different templates tend to activate different circuits in the model. This leads us to argue that it may be challenging to find a comprehensive circuit that explains and controls the model's behavior on a task, which can be expressed in countless templates, suggesting that LLMs may be inherently hard to steer. We show that sparsity, which has been claimed to form more compact and interpretable task circuits, fails to solve this problem. Regarding sample-wise variance, we argue that it is largely benign: extremely poor unfaithfulness scores often stem from how unfaithfulness is defined, rather than from defects in the measured circuits. We show that the magnitude of unfaithfulness is affected by selective contribution scaling, a neural mechanism that accounts for the extremely poor scores sometimes observed.

</details>


### [193] [A Unified Causal-Origin Taxonomy of Distributional Shifts in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16933)
*Ardianto Wibowo,Paulo E Santos,Amer Baghdadi,Matthew Stephenson,Karl Sammut,Jean-Philippe Diguet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果起源的统一分类法，将强化学习中的分布偏移（包括ID/OOD泛化与非平稳设置）统一纳入POMDP框架下的结构性组件变化中，并引入评估指标以衡量偏移影响与适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注缓解分布偏移而非其因果起源，且ID/OOD泛化与非平稳设置在形式上缺乏统一关系。

Method: 基于POMDP将交互过程分解为状态分布、观测过程、策略、奖励和转移动力学等结构性组件，结合偏移时间边界，将监督学习中的数据集偏移原理迁移至RL。

Result: 建立了内部（智能体驱动）与外部（环境驱动）的分布偏移分类，以及显式、隐式和混合偏移的区分；统一了ID/OOD泛化与非平稳性；提出了性能退化与恢复的评估指标。

Conclusion: 该分类法为系统性分析RL在分布偏移下的鲁棒性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) systems often degrade when operating conditions differ from those previously encountered, reflecting distributional shifts in the underlying data-generating process. Such shifts may occur between training and evaluation, as in In-Distribution (ID) and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization, or within non-stationary settings where environment dynamics evolve over time. However, the formal relationship between these views remains unclear, and existing work mainly focuses on mitigation rather than the causal origin of shift within the agent-environment interaction. This work develops a unified causal-origin taxonomy that characterizes sources of distributional shift in RL and relates ID/OOD generalization to non-stationary settings. We transfer the classical dataset-shift principle from supervised learning to RL by reformulating distributional shift in terms of the generative interaction process. Using a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP), we decompose the interaction into structural components, including the state distribution, observation process, policy, reward, and transition dynamics, together with the shifted-time boundary. The proposed taxonomy distinguishes internal, agent-driven, and external, environment-driven, distributional shifts. The shifted-time boundary perspective further characterizes explicit, implicit, and hybrid shifts. This formulation unifies ID/OOD generalization and non-stationarity as structured changes in the underlying process. We also introduce an evaluation framework for measuring shift impact and adaptation through performance degradation and recovery metrics. By grounding distributional shift in the causal-origin structure of RL, this work supports systematic analysis of robustness under distributional shift.

</details>


### [194] [Scalable Circuit Learning for Interpreting Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16939)
*Naiyu Yin,Dennis Wei,Tian Gao,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CircuitLasso，一种基于稀疏线性回归的可扩展电路学习方法，能够以极低的计算成本从稀疏自编码器特征中恢复与最先进干预方法结构精度相当的网络电路，并揭示可解释语义特征在模型中的传播过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于干预的电路学习方法在处理高维稀疏自编码器特征时计算成本过高，而原始神经元又存在多义性问题导致学习到的电路难以解释，因此需要一种可扩展且可解释的电路学习方法。

Method: 基于稀疏线性回归（Lasso）的电路学习方法CircuitLasso，将电路发现问题转化为稀疏回归任务，从SAE特征空间中高效学习组件间的连接关系。

Result: 在基准数据上，CircuitLasso恢复电路的结构精度与最先进的基于干预的方法相当，但计算成本显著降低；能够高效揭示SAE特征间的关系，展示可解释语义特征在模型中的传播；基于电路洞察的领域泛化任务在更低成本下达到可比性能。

Conclusion: CircuitLasso提供了一种高效、可解释的电路学习方法，克服了现有方法在处理高维SAE特征时的计算瓶颈，且学到的电路具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: A prominent research direction in mechanistic interpretability is learning sparse circuits over LLM components to reveal how they jointly produce model behavior. However, raw neurons are polysemantic, making learned circuits hard to interpret. Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features alleviate this, but their high dimensionality makes existing intervention-based circuit learning methods computationally prohibitive. We propose CircuitLasso, a scalable circuit-learning approach based on sparse linear regression. CircuitLasso recovers circuits whose structural accuracy matches that of state-of-the-art intervention-based methods on the benchmark data, at a fraction of the computational cost. For interpretability, CircuitLasso efficiently uncovers relationships among SAE features, showing how human-interpretable semantic features propagate through the model and influence its predictions. Finally, we validate the utility of our learned circuits by leveraging their insights to achieve comparable performance at substantially lower cost on a domain-generalization task.

</details>


### [195] [Beyond the Smile: A Hybrid Convolutional VAE for Crypto Volatility Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16961)
*Sadanand Singh,Allam Reddy,Manan Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种卷积变分自编码器结合二次微笑曲线重拟合的混合预测器，用于加密货币隐含波动率曲面的缺失值填充。在BTC/ETH期权数据上，混合预测器在50%缺失率下将误差从单独微笑重拟合的7.00 vol点降至0.83 vol点；在整期限结构缺失的极端场景下，生成模型是唯一可行的预测器。联合训练提升9-27%性能，且预测结果无套利。训练和评估基础设施已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币期权隐含波动率曲面存在大量缺失值（如流动性不足导致的数据稀疏问题），传统参数化微笑曲线重拟合方法在高缺失率下误差极大，需要更鲁棒的曲面补全方法以支持风险管理、定价等下游应用。

Method: 提出卷积变分自编码器（CVAE）对6×7张量-期限网格的波动率曲面进行建模，并设计确定性按期限路由规则，将CVAE输出与二次微笑曲线重拟合混合。训练数据为2023年5-10月BTC和ETH的6,034张小时频率Binance期权波动率曲面。

Result: 混合预测器在50%缺失率下达到0.83 vol点误差（单独微笑重拟合为7.00）；整期限缺失时生成模型误差1.5-1.9 vol点（微笑重拟合9.6-13.1）；联合训练提升9-27%；有效检测异常市场事件（8月17日闪崩、10月底ETF预期反弹）。

Conclusion: CVAE混合预测器在加密货币波动率曲面补全任务上显著优于纯参数化方法，具有无套利特性和异常检测能力，联合训练揭示了BTC/ETH共享的高度相似的波动率流形结构。

Abstract: We present a convolutional variational autoencoder for cryptocurrency implied-volatility surfaces, together with a deployable predictor that combines it with a quadratic smile re-fit through a deterministic per-tenor routing rule. Trained on 6,034 fully-filled hourly Binance Options surfaces of BTC and ETH spanning May-October 2023 and parameterised on a common $6 \times 7$ tenor-delta grid, the model attains a hidden-cell surface-completion RMSE in the 0.94-1.56 vol-point range across both markets and mask rates 10-50%. The hybrid predictor attains 0.83 vol points at 50% masking against 7.00 for the smile re-fit alone, an eightfold reduction obtained at no additional inference cost. Under structurally-correlated hole patterns that emulate the withdrawal of an entire tenor of strikes, the smile re-fit incurs 9.6-13.1 vol points of error while the learned model remains at 1.5-1.9, isolating a regime in which the generative model is the only viable predictor. Joint training on BTC and ETH improves the in-distribution model on both markets by 9-27% relative to the better-performing single-symbol counterpart, indicating a substantially shared vol-surface manifold across the two largest cryptocurrencies over the observation window. The hybrid is calendar- and butterfly-arbitrage-free at the listed strikes, a property that the parametric smile re-fit alone fails at high mask rates. The per-snapshot reconstruction error of the trained model flags the late-October ETF-anticipation rally and the August $17$, $2023$ flash crash as elevated-error periods without supervision. All training and evaluation infrastructure is released to support reproducible follow-on work.

</details>


### [196] [Scalable Pairwise Kernel Learning with Stochastic Vec Trick](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16979)
*Napsu Karmitsa,Tapio Pahikkala,Antti Airola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPaiK，一种可扩展的核学习方法，通过随机广义vec技巧（sGVT）大幅降低成对核学习的计算和内存开销，使核方法能够应用于大规模成对学习数据集，在七个药物-靶标亲和力数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对学习（如药物-靶标亲和力预测）中，核方法虽然表达能力强，但计算和内存需求过高，难以扩展到大规模数据集。现有方法在可扩展性与表达力之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出SPaiK方法，核心创新是**随机广义vec技巧（sGVT）**——稀疏Kronecker积乘法的随机化扩展，用于高效计算成对核矩阵，大幅降低计算和内存开销。

Result: 在七个真实药物-靶标亲和力数据集上进行评估，与当前最先进的成对学习方法比较，SPaiK展现出竞争性甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: SPaiK通过sGVT使得基于核的成对学习能够应用于此前无法企及的大规模数据集，在保持核方法表达能力的同时实现了可扩展性。

Abstract: Pairwise learning is a specialized form of supervised learning that focuses on predicting outcomes for pairs of objects. In this work, we introduce SPaiK, a new scalable kernel learning method tailored for pairwise settings. Our approach preserves the expressive power of kernel methods while substantially reducing computational and memory requirements. The key innovation is the stochastic generalized vec trick (sGVT), a stochastic extension of the sparse Kronecker product multiplication algorithm, which enables efficient large-scale training with pairwise kernels. By incorporating sGVT, SPaiK makes it possible to apply kernel-based pairwise learning to datasets of a size previously out of reach. We evaluate the performance of SPaiK on seven real-world drug-target affinity datasets and compare the results with state-of-the-art methods in pairwise learning.

</details>


### [197] [Analytic Torsion and Spectral Gap Capture Persistent-Laplacian Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16990)
*Jernej Grlj,Aaron D. Lauda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持久拉普拉斯算子（PL）的高维度和不同过滤尺度下的“变长”问题，本文提出一种紧凑谱表示方法，将PL蒸馏为三个数学不变性：贝蒂数、谱间隙和解析挠率。实验表明，该降维特征空间能捕捉全谱的核心预测信号，有时甚至优于全谱，同时显著降低计算开销并抑制高频噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久拉普拉斯算子的全特征谱虽比持久同调提供更丰富的几何表示，但存在高维度和跨过滤尺度的“变长”问题，阻碍其直接用于学习任务。

Method: 提出紧凑谱表示方法，将持久拉普拉斯算子蒸馏为三个数学上具有根基的不变性：贝蒂数、谱间隙和解析挠率。

Result: 在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上，该降维特征空间能捕捉全谱的核心预测信号，部分情况下性能甚至超越全谱，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频特征值引入的噪声。

Conclusion: 这三个不变性为谱几何与拓扑学习之间提供了一种有原则的、固定长度的接口。

Abstract: While persistent Laplacians (PL) offer a richer geometric representation of data than persistent homology, utilizing their full eigenspectrum for learning tasks is often hampered by high dimensionality and the ``varying length'' problem across different filtration scales. We propose a compact spectral representation that distills the persistent Laplacian into three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, the spectral gap, and analytic torsion. Across benchmark datasets including MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT, we demonstrate that this reduced feature space captures the essential predictive signal of the full spectrum, and in some cases outperforms it, while significantly reducing computational overhead and preventing the noise introduced by higher-frequency eigenvalues. Our results suggest that these invariants provide a principled, fixed-length interface between spectral geometry and topological learning.

</details>


### [198] [From Tokens to Policy: Causal and Interpretable Heterogeneous Treatment Effects Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17010)
*Riccardo Cadei,Frank Otchere,Nyasha Tirivayi,Gustavo Angeles Tagliaferro,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEXIS方法，通过多模态预治疗表征和马尔可夫毯发现，在受控实验中实现可解释且无混杂的异质性治疗效果因果识别，并在非洲反贫困项目中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTE识别方法在表达性与可解释性之间权衡，且当存在未测量的异质性驱动因素时，无论哪种方法都会产生无因果解释的虚假HTE表征。因此需要一种既能利用多模态预治疗数据又能保证因果可解释性的方法。

Method: 将HTE识别重新表述为在充分且对齐的预治疗表征上的马尔可夫毯发现问题，提出NEXIS（Neural Exposure Interaction Search）迭代程序，具有可证明且经验验证的一致性选择。

Result: 在两个非洲反贫困项目中部署NEXIS，通过添加卫星影像捕获先前未测量的环境效应修饰因子，产生新颖、可解释且具有指导意义的优化建议。

Conclusion: 借助更广泛的预治疗测量（多模态、多视角）和可扩展的少监督表征，通过马尔可夫毯发现实现HTE的因果识别是可行的，NEXIS能提供可解释且可操作的政策指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Treatment Effect (HTE) identification is crucial to explain the impact of an intervention and optimize our policies accordingly. Existing approaches trade expressivity for interpretability, but, if some active heterogeneity drivers are unmeasured, methods at both ends of this spectrum allow for spurious HTE characterization with no causal reading. In this work, we focus on controlled experiments and argue that an oracle HTE causal characterization via the latent interactors is now within reach, thanks to (i) more extensive pre-treatment measurements, i.e., multi-modal and multi-view, and (ii) scalable representations with minimal human supervision. We then re-frame HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem on a sufficient and aligned pre-treatment representation, and introduce Neural EXposure Interaction Search (NEXIS), an iterative procedure with provable and empirically validated consistent selection. We deploy NEXIS on two anti-poverty programs in Africa, augmenting each with satellite imagery capturing previously unmeasured environmental effect modifiers, leading to novel, interpretable and prescriptive guidelines to optimize the programs' next iterations.

</details>


### [199] [HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17028)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAMON是一种被动衍射光学预测核心，通过将历史值编码到光学孔径上，利用级联可训练相位掩模和自由空间衍射直接输出预测结果，在多个标准基准上优于或接近最强数字基线，且推理时仅需一次被动光学传播，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准时序预测基准中核心算子往往低复杂度且近似线性，可能无需Transformer那样的密集叠加表示。这引发了基础层问题：如果预测算子本质简单，是否必须用学习的数字时序混合来实现？

Method: 将历史值编码到光学孔径上，未来位置保持暗区，通过级联可训练相位掩模与自由空间衍射，直接在输出场中形成预测结果。推理时仅需单次被动光学传播，无需可训练数字序列混合层。

Result: 在ETTm2所有预测长度和ETTh2除最长外所有长度上超越最强数字基线，MSE最高改善14%；在Weather上具有竞争力；在其余ETT设置及高通道数Traffic和Electricity数据集上略逊于最强基线。消融实验（相位编码、强度兼容读出、相位扰乱）及交叉模拟验证表明预测来自承载数据的光学场而非数字预测头。

Conclusion: HAMON定义了一个具体的光学硬件目标和被动物理序列混合方案，证明低复杂度的预测算子可以通过被动光学方式高效实现。

Abstract: Simple linear and frequency-domain models remain surprisingly competitive in long-horizon time-series forecasting, and recent mechanistic evidence suggests that standard forecasting benchmarks may not require the dense superposed representations that make transformers powerful in other domains. This raises a substrate-level question: if the core forecasting operator is often low-complexity and approximately linear, does it need to be implemented as learned digital temporal mixing? We introduce HAMON, a passive diffractive optical forecasting core in which historical values are encoded onto an optical aperture, future positions are left dark, and cascaded trainable phase masks with free-space diffraction shape the forecast directly in the output field. At inference, prediction is performed by a single passive optical propagation pass with no trainable digital sequence-mixing layer.
  Across standard benchmarks, HAMON outperforms the strongest digital baselines considered on ETTm2 at all horizons and on ETTh2 at all but the longest horizon, improving MSE by up to 14\% and doing so consistently across horizons rather than at isolated points. It is competitive on Weather and trails the strongest baselines on the remaining ETT settings and on the high-channel-count Traffic and Electricity datasets. Phase encoding, intensity-compatible readout, and phase-scrambling ablations, together with a TorchOptics cross-simulator check, indicate that the forecasts arise from the data-bearing optical field rather than from a digital forecasting head. Because the passive core uses standard Fourier optics, HAMON defines a concrete target for optical hardware and for passive physical sequence mixing.

</details>


### [200] [ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17024)
*Violet Xiang,Amrith Setlur,Chase Blagden,Nick Haber,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpRL方法，利用人类编写的问答数据作为奖励支架（reward scaffolds），通过LLM裁判对策略采样轨迹进行过程级或结果级奖励评估，实现基于强化学习的中间训练（mid-training），有效提升大模型在复杂数学推理任务上的表现，并优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励强化学习虽能提升LLM推理能力，但其成功严重依赖基座模型的覆盖度。实践中常通过中间训练（mid-training）在人工筛选的推理轨迹上教授分解、验证、自我纠正等基础技能，但此策略需手动指定学习内容，且不清楚这些基础技能的组合是否足以解决需要更广泛解题策略的难题。因此，需要更自动化的方法来提升RL中间训练的效果。

Method: 提出ExpRL方法：使用大规模人工编写的问答数据，但不将参考解答作为模仿目标，而是将其作为奖励支架（reward scaffolds）。策略从原始问题提示中采样，参考解答对策略隐藏，仅用于构建问题特定的评分标准（rubrics），由LLM裁判对策略采样的推理轨迹进行结果级或过程级密集奖励评估，从而强化部分进展、有用的中间归约以及稀疏最终答案奖励难以捕捉的有效推理行为。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理任务上，ExpRL的RL中间训练效果优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，并为后续的稀疏奖励RL提供了更好的初始化。额外跨领域实验表明ExpRL可扩展到纯数学以外的场景。

Conclusion: 利用参考解答作为奖励支架而非模仿目标，通过LLM裁判提供密集奖励信号进行RL中间训练，是一种比传统SFT和稀疏奖励RL更有效的LLM推理能力提升策略，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.

</details>


### [201] [Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17035)
*Xiaolin Li,Ning Wang,Ninghui Li,Wenhai Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了差分隐私（DP）能天然增强联邦学习（FL）对后门攻击鲁棒性的假设。通过实验发现，DP会掩盖恶意更新的统计特征，使现有防御失效。作者提出RING攻击，利用DP的掩蔽效应协同构造对抗扰动，在六种防御和四种数据集上取得平均90.3%的攻击成功率，暴露了DP-FL中的安全性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"差分隐私天然增强联邦学习对后门攻击鲁棒性"这一普遍假设，揭示DP与后门防御之间的根本性张力。

Method: 通过实证分析两种基线攻击策略，发现DP的掩蔽效应使现有防御失效；提出RING攻击，利用DP特性协同构造对抗扰动以隐藏恶意更新，同时最大化攻击影响；RING作为与底层后门技术无关的扰动层，可组合现有攻击。

Result: 在四种图像和文本数据集、非独立同分布场景下，RING面对六种最先进防御达到平均90.3%的攻击成功率，比基线策略提升高达26.08倍。

Conclusion: DP-FL存在根本性安全漏洞，缓解该威胁需要显著的效用权衡，现有防御在DP约束下无法有效检测RING攻击。

Abstract: Prior research suggests that differential privacy (DP) inherently enhances the robustness of federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. Through an empirical analysis of two baseline attack strategies, we uncover a fundamental tension in DP-FL: while bypassing DP allows state-of-the-art defenses to detect and filter malicious updates, complying with DP inadvertently masks their distinguishing statistical characteristics. Consequently, existing defenses become ineffective as DP reduces the raw backdoor signal. Building on this masking effect, we propose RING, a novel attack that explicitly exploits DP to conceal malicious contributions while maximizing attack impact. By collaboratively crafting adversarial perturbations, compromised clients reconstruct a strong backdoor signal during aggregation without triggering anomaly detection. RING operates as a perturbation layer that is agnostic to the underlying backdoor technique, making it broadly applicable and composable with existing attacks -- a property that significantly amplifies the threat it poses to DP-FL. Extensive evaluations across four image and text datasets under non-iid distributions show that RING achieves an average attack success rate of 90.3% against six state-of-the-art defenses under a moderate privacy budget, an improvement of up to 26.08x over baseline strategies. Finally, we evaluate potential countermeasures and find that mitigating this threat incurs significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security gap in the deployment of differentially private FL.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Audited Conformal Prediction for Classification under Unknown Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14909)
*Yanfei Zhou,Rizal Fathony,Nam H. Nguyen,Matteo Sesia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出审计共形预测（ACP）方法，利用少量目标域标注数据训练审计模型识别高失败风险样本，并将其整合到共形预测框架中，在保证边际覆盖的同时提升条件覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练分类模型在未知分布偏移下部署时，现有共形预测方法虽能保证边际覆盖，但条件覆盖（尤其是对偏移子群体）表现不佳，亟需提升条件覆盖的可靠性。

Method: ACP方法：1）利用少量目标域标注数据训练审计模型预测遗留模型在各输入上的失败概率；2）提出两种集成策略——策略A针对边际覆盖优化条件性能，策略B提供显式的组条件覆盖保证；3）两种策略均有理论保证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，ACP相比现有方法能有效提升条件覆盖，并揭示了预测集大小与条件覆盖之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: ACP通过在共形预测中引入审计模型，实现了在未知分布偏移下更可靠的预测集构建，尤其提升了偏移子群体上的覆盖均匀性，具有理论与实用价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of uncertainty quantification for a pretrained classification model deployed under unknown distribution shift. We propose Audited Conformal Prediction (ACP), a method that leverages a small labeled dataset from the target population to train an auxiliary audit model identifying inputs where the legacy model is likely to fail. By integrating the audit model's outputs into the conformal prediction framework, ACP produces prediction sets that guarantee marginal coverage while achieving substantially higher conditional coverage in practice than existing approaches. We develop and analyze two complementary integration strategies -- one targeting marginal coverage with improved conditional performance, the other providing explicit group-conditional coverage guarantees -- and establish theoretical guarantees for both. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the method and illustrate trade-offs between prediction set size and conditional coverage.

</details>


### [203] [Conformal Candidate Certification for Offline Model-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15217)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Conformal Candidate Certification (CCC)的后处理包装器，为每个候选设计计算校准的单侧置信下界，仅通过那些下界超过目标阈值的候选。通过加权共形预测利用同一个代理模型提供重要性权重，无需额外的密度比估计步骤，解决了离线模型优化中候选分布偏移导致代理模型排名不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线模型优化方法无法为每个候选设计提供满足目标阈值的统计保证，导致在分布外区域优化器越激进时代理模型的排名越不可靠。

Method: 提出CCC方法：1) 对代理模型进行熵正则化的最大化，诱导出Gibbs倾斜提案分布；2) 利用同一代理模型计算重要性权重，用于加权共形预测；3) 为每个候选设计计算校准的单侧置信下界，仅保留下界超过目标阈值的候选。

Result: 在合成实验中，CCC在名义覆盖率为0.90的条件下，对一个激进的候选池中16.7%的候选进行了认证，经验覆盖率达到0.990；而忽略协变量偏移的标准共形预测覆盖率降至0.416。

Conclusion: CCC能够有效为离线模型优化中的候选设计提供统计保证，显著优于忽略分布偏移的简单共形预测方法。

Abstract: Offline model-based optimization (MBO) proposes candidates by optimizing a surrogate trained on a fixed historical dataset. Because candidates are deliberately out-of-distribution, surrogate rankings are least reliable exactly where the optimizer is most aggressive, yet existing methods provide no per-candidate statistical certificate that a design meets a target threshold. We propose \emph{Conformal Candidate Certification} (CCC), a post-hoc wrapper that attaches a calibrated one-sided lower bound to each candidate and advances only those whose bound exceeds the target. We show that entropy-regularized surrogate maximization induces a Gibbs-tilted proposal, so the same surrogate supplies importance weights for weighted conformal prediction without a separate density-ratio estimation step. In a controlled synthetic study, CCC certifies $16.7\%$ of an aggressive proposal pool with empirical coverage 0.990 at nominal 0.90, while standard conformal prediction ignoring the covariate shift collapses to 0.416 coverage.

</details>


### [204] [Finite Resources False Discovery Rate Control in Structured Hypothesis Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15393)
*Binyamin Perets,Shie Mannor*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个能够在有限空分布样本和结构化假设空间下控制错误发现率（FDR）的框架，利用再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）表示结构信息，并给出了两种决策规则：第一种保证精确FDR控制，第二种通过镜像统计量适应计数空间以最大化统计效能，同时利用RKHS的可处理性研究有限数据不确定性，进而提出空分布样本的高效分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖大规模假设检验，但面临三大挑战：获取空分布参考数据资源密集、有限样本导致p值不确定性、假设空间存在需考虑的结构信息。现有方法难以同时应对这些问题。

Method: 提出基于RKHS的框架，将假设空间的结构信息通过再生核表示；设计两种决策规则：规则一保证精确FDR控制，规则二将镜像统计量控制扩展到计数空间（通过分析框架评估镜像对称性放宽时的FDR控制）；利用RKHS的可计算性直接研究有限数据不确定性。

Result: 两种决策规则对结构误设定均具有稳健性，但在精确FDR控制与统计效能之间存在不同权衡；基于有限数据不确定性分析提出了空分布样本的高效分配策略。

Conclusion: 该框架为结构化的假设空间和有限空分布样本场景提供了FDR控制的可行方案，兼顾理论保证与实际效能，并指导实验资源分配。

Abstract: Scientific discovery relies on large-scale hypothesis testing. However, the capacity to identify true discoveries while controlling false discovery faces major challenges: obtaining relevant reference data (the null distribution) is resource-intensive, leaving finite-data uncertainty, and the procedure should account for the inherent structure in the hypothesis space, when such structure exists. Here, we present a framework for controlling the false discovery rate both when each hypothesis is evidenced only by a finite count of null draws, leaving its p-value uncertain, and when the hypothesis space carries arbitrary structure, requiring only that the structure be represented through a suitable reproducing kernel. We present two decision rules that are both robust to structural mis-specification, yet offer a distinct trade-off between exact FDR control and statistical power. The first rule guarantees exact FDR control; the second maximizes power by adapting mirror-statistic control into count space, utilizing an analytical framework to assess FDR control when exact mirror symmetry is relaxed. Furthermore, the tractability gained by the RKHS framework allows us to directly investigate finite-data uncertainties, which we leverage to suggest a policy for the efficient allocation of null distribution samples.

</details>


### [205] [The Reverse Telescoping Coordinate System for Positive Definite Matrices: Geometry, Computation, and Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15442)
*Anindya Bhadra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的无约束坐标系，通过逆望远镜映射将SPD矩阵参数化为体积（对数行列式）和形状（对数相对对角尺度与偏协方差）的分离表示，实现了雅可比仅依赖于对数行列式、O(p²)高效计算等关键性质，并基于此设计了体积-形状分离的生成流模型，在高达p=200的SPD矩阵生成任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SPD矩阵参数化方法（如矩阵对数）存在雅可比依赖复杂、无法分离体积与形状等局限性，限制了生成建模的效率与灵活性。本文旨在设计一种新的坐标系，使得SPD矩阵的约束条件转化为可分离的、易于建模的形式，从而简化生成模型的设计。

Method: 通过逆望远镜映射RT(x)将SPD矩阵Θ表示为三个独立分量：对数体积v（标量）、形状参数d（p-1维对数相对对角尺度）和r（p(p-1)/2维偏协方差）。该构造使得矩阵与其逆的符号表示无损且可高效计算（O(p²)），雅可比行列式仅依赖于v。基于此，提出分裂体积-形状流模型：形状部分沿单位行列式路径由条件流匹配训练，体积部分由独立的一维流建模。

Result: 在p=200的高维合成双峰目标上验证了生成建模效果；在fMRI数据训练的脑连接网络生成任务中展示了实用性；同时在SPD流形上的本征扩散任务中也取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的坐标系将SPD约束转化为分离的体积与形状表示，反而比无约束矩阵的生成建模更容易（因有行列式作为天然的体积归一化信号）。该工作揭示了SPD约束在生成建模中可作为一种引导力量而非障碍。

Abstract: We design a new unconstrained coordinate system where a $p\times p$ symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrix $Θ$ is represented by a reverse telescoping map $Θ(x)=\rm{RT}(x)$, with $x=(v,d,r)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{(p-1)}\times\mathbb{R}^{p(p-1)/2}$, representing respectively the log volume or log determinant; and the shape, as encoded by log relative diagonal scales and partial covariances among the nodes. This construction results in important properties not available in other charts, e.g., matrix logarithm, such as Jacobian depending on only the log-determinant. A useful feature of our construction is $x$ contains a lossless symbolic representation of both the matrix and its inverse. Many important computations involving a matrix and its inverse can be performed in $O(p^2)$ in the transformed domain, while it is the rendering of results in matrix forms (on demand) that must incur an $O(p^3)$ cost. Moreover, two unit-determinant matrices in the transformed domain can be joined by a straight line with pathwise unit determinant. For generative modeling, this allows designing a split volume-shape flow model trained by conditional flow matching for transporting the shape over the unit-determinant path, with a separate one-dimensional flow for transporting the volume or the determinant. The forbidding SPD constraint, tamed thus into a powerful guiding force, leads to the surprising insight that it is in some sense easier to design a volume-normalized shape flow for SPD compared to the unconstrained $\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}$, with no intrinsic notion of volume to aid normalization, unlike the determinant of SPD matrices. We apply our construction for up to $p=200$ in generative modeling of SPD matrices on a difficult synthetic bimodal target, and in generating brain connectivity networks by models trained on fMRI data; as well as in intrinsic diffusion on the SPD manifold.

</details>


### [206] [Structured Nonparametric Variational Inference for Dependent Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15458)
*Yuda Shao,Zhiling Gu,Shan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化非参数变分推断（SN-VI），利用多元样条技术对后验分布中的复杂隐变量依赖关系进行建模，突破了传统平均场假设的限制，具备理论保证和自动依赖发现能力，在计算机视觉和空间转录组学等应用中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分推断方法依赖平均场假设，无法捕捉隐变量间的复杂依赖关系，导致后验近似精度受限。本文旨在提出一种能够灵活建模任意形状后验分布并自动发现隐变量依赖结构的非参数变分推断框架。

Method: 提出SN-VI框架，采用多元样条技术对后验近似中的隐变量依赖关系进行非参数建模；推导了变分目标的下界并证明了后验估计的渐近一致性；设计了自动识别依赖隐变量及其依赖结构的算法，无需人工指定。

Result: 仿真实验验证了SN-VI在逼近具有有界支撑和复杂依赖关系的后验分布方面的有效性；在高维结构化数据（计算机视觉数据集和空间转录组学）中，SN-VI提升了生成模型性能，并通过学习到的依赖结构有效揭示耦合的生物信号。

Conclusion: SN-VI提供了一种灵活、准确且具有理论保障的非参数变分推断方法，能够突破平均场假设限制，自动建模隐变量依赖关系，在高维结构化数据应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Variational inference (VI) is a core engine of modern AI, enabling scalable approximate Bayesian learning and uncertainty-aware training of large probabilistic and generative models. In this paper, we propose Structured Nonparametric Variational Inference (SN-VI), a novel framework for modeling complex dependencies among latent variables in posterior approximation, leveraging multivariate spline techniques. Unlike traditional methods that rely on the mean-field assumption, SN-VI preserves intricate latent variable dependencies, providing a flexible and accurate approximation of posteriors with arbitrary shapes. We establish rigorous theoretical guarantees, including the derivation of the lower bound for the variational objective and proof of asymptotic consistency in posterior estimation. To facilitate practical implementation, we develop an algorithm that automatically identifies dependent latent variables and their underlying dependence structure, without requiring manual specification. Simulation studies validate the effectiveness of SN-VI in approximating posterior distributions with bounded support and complex dependencies. The proposed method has been successfully applied to high-dimensional structured data, including computer vision datasets and spatial transcriptomics. In these applications, SN-VI demonstrates improved generative model performance and effectively uncovers coupled biological signals through the learned dependency structure.

</details>


### [207] [Ricci-Filtration: Boosting Retrieval-Augmented Generation Reranker to Query-Answer Tasks by Discrete Ricci Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15482)
*Tian Qin,Wei-Min Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何曲率（Ricci流）的RAG检索重排序增强方法——Ricci-Filtration。该方法将查询和初始检索块构建为图网络，利用离散曲率和Ricci流评估每个文档块的结构重要性，先通过曲率过滤噪声块，再经重排序器提升生成性能。理论证明了归一化离散Ricci流可检测社区结构，实验表明该方法在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上优于多种基线重排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受连续Ricci流（曲率引导的扩散过程）和离散Ricci流（通过收缩/拉伸边权重来分离聚类）启发，旨在解决RAG系统中初始检索块中存在噪声、影响生成质量的问题，提出一种基于几何曲率的过滤-重排序增强框架。

Method: 1. 将输入查询和初始检索块建模为图网络，节点为查询和文档块，边权重由嵌入向量相似度定义；2. 利用离散Ricci曲率和Ricci流评估各文档块相对于查询节点的结构重要性；3. 先根据几何曲率过滤初始块（去除大权重且负曲率的噪声块）；4. 再通过重排序器处理剩余块以增强生成性能；5. 理论证明归一化离散Ricci流可通过边权重的渐近行为检测社区结构。

Result: 在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上，Ricci-Filtration优于多种基线重排序方法。消融实验表明，在不同架构下该框架普遍优于基线，证明了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于离散Ricci流的几何过滤机制能有效检测并去除RAG检索中的噪声文档块，为检索增强生成提供了一种新颖的几何视角的重排序增强方法，具有较好的鲁棒性和通用性。

Abstract: Ricci flow is a curvature-guided diffusion process that deforms space by shrinking regions of high positive curvature and expanding those with negative curvature. Similarly, discrete Ricci flow on weighted graphs modifies edge weights by shrinking edges with positive Ricci curvature and stretching those with negative Ricci curvature, effectively increasing the separation between clusters. Inspired by these two cornerstone works, we propose a geometry-based RAG reranker enhancement procedure called Ricci-Filtration. By modeling the input query and initial retrieved chunks as a network, where the input query and chunks serve as nodes and embedding-based pairwise relations define an initial graph, Ricci-Filtration leverages discrete curvature and Ricci flow to evaluate the structural importance of each chunk with respect to the user query. The system first filters the initial chunks based on their geometric curvature relative to the query; then, a reranker processes the remaining chunks to enhance generative performance. We theoretically prove that normalized discrete Ricci flow can detect community structures by identifying distinct asymptotic behaviors in edge weights. This supports the removal of ``noisy'' document chunks characterized by large weights and negative Ricci curvature relative to the query node. Extensive experiments confirm that Ricci-Filtration outperforms several baseline reranking methods in accuracy, precision, recall, and F1 scores. Furthermore, ablation studies demonstrate that the Ricci-Filtration generally outperforms the baseline under various settings, highlighting the framework's robustness across different architectures.

</details>


### [208] [Phase Transition in Convex Relaxations for Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15581)
*Laurent Massoulié,Sushil Mahavir Varma,Louis Vassaux,Irène Waldspurger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了相关高斯正交系综（GOE）矩阵的图对齐问题，证明当相关参数σ = o(n^{-1/2}/log^4 n)时，基于双随机矩阵和单位超立方体的凸松弛解X*能高精度恢复真实置换矩阵Π*（即||X*-Π*||_F^2 = o(n)），并精确刻画了该误差从o(n)到Ω(n)的相变阈值为σ = Θ̃(n^{-1/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计虽然信息论上最优，但计算上等价于二次分配问题，难以求解。因此需要寻找计算上可行且理论上可保证恢复效果的凸松弛方法。

Method: 分析基于最小化||AX - XB||_F的两个凸松弛：双随机矩阵约束和单位超立方体约束；通过概率分析证明解矩阵与真实置换矩阵的Frobenius范数误差上界。

Result: 当σ = o(n^{-1/2}/log^4 n)时，两种凸松弛的解均满足||X*-Π*||_F^2 = o(n)，经简单后处理可恢复除消失比例顶点外的所有对应关系；并精确刻画了误差在σ = Θ̃(n^{-1/2})处从o(n)到Ω(n)的相变。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了先前的结果，并将结论从双随机松弛推广到超立方体松弛，精确刻画了凸松弛恢复误差的相变阈值。

Abstract: We study the graph alignment problem for correlated Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE) matrices, where the goal is to recover a hidden vertex permutation given two correlated symmetric Gaussian matrices $(A, B)$ with correlation $1/\sqrt{1+σ^2}$. While the maximum likelihood estimator is information-theoretically optimal, its computation, which reduces to a quadratic assignment problem, is intractable. Motivated by this, we analyze convex relaxations based on minimizing $\|AX - XB\|_F$ over the set of doubly stochastic matrices and the unit hypercube. We show that when the correlation parameter satisfies $σ= o(n^{-1/2}/\log^4 n)$, the solution of either relaxation $(X^\star)$ concentrates around the ground-truth permutation matrix $(Π^\star)$, i.e., $\|X^\star-Π^\star\|_F^2 = o(n)$, implying recovery of all but a vanishing fraction of vertices after simple post-processing. Combined with existing lower bounds, our results precisely characterize that $\|X^\star-Π^\star\|_F^2$ transitions from $o(n)$ for $σ= \tilde{o}(n^{-1/2})$ to $Ω(n)$ for $σ= \tildeΩ(n^{-1/2})$. In doing so, our analysis significantly tightens prior results and extends them beyond doubly stochastic relaxations.

</details>


### [209] [Information Gap and Feasibility-Aware Inference in Binomial Logistic Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15665)
*Yuta Hayashida,Shonosuke Sugasawa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了二项逻辑斯蒂混合模型中成分检测与标签恢复之间的信息差距，发现存在一个"可检测但不可恢复"的区域，并提出了两种可行感知推断方法来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准似然准则（如BIC）能够检测到混合结构中两个成分的存在，但这并不保证对应的标签是可恢复的。作者希望揭示并解决这一内在信息差距，避免误导性的成分选择和不可靠的后验标签概率。

Method: 通过理论分析证明观测数据对混合结构的证据与每个观测用于标签恢复的信息在成分分离度上具有不同的局部阶数，且只有前者随样本量累积。提出两种可行感知推断方法：(1) 带有后验熵惩罚的可恢复性感知BIC；(2) 熵正则化估计器，缓解MLE产生过度分离成分和过度集中后验责任的问题。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论预测的"可检测但不可恢复"区域的存在，并证明所提方法能避免误导性成分选择，改善后验标签概率的校准。

Conclusion: 在固定试验次数的二项逻辑斯蒂混合中，存在检测与恢复之间的固有信息差距，本文提出的可行感知方法能有效弥补这一差距，提升模型选择的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper studies the information gap between mixture detection and label recovery in binomial logistic mixtures. Standard likelihood-based criteria such as the Bayesian information criterion (BIC) can detect the presence of two components, but this does not guarantee that the corresponding labels are recoverable. We show that this gap is intrinsic to binomial logistic mixtures with a fixed number of trials: observed-data evidence for mixture structure and per-observation information for label recovery have different local orders in the component separation, and only the former accumulates with the sample size. As a result, there exists a detectable-but-unrecoverable regime in which BIC selects two components while the posterior labels remain essentially uninformative. To address this issue, we propose two feasibility-aware inference procedures: a recoverability-aware BIC with a posterior-entropy penalty and an entropy-regularized estimator that mitigates the tendency of the maximum likelihood estimator to produce overly separated components and overly concentrated posterior responsibilities. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted gap and demonstrate that the proposed methods avoid misleading component selections and improve the calibration of posterior label probabilities.

</details>


### [210] [Stochastic trace estimation with tensor train random vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15679)
*Zvonimir Bujanović,Daniel Kressner,Hrvoje Olić*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯随机张量列向量作为随机迹估计的结构化替代方案，证明了当张量列秩 r ≥ d-1 时，基于中位数均值变体的估计器可达到与经典非结构化高斯向量相同的精度 ε 和失败概率 δ 的维度无关保证，并进一步证明了在子空间嵌入和 Nyström++ 框架中的样本复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在张量结构设定下，非结构化高斯或Rademacher测试向量的存储和计算成本过高，而低秩张量积向量虽然廉价但样本复杂度随张量阶数指数增长。因此需要一种在计算效率和样本复杂度之间取得平衡的结构化替代方案。

Method: 采用高斯随机张量列（tensor train）向量作为结构化测试向量，分析了其在Girard-Hutchinson迹估计器中的表现，提出了中位数均值变体；并进一步研究了其在 oblivious 子空间嵌入和 Nyström++ 框架中的应用。

Result: 当张量列秩 r ≥ d-1 时，随机张量列向量可实现维度无关的保证，与经典非结构化高斯向量的精度和失败概率依赖关系相同；对于 k 维目标子空间，需 O(ε^{-2}(k+log(1/δ))) 个样本；在 Nyström++ 框架中，在额外谱尾条件下可达到 O(ε^{-1}) 的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 明确了随机张量列向量在随机迹估计中的潜力和局限性，为实际应用中的向量选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Stochastic trace estimation is a standard tool for approximating the trace of a large-scale matrix available only through matrix-vector products. However, in tensor-structured settings, unstructured Gaussian or Rademacher test vectors may be prohibitively expensive to store and compute with, while cheaper rank-one tensor-product vectors can require sample complexities that grow exponentially with the tensor order. This work studies Gaussian random tensor train vectors as a structured alternative for stochastic trace estimation. We show that, with a suitable choice of the tensor train rank, random tensor train vectors recover dimension-independent guarantees for the Girard--Hutchinson estimator. In particular, a median-of-means variant with tensor train rank $r \geq d-1$ achieves the same dependence on the accuracy $\varepsilon$ and failure probability $δ$ as the classical estimator based on unstructured Gaussian vectors. We further prove an oblivious subspace injection result for sketches formed from independent Gaussian random tensor train vectors: tensor train rank $r\geq d-1$ and $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2}(k+\log(1/δ)))$ samples suffice for a $k$-dimensional target subspace. Finally, we investigate the use of such sketches within the Nyström++ framework. We show that the resulting estimator can achieve the desired $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1})$ sample complexity under an additional spectral-tail condition. These results provide clarififcation on both the potential and the limitations of random tensor train vectors in stochastic trace estimation.

</details>


### [211] [Spectral Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Structured Non-Exchangeable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15950)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出光谱自适应共形预测方法，通过谱相似性加权校准残差并自适应更新误覆盖率，为非可交换时间序列数据提供有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多时间序列数据具有季节性、周期性模式或结构化依赖，不满足可交换性假设，导致传统共形预测方法失效，需要一种能利用时间结构进行预测区间构建的方法。

Method: 该方法由两部分组成：(1) 使用局部谱相似性计算加权共形分位数，选择与当前测试点相关的校准残差；(2) 在线更新目标误覆盖率，以校正不确定性变化时的长期误检率。同时引入有效样本量诊断来监控谱加权效果。

Result: 给出了固定谱加权分位数的近似覆盖结果和自适应更新的确定性长期校准结果。通过重复性模式和缓慢变化频率的模拟实验以及三个美国真实数据示例验证了混合方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合方法（谱加权+自适应更新）能改进单独的固定谱加权方法，但谱加权需要通过有效样本量诊断进行监控，以确保其有效性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction gives prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage when the data are exchangeable. Many time-indexed datasets are not exchangeable. They have seasons, recurring regimes, changing frequencies, or other forms of structured dependence. This paper studies a simple way to use that structure. We propose spectral adaptive conformal prediction, a method that forms weighted conformal quantiles using local spectral similarity and then updates the target miscoverage level online. The spectral weights choose calibration residuals that look relevant to the current test point. The adaptive update corrects the long-run miss rate when uncertainty changes over time. We give an approximate coverage result for the fixed spectral weighted quantile and a deterministic long-run calibration result for the adaptive update. Simulations with recurring regimes and slowly changing frequencies, together with three U.S. real-data examples, show that the hybrid method can improve on fixed spectral weighting, while also showing that spectral weighting must be monitored through effective sample size diagnostics.

</details>


### [212] [PromptShift-CRC: Drift-Aware Conformal Risk Control for Foundation Models Under Prompt and Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15964)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptShift CRC，一种针对基础模型在提示漂移和领域偏移下能自适应控制风险的校准方法，通过嵌入提示和响应、加权校准示例、在线更新风险水平，在合成和真实基准测试中优于静态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署时，接收的提示会快速变化（用户、主题、策略变化），导致固定校准不可靠。现有共形预测方法在校准数据与未来数据分布一致时表现良好，但在分布漂移下失效。

Method: PromptShift CRC方法：嵌入提示和响应，测量当前提示流与校准池的距离，对相关或近期校准示例赋予更高权重，在观察违规后在线更新风险水平。提供三种诊断指标：实现风险误差、提示漂移度和有效校准规模。

Result: 在合成提示漂移基准上，静态共形风险控制在漂移后严重失效，而PromptShift-CRC在自适应基线中覆盖率最佳。在问答、毒性检测、摘要事实性和长上下文幻觉风险的公开基准流上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 给出了方法在分布不匹配和加权分位数不确定性条件下控制风险的条件。PromptShift CRC能有效应对提示漂移场景下的风险控制问题。

Abstract: Foundation models are now used in settings where the prompts they receive can change quickly. Users change, topics change, policies change, and the model may suddenly face a kind of request that was rare in the calibration data. This makes fixed calibration risky. Conformal prediction and conformal risk control give model-agnostic ways to control error, but they work best when the calibration data still look like the future data. This paper develops PromptShift CRC, a drift-aware conformal risk control method for foundation-model outputs under prompt and domain shift. The method embeds prompts and responses, measures how far the current prompt stream has moved from the calibration pool, gives more weight to relevant or recent calibration examples, and updates the risk level online after observed violations. It reports three practical diagnostics: realized risk error, prompt drift, and effective calibration size. We give conditions under which the method controls risk up to terms for distribution mismatch and weighted quantile uncertainty. In a synthetic prompt-shift benchmark, static conformal risk control fails sharply after drift, while PromptShift-CRC gives the best coverage among the adaptive baselines considered. We then evaluate the same calibration layer on public benchmark derived streams for question answering, toxicity, summarization factuality, and long-context hallucination risk

</details>


### [213] [Closing the Approximation Gap in Simulation-free Latent SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16138)
*Henry D. Smith,Brian L. Trippe,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Helmholtz-SDE，一种无仿真变分推断算法，用于从噪声观测中恢复潜在随机微分方程系统，在保持无仿真方法高效性的同时，达到与仿真方法相当的推断精度，尤其在高后验不确定性场景下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无仿真VI算法通过参数化后验的瞬时边际而非漂移来避免数值仿真，但其参数化限制了对后验的近似能力，导致后验推断和参数学习效果退化。本文旨在弥合无仿真方法与仿真方法之间的性能差距。

Method: 提出Helmholtz-SDE算法，通过优化与指定边际分布集合兼容的路径律（path laws），在保持无仿真计算效率的同时扩展了近似后验的表达能力。

Result: Helmholtz-SDE比现有无仿真方法更准确地恢复动力学系统，在高后验不确定性下增益最大；同时以极小的运行时开销达到与仿真VI方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 无仿真VI的效率不必以牺牲推断精度为代价，Helmholtz-SDE通过更灵活的路径律参数化成功兼顾了计算效率与模型性能。

Abstract: Recovering dynamical systems from noisy observations is a recurring challenge across scientific domains, including neuroscience and physics. Latent stochastic differential equations (SDEs) address this by modeling the system as an unobserved state that evolves according to a learnable SDE and generates the observations. Variational inference (VI) provides a tractable objective for fitting latent SDEs. Traditional VI algorithms evaluate this objective by numerical simulation over a time discretization, trading fidelity for computational cost. A recent class of algorithms, simulation-free VI, sidesteps this tradeoff by parameterizing the posterior through its instantaneous marginals rather than its drift.
